# Outlands forum knowledge base

Generated by `harness/forum_kb.py digest` on 2026-10-09 02:50 UTC. Do not edit by hand.

- Corpus: 2017-04-17 to 2026-10-02, sections General Discussion, Announcements, Player Guides & Macros, New Player Questions, Active Development, Corpse Creek.
- Facts by verdict: consensus 821, single_source 211, official 167, disputed 90, outdated 36, not_useful 3.
- Model: claude-sonnet-5-5; extraction prompt x1, adjudication a1.
- Confidence: everything here is community-derived. `official` = stated by staff in an official section (Announcements, Active Development); `consensus` = at least two independent players agree and outnumber the dissent; `single_source` = a cluster of two or more players' claims that only one of them supports. Claims of a single player without staff support aren't adjudicated, so they aren't listed. Facts last seen before 2023-01-01 are listed but not promoted to the knowledge store (Outlands overhauled many systems in 2022-2024). Verify in game before relying on it.

## Skills & training

- **animal form abilities**: Each Animal Form has 4 Form Abilities, which are activated by consuming Druid Symbols. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **animal form upgrades**: Each Animal Form has 6 Upgrades of which players can select 3, and Upgrades can only be changed while not currently in that Animal Form. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **druid tome menu**: Double-click the Druid's Tome to open the Druid Tome Menu, where you can view Abilities, Upgrades, Weapon Modifications, Bonuses, and Passive Abilities for each Animal Form. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **druidism requirements**: Players need 80 Druidism and 80 Animal Lore to adopt an Animal Form. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **druidism skill**: Druidism is a new skill centered on shapeshifting into Animal Forms for a limited duration, and a player's effective Druidism skill is capped by their Animal Lore skill. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **geomancy skill**: Geomancy is a new skill that allows players to construct Golems as Summoned Followers. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **golem types and skill requirements**: There are 4 Golem types (Acid, Flesh, Iron, Wood), each requiring a Secondary Skill that caps effective Geomancy for that Golem, and players need at least 80 Geomancy and 80 Secondary skill to summon one. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **snooping visual item sifting**: With at least 50 snooping and the relevant option enabled, you can visually sift through the items in another player's inventory when opening it; this only moves items visually for you, and re-opening the container resets them to their original positions. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **spellchaining skill**: Spellchaining is a new skill for spellcasters that rewards progressively increasing bonuses for casting offensive spells along a sequence called a Spell Chain. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **stealing skill target cursor**: The stealing skill target cursor can only be executed by manually clicking or by using the thief's target. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **taming system updates**: The Tidal Tomb expansion updates the Taming System with new Experience Levels, Redline upgrades, Passive Taming and Skill Gain Overhaul, Healer's Codex integration, and Command Aspect adjustments. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-01–2025-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32437/)
- **thief's target eligibility**: Only items inside another player's inventory can be assigned as the thief's target; items worn or held by a player and items on non-player NPCs cannot be assigned. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **thief's target expiration**: An item assigned as the thief's target expires after 60 seconds, tracked by a new cooldown type. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **alchemy potion bonuses**: Alchemy skill scales potion performance: it lengthens Strength and Agility potion duration, increases healing potion effectiveness, and improves cure chances, with bonuses growing as skill rises toward 100-120 (in PvE and to a lesser extent in PvP). _(consensus, conf 0.80, 7 authors, 2017-09-01–2024-03-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29111/)
- **anatomy skill effects**: Anatomy passively increases weapon damage (about 20%) and bandage healing (about 20%), and is required to cure with bandages (60) and resurrect others with bandages (80). _(consensus, conf 0.80, 8 authors, 2018-01-12–2021-05-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21822/)
- **animal taming skill gain per creature**: Animal Taming skill gain from a given creature ends once that creature is successfully tamed, so releasing and re-taming it gives no further gains. Gains also stop after 1.0 total skill has been gained from it, and gains at higher skill are rare, around 2% per attempt. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2018-10-18–2018-12-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7610/)
- **arms lore disarm effect**: Arms Lore enables frequent weapon disarm effects on mobs, which increase damage dealt to the target; reported duration is about 10 seconds with a 30-second cooldown, and it is considered essential for melee and archery damage. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2017-09-01–2026-05-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33146/)
- **arms lore effects**: Arms Lore unlocks disarm at 80 and further skill raises the chance to land weapon specials in PvM and reduces durability loss; it gives no additional PvP benefit past 80, does not affect crafting chance or quality, and can be used on an item for more information. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2018-12-22–2026-03-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32905/)
- **camping skill benefits**: The Camping skill increases PvM damage (reported as about +20-25% at 100 skill), raises backpack weight capacity by up to 200 stones and item count by up to 50 (scaling with skill/100), and enables a fast travel system. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2018-05-14–2025-03-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31506/)
- **camping skill benefits**: Camping increases carry weight and item count, adds a damage bonus, and enables free travel by hiking around the world map once locations are unlocked, plus camp recall and logging out in the wilds, without risking runes or an unblessed runebook. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2018-11-18–2025-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31071/)
- **forensic evaluation benefits**: Forensic Evaluation gives bonus damage and a stronger spell effect, and it yields extra leather (including colored leather and feathers) when skinning corpses, which is sold for gold or used for blank scrolls and books. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 8 authors, 2018-12-08–2024-10-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30454/)
- **forensic evaluation benefits**: Forensic Evaluation increases damage (about 25% at Grandmaster 100) and also improves effective barding skill (about 10%) and leather yield, and combined with Tracking it enables the hamstring ability. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2018-04-23–2020-11-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19476/)
- **inscription bonuses**: Inscription lengthens the duration of buff spells such as Protection, Reactive Armor, Bless and Magic Reflect, and adds spell damage, being the second largest spell damage bonus for Necromancy. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2017-09-01–2025-01-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31013/)
- **lumberjacking and mining damage bonus**: Lumberjacking and Mining add a weapon damage bonus when used as combat skills (Lumberjacking for axes, Mining for maces), worth about 20% in PvM and 10% in PvP at GM skill. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2017-09-03–2020-12-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19910/)
- **magic resist**: Magic Resist (Resisting Spells) reduces incoming spell damage, roughly 10-30% at 80-100 skill, and is widely used by PvM and PvP characters. It is diminished in PvP, but spell damage against GM resist is still reported as very high, and Resist potions do not work in PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 9 authors, 2017-09-01–2026-07-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33469/)
- **tamer skill build**: A tamer needs Animal Taming, Animal Lore and Veterinary (about 300 skill points), usually plus Herding and some healing. Later builds may trade Veterinary and Magery down to 80 for 120 Taming and Lore, which costs about 40 more points than 100 in each, and progress needs skill balls, scrolls and command cores. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 7 authors, 2018-11-27–2022-02-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24457/)
- **taste identification mana regeneration**: Taste Identification amplifies the mana regeneration bonus from eating delectable food, so it is a core skill for mage and summoner builds, with higher levels (up to 120) noticeably improving meditation and adding some poison damage. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 8 authors, 2018-11-19–2026-07-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33469/)
- **total skill point cap**: Outlands has a base total skill cap of 700 points, which can be raised to 720 with items, and builds are balanced around 720. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2019-02-05–2026-07-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33340/)
- **tracking skill bonuses**: Tracking gives an effective barding skill bonus of up to about 10 at 100 skill against the marked target while hunting is active, plus some extra damage, hamstring, and awareness of nearby red players. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 10 authors, 2019-01-02–2020-09-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18959/)
- **tracking skill for pk avoidance**: The Tracking skill shows nearby players, giving early warning of approaching PKs so a player can react and escape. It is commonly added to PvM templates at roughly 80-100 skill, and higher skill (100-120) detects PKs before they detect you, though you must react immediately and not farm into a dead end. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 12 authors, 2019-01-08–2026-07-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33339/)
- **tracking skill for red detection**: The Tracking skill, kept active while farming or hunting, alerts a player to nearby or approaching reds via the track window and arrows, letting them leave before being killed and check whether the red is alone or in a group. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 13 authors, 2018-12-17–2025-03-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31506/)
- **alchemy potion buff duration**: Alchemy extends buff potion durations. A modest amount (about 20 points) roughly doubles them, and around 40 to 60 Alchemy gives about 5 to 6 minutes instead of 2. Raising Alchemy to 60 is cheap, but going above 60 gets expensive. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-01-15–2021-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21271/)
- **animal taming skill gain bonuses**: Animal Taming does not benefit from the 5x skill gain bonus, active skill gain tricks, or the newbie dungeon bonus; only passive gains apply unless staff say otherwise. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2017-10-24–2018-12-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7610/)
- **animal taming skill gain rate**: Raising Animal Taming above roughly 90-95 is extremely slow, around 0.3 to 0.4 points per day of taming, and gains at lower levels come in ranges such as 70-72.5 and 75-77.5 from active taming with passive gains between them. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-12-04–2020-04-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17206/)
- **animal taming training and pet use**: Taming skill gains come from failed attempts on creatures, up to 1.0% per creature, with passive gains matching active gains per the beta-era guide; pets are usable at 75 taming, and Animal Lore must be paired with taming to control them effectively. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-06-10–2021-05-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21930/)
- **best way to train lockpicking**: Picking dungeon chests, even on the first dungeon level, gives faster Lockpicking and Remove Trap gains than the training lockbox or paragon chests, though New Player Dungeon chests give no gain at 50 or above in those skills. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-08-17–2019-01-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9114/)
- **camping skill benefit**: Camping lets a character stay in a dungeon longer and carry more loot before leaving, at the cost of a greater chance of being PKed while carrying higher-value loot. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2019-01-14–2021-05-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21755/)
- **inscription and spell scrolls**: With Inscription a mage can make spell scrolls without reagents and use them instead of carrying many reagents; casting from a scroll without Inscription only removes the chance to fizzle, and the caster still needs the minimum Magery for the spell. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-11-22–2019-11-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15745/)
- **lumberjacking damage bonus**: Lumberjacking gives a small damage bonus when fighting with axe weapons, and reaching a high skill total (720) grants a 10% PvP damage bonus; Forensic Evaluation or Tracking may provide the same PvP bonus more easily. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-07-19–2021-05-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21821/)
- **magery skill**: Magery is the skill that lets a character cast spells, including cures, greater heals and buffs, and casting spells on yourself can raise it. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-12-05–2022-07-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25561/)
- **meditation mana recovery**: Meditation increases mana recovery, with a passive regeneration component and an active regeneration when the skill is used, and skill can be gained while mana refills from below maximum. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-04-05–2022-07-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25561/)
- **passive taming gains**: Passive taming gains are points banked on your character, up to 2.5, that are used when your tamed pets kill higher tiered or harder monsters, giving a chance at a 0.1 Taming gain. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-06-10–2020-03-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16682/)
- **skill cap and skill orbs**: The total skill cap is 700, and using 20 +1 skill cap orbs raises it to 720; skill scrolls raise individual skill caps (up to 120), but the total must still fit within the total cap. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-11-14–2021-06-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22003/)
- **spirit speak uses**: Spirit Speak is used to charge spirit spell stones by drawing energy from nearby corpses (up to nearly a screen away), and also lets you see and understand player ghosts; stones and summons add damage and let you meditate. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-01-12–2019-11-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15646/)
- **spirit speaking summon duration and dispel resistance**: Spirit Speaking lengthens summoned creature durations and increases their resistance to dispel, mass dispel and creature Auto-Dispel effects, so summons last longer and can tank, saving mana. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2017-09-01–2019-10-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15174/)
- **stealth skill gain**: On Outlands, stealth skill gain chances occur only when the Stealth skill is activated, not on each step, so a training macro needs only UseSkill and a wait of about 10 seconds, with no walking. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-11-14–2018-11-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5105/)
- **taming targets at 95 skill**: At about 95 Taming, players can tame Molten Mongbats (Inferno level 2) instead of dragons, since dragons are hard to tame repeatedly and break peace quickly, though the dragons just west of Andaria are also considered a good option. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2019-04-24–2020-03-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16768/)
- **total skill cap**: A new character is capped at 700 total skill points, which the player allocates freely; the Skill Mastery system can raise the total cap to 720, and total stats are capped at 225. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-10-15–2021-12-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/23752/)
- **town stealing guard consequences**: In town, a thief noticed by a human NPC is killed by guards, and one noticed by a player is killed if that player calls guards within 15 seconds; each guard death for stealing adds a cumulative 10% notice chance in that town for 12 hours, and NPCs without line of sight can still occasionally notice. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-12-11–2019-04-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13002/)
- **tracking damage bonus**: Tracking gives a damage bonus while active (Hunting mode or tracking a target): 25% * (Tracking Skill / 100) against creatures in PvM, and 10% * (Tracking Skill / 100) against players in PvP; whether it passes to summoned creatures is unclear. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-08-25–2022-12-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26634/)
- **tracking skill for pk awareness**: Tracking trains quickly and passively, and gives PK awareness that helps avoid most solo PK situations, including for miners who pair it with an auto-recall script. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2019-01-29–2024-06-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29810/)
- **treasure chest skill requirements**: Cartography on any character, plus Lockpicking and Remove Trap on the chest opener, are mandatory for treasure hunting, while Mining is optional and only extends digging range. The required lockpicking level is the chest's base requirement minus bonuses from harvest aspect, GM tools and tool colour. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-08-11–2021-02-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20343/)
- **wrestling and resist for pk survivability**: Training Wrestling and Resist (plus Tracking) improves survivability against PKs and also helps in PvM, so a defenseless glass-cannon build is riskier. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-11-19–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31813/)
- **80 tracking pvp damage bonus**: Having 80 Tracking skill on Outlands grants a damage bonus of about 20% more bow damage in PvE and about 8% in PvP, in addition to its tracking function. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-03-19–2025-02-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31417/)
- **active skill gain bonus**: Outlands has an active skill gain bonus that rewards actively training skills: after killing a creature, a player reportedly receives 5x skill gains for 10 minutes, and players use UOSteam scripts to automate this. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-01-07–2019-02-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9242/)
- **aegis imp taming requirement**: Aegis Imps require 100 (GM) Animal Taming to tame, so they cannot be used for 95-100 taming. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-14–2019-01-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7778/)
- **anatomy damage bonus**: Anatomy is a core damage skill, not a supplemental one, and provides a 20% damage bonus (notably in PvP) that does not count against the supplemental damage cap; it also adds 20% to bandage healing. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-05-07–2020-07-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18197/)
- **animal lore requirement for control**: A creature can be tamed with its listed Taming skill, but you need matching Animal Lore to control it and have it respond to commands. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-10-02–2019-04-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12602/)
- **arms lore disarm**: Arms Lore (reportedly 80+) enables the Disarm special move with weapons or wrestling, which works in PvP and gives a significant damage boost against mobs, and it also raises special-move hit chance, including Hinder with a bow. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-09-03–2019-11-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15543/)
- **backstabber thief build**: A backstabber template is the recommended build for a thief who wants to kill monsters in one or two hits and for players who struggle against player killers; one example uses 100 Macefighting, Stealing, Hiding, Stealth, Tracking, Camping and Forensic Evaluation. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2024-05-23–2026-06-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33291/)
- **bandage healing versus greater heal**: Bandage healing speeds up with higher dexterity, down to about one heal every 10 seconds, and is reliable but delayed. It heals about as much as one Greater Heal but takes longer, and Greater Heal is instant cast. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-09-16–2024-10-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30411/)
- **camping skill benefits**: Camping lets you set camp and increases carrying weight and item capacity, with benefits at any level and notable ones at 80, including hiking to many locations without a runebook or mage gate. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-10-02–2024-08-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30291/)
- **cartography requirement for treasure hunting**: The treasure hunting character does not need Cartography skill to dig up treasure or open chests; Cartography is only needed to decode the map, which an alternate character can do. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-10-19–2019-08-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14551/)
- **casting with a shield**: A character with at least 80 Magery and 80 Parry can cast spells while keeping a shield equipped. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-01-11–2023-11-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28483/)
- **detect hidden and tracking range**: Detect Hidden increases the distance at which players using the Tracking skill can discover Hidden players; one player estimates a GM Detect Hidden, 120 Tracking character can spot a GM Hiding, 120 Stealth player from roughly 60 tiles outside and roughly 30 tiles inside dungeons. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-26–2022-02-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24471/)
- **dexxer template**: A dexxer template is built around 100 Tactics and 100 weapon skill with low Dexterity, and the wiki recommends a Survival Dexxer with 100 Str, 100 Dex, 25 Int and 100 in Tracking, Swords, Tactics, Anatomy, Healing, Resisting Spells and Forensic Evaluation. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-01-23–2023-02-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26971/)
- **discordance damage effect**: Discordance can affect multiple enemies, reducing their damage output and making them take 25% more damage, rising to 50% if the mob is also peaced or provoked. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2017-09-01–2020-01-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16201/)
- **disguise kit stealing bonus**: Wearing or using a Disguise Kit adds a flat 25% to stealing success, alongside the hidden bonus and bonus for disarmed or hamstrung targets, so a hidden thief with a kit at 100 stealing has 100% to steal items of 10 stones or less. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-11–2019-04-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13002/)
- **hamstring ability from wrestling and anatomy**: Combining Wrestling with Anatomy grants the Hamstring ability, so no third skill is needed for it. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-02-18–2019-03-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10955/)
- **healing skill**: Healing increases the chance and amount healed with bandages and, at high enough skill, allows curing poison and resurrecting others; it provides a far larger bandage-heal bonus than Anatomy (about five times), and a starting character with Healing receives extra bandages. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-07-19–2019-01-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8807/)
- **healing skill bandage abilities**: Healing lets a player bandage themselves and others, cure poison at roughly 60 skill, and resurrect other players at roughly 80 skill, with Anatomy also contributing. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-11-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3881/)
- **herding skill benefit**: Herding only increases the damage dealt by a character's tamed and summoned creatures (a minor damage bonus, with following damage and command suit benefits) and does not help the character's own damage. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-07-19–2026-07-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33469/)
- **hiding and stealth system messages**: On Outlands, Hide gives 'You have hidden yourself well' on success and 'You can't seem to hide here' in a bad spot; Stealth gives 'You begin to move quietly.' on success, 'You fail in your attempt to move unnoticed.' on failure, 'You must hide first' if not hidden, and 'You are not hidden well enough.' with insufficient Hiding. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-18–2019-04-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12347/)
- **hiding and stealth training**: To train Hiding and Stealth on Outlands, use Hiding while not hidden (and until about 80 skill), then use Stealth while hidden, re-hiding when hidden status is lost, pausing about 10.2 seconds between uses; keep a 5x skill gain buffer active, train somewhere safe like a cemetery or forest, and use Hiding only once Stealth reaches 100. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-12–2019-02-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9290/)
- **inscription benefits**: Inscription increases spell damage for spellcasters and gives a chance for spell scrolls to be returned on use; higher skill also makes scroll-cast spells more potent, and inscribed scrolls can be sold to other players. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-22–2022-07-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25561/)
- **item id repeat identification**: The same item can be identified repeatedly with the Item ID skill, so a macro that identifies one item over and over can train the skill. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-01-15–2019-01-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8363/)
- **item identification active skill gain bonus**: Item Identification no longer benefits from the active skill gain bonus, although its base skill gain was reportedly increased around the time the bonus was removed. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-01-18–2019-02-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9175/)
- **item identification bonus to vanquishing weapons**: Item Identification doubles the vanquishing weapon damage bonus from 25% to 50% at 100 skill (55% at 120), and it only benefits magic weapons. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2024-07-07–2024-10-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30457/)
- **lockpicking and remove trap for treasure chests**: Higher-level treasure maps need higher Lockpicking and Remove Trap: GM/GM with valorite tools handles nearly everything except possibly level 8, 90/90 is slow on level 5/6, and low-level maps and dungeon chests can be done with GM Lockpicking and 0 Remove Trap. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-05-26–2021-10-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/23247/)
- **mace fighting strength**: Higher Mace Fighting skill reduces misses, and players regard mace fighting as a leading melee skill, ahead of swordsmanship against low-end monsters and as the top PvP melee choice thanks to its armor piercing. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-06-09–2020-11-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19740/)
- **mage wrestling purpose**: Mages take Wrestling to avoid spell interruption, since unarmed Wrestling counts as their weapon skill and helps thwart melee attacks. It can also improve their spells. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-05-06–2022-07-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25561/)
- **magery training 60-100**: Magery can be trained by casting Invisibility on yourself from 60-80, Flame Strike on yourself from 80-90 (a healer is required), and Resurrection on a ghost alt from 90-100. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-04-15–2019-05-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13228/)
- **mining skill and treasure chest search radius**: Higher Mining skill increases the radius in which a shovel can find a treasure chest from a treasure map, while at 0 Mining the player must be directly on the chest location. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-01-05–2024-05-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29446/)
- **musicianship bard skill cap**: Musicianship is required for the other bard skills and caps their effective level, so a bard skill at 100 only counts as 80 if Musicianship is 80. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2021-03-28–2022-03-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24749/)
- **passive taming bank maximum**: A player banks 0.1 passive taming points for every 0.1 Taming gained from taming creatures, up to a maximum of 2.5 points banked at one time. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-06-10–2019-02-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10275/)
- **poisoning skill benefits**: Poisoning gives a large chance not to consume poison charges when poisoning a target, lets you poison weapons and re-apply poison mid-battle, and gives a chance to cast Greater and Deadly Poison. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2017-09-01–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **poisoning skill requirements for poison levels**: Poison level depends on Poisoning skill: Lesser 0-40, Regular 35-60, Greater 55-80, Deadly 75-100, Lethal 95-120; the wielder needs about 100 Poisoning to apply Lethal poison in combat, and 120 maximizes it. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-10-12–2018-11-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5126/)
- **pvp mage template and damage bonus cap**: The basic PvP mage template uses magery, meditation, evaluating intelligence, wrestling and magic resist, and supplemental damage skill bonuses are capped at 20% in PvP both inside and outside Arenas. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-10-11–2019-04-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12091/)
- **resisting spells**: Resisting Spells reduces spell damage taken (about 12.5% minimum to 37.5% maximum at 100 skill) and gives a chance to absorb creature spell damage, which can be returned in melee strikes, but the absorb does not apply in PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-03-04–2024-10-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30409/)
- **resisting spells usefulness**: Resisting Spells is valuable mainly against player killers in PvP, so PvM-focused builds can lower it and put points into Focus or a damage skill; at 100 skill it gives full protection from spell interruption by creatures and environmental damage, but not in PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2021-03-19–2024-10-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30409/)
- **scarab as early tank pet**: At 70 Taming, tame and bond a Scarab as your tank pet; it has 100 resist spells and is tanky, making it good for early lich gold farming, with Searing Mantis and Asp usable for DPS. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2020-02-27–2020-08-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18570/)
- **skill cap above 100**: Weapon and combat skills such as Archery cap at 100 and cannot be raised higher through normal training; going above 100 requires scrolls. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-19–2019-09-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15033/)
- **slug taming requirements**: Colossal Slugs require 75 Taming and take 2 pet slots while offering strong DPS, while Giant Slugs require 65 Taming, take 1 slot, and have a much smaller life pool. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-04-15–2020-02-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16651/)
- **spellbook potency mana refund**: Spellbook potency gives a chance to refund mana on cast: Potent 5%, Surpassingly Potent 10%, Eminently Potent 15%, Exceedingly Potent 20%, Supremely Potent 25%; with full aspect levels a total refund chance of 43% is possible, which speeds Magery training. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-10-28–2022-09-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26020/)
- **spyglass map deciphering bonus**: A spyglass adds a bonus to effective cartography skill when deciphering treasure maps, and an exceptional quality spyglass reportedly allows 100% accuracy. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-05–2022-10-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26068/)
- **standard 5x mage template**: The standard 5x mage template is Magery, Meditation, Evaluating Intelligence, Resisting Spells, and Wrestling, leaving two additional skills for synergy, such as Swords and Tactics for a tank mage. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-28–2020-09-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18959/)
- **stealth archer skill template**: The common stealth archer template is about 120 Stealth (100 early), 100 Hiding, Archery, Tactics, plus Tracking and Camping at 100 and 80-100 Arms Lore, with variants such as 50 Healing. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-02-21–2020-08-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18611/)
- **summoner mage template**: A typical Summoner template is built around 100 Magery, Meditation and Evaluating Intelligence with Spirit Speak and Taste Identification raised to 120, plus optional skills such as Tracking, Camping or Forensic Evaluation. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-07-25–2021-09-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22907/)
- **taming skill scroll cost**: Raising Animal Taming and Animal Lore via skill scrolls is expensive, at roughly 50-65k gold per scroll, and reaching 120 Taming costs at least about 1 million gold. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-04-15–2019-06-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13990/)
- **tank pet taming macro**: A taming macro uses object IDs of target mobs and bandages the tank pet via autotargetobject when its hits drop below a threshold (e.g. 82); to use it, pull the mob, run back to your tank pet, then run the macro and select the creature to tame and your tank pet. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-05–2019-01-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8320/)
- **taste identification benefits**: Taste Identification, used on the ground, gives resistance to bleeds, poisons and disease, and may also add swing speed. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-10-17–2021-10-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/23020/)
- **taste identification effects**: Taste Identification strengthens poison builds by increasing the potency of your own poisons, including Poison spell damage, and makes them harder to cure; it also improves energy regained from eating and gives poison resistance. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-10-17–2022-07-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25561/)
- **taste identification poison damage bonus**: Player-applied poison, from spells or weapons, deals extra damage against creatures equal to 50% multiplied by Taste Identification skill divided by 100, so a 120 skill poisoner deals substantially more damage. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-10–2024-04-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29281/)
- **tracking and forensic evaluation bard bonus**: Tracking and Forensic Evaluation increase a bard's chance of successfully using barding skills, though the effect is modest. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-17–2019-03-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11476/)
- **tracking hamstring ability**: Having 80 Tracking on a template with Wrestling grants the Hamstring special ability, and Tracking also adds spell damage and enables scouting. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-06–2021-03-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21179/)
- **veterinary for tamers**: Tamers who take Peacemaking, and mage tamers with Magery and Meditation, generally do not need Veterinary. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2020-02-26–2021-05-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21822/)
- **viewing skill gain chance**: Click your own character and choose 'show skill gain chance', or type the command [showskillgain, to see your percentage chance to gain skill on each action; an active gains bonus shows the chance raised by 5x or 10x. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-10-02–2024-10-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30506/)
- **wrestling benefits**: Wrestling is valuable for both PvM and PvP defense: it greatly reduces enemy hit chance, which lowers damage taken and spell interruption, and it gives a mana refund chance and raises the refund cap for casters. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2021-03-19–2026-07-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33469/)
- **120 taming pet strength**: Pets tamed at 120 Taming deal noticeably more damage than lower-level pets (estimated 30-50% more offense, not double) but are not much more durable than pets tamed at 105-110. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-15–2019-11-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15675/)
- **aegis imp taming location**: Aegis Imps spawn as 4 imps near the stairs to level 2 of Aegis Keep and can be dragged to a secluded hallway for taming; PKs may frequent the area, so taming naked reduces the risk of being targeted, and they are a target for 100-102.5 Taming. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-06-10–2019-02-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10276/)
- **afk macro training**: Players use macros to train skills AFK overnight and keep their active bonus, though the gains from overnight macroing are very slow. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-19–2019-01-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7837/)
- **alchemy and explosion potions**: GM Alchemy gives a large damage bonus to explosion potions, and recent Alchemy buffs have made explosion potions stronger for mage burst combos. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-10–2024-10-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30426/)
- **alchemy explosive potion damage**: Alchemy gives damage bonuses to Explosive Potions, which hit very hard against groups of creatures and work in both PvM and PvP. With Telekinesis, sticky potions can be thrown at creatures or players. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-09-01–2021-03-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21179/)
- **alchemy on mages**: Alchemy gives a solo mage some survivability and cash, but the benefit is dulled because Magery alone provides most of the same benefits, so it is mainly useful for crafting and for PvP damage with greater explosion potions. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-09-25–2021-03-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21166/)
- **alchemy potion duration**: Alchemy increases potion buff duration, so even low skill (around 20) is useful for PvM characters and extra alchemy points are popular, though 120 alchemy on an adventurer is rare. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-10–2022-04-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24966/)
- **alchemy versus parrying for defense**: Players generally prefer Alchemy over Parrying for defense: heal potions save mana and cast time, Panacea can nullify disease, and Explosion potions are a good early damage source, while Parry is good but unreliable against burst damage; Parrying pairs well with Void Aspect since healing comes anyway. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-26–2022-09-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25937/)
- **anatomy for healing**: Anatomy increases the amount healed by bandages, and about 60 Anatomy is required to cure poison with the Healing skill. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-05–2019-09-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14946/)
- **ancient daemon casting skill requirements**: Casting ancient daemons requires at least 200 skill points in the summoning skills, and about 300 with Meditation if you want to cast many of them. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-27–2018-11-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5327/)
- **animal taming gain limits per creature**: Maximum Taming gain per creature is 5.0 at 0-50 skill, 1.0 at 50-70, 0.5 at 70-90, and 0.2 at 90-119.9. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-27–2020-03-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16764/)
- **animal taming skill needed for pets**: Taming familiars requires about 55 Animal Taming, while the best tamable pets require around 115-120. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-05-30–2018-12-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6037/)
- **animal taming training cycle**: Train Taming in batches of 2.5: tame creatures until you have 2.5 skill of stored passive gains, then kill mobs with your pets until the stored points are used, and repeat. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-27–2020-03-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16682/)
- **archery backstab training at zoo**: Train archery with backstab at the Prevalia Zoo by running a Razor script that repeatedly hides, stealths, toggles war mode and attacks a monster about every 20 seconds; stand behind other players and target a monster they are already fighting so you are not aggro'd, since aggro prevents re-hiding. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-05-14–2021-02-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20716/)
- **arms lore combat bonuses**: Arms Lore at 80 adds about +8% special-attack chance (18% with fast stacking on maces) and enables disarm, which grants a damage boost (about +25) in PvM. This makes it valuable in PvM templates but not very useful in PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-22–2020-05-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17450/)
- **arms lore effect**: Arms Lore increases your weapon special move chance, which can substantially increase damage. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-05–2019-10-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15293/)
- **arms lore effects**: Arms Lore increases how often weapon specials trigger and lets you disarm mobs for extra damage (roughly 25% more for you and your pets at 80 skill). _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-08-12–2019-09-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14946/)
- **arms lore for dexer tamers**: A dexer tamer can drop Arms Lore without a major DPS loss only when wearing leveled Water Aspect armor, which gives an extra weapon proc chance; other aspects perform poorly without it. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-08-08–2020-08-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18620/)
- **backstab archery training**: Training Archery by shooting while hidden (backstab) greatly raises skill gain chance (about 24% vs 2% at 86 archery, 29% at 99.9 with 5 dex) and uses far less ammo; lower dex improves gain chance, and it also trains Hiding, Stealth, Tactics and Arms Lore. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-05-14–2021-02-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20716/)
- **banking and using passive taming gains**: To bank a passive taming gain, kill the newly tamed animal while it is still tamed after gaining 0.1 active taming; spend the bank by hunting with pets, which must do the damage (the player may assist), and with a full 2.5 bank at a tier like 52.5 hunting 2.5 gains takes you to the next tier (e.g. 55) and resets the bank to 0. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-06-10–2018-12-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7610/)
- **bard builds**: A bard summoner build needs decent Lyric aspect, high-level instruments, and 120 Music, Peace and Discord, and cannot fight back against PKs but can reach very high DPS, while the 720-cap Provo Bard uses Lyric Aspect with 120 Music, 120 Provocation, 100 Magery, Evaluating Intelligence, Meditation and Tracking, and 80 Spirit Speak, needs no tank summon, and uses Tracking to detect PKs. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-08–2020-11-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19478/)
- **bard codex self taught**: The Bard Codex Self Taught effect lets a player substitute up to 40/80/120 points (by level) of their lowest printed skill among Discordance, Peacemaking, and Provocation for Musicianship requirements, so all three skills must have printed points or the replacement is effectively 0. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-12-18–2024-12-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30956/)
- **bard skill cap from musicianship**: Bard skill caps tied to Musicianship apply only to bonuses to bard skills, so a character training Musicianship to 120 will never hit it, and it matters only for unusual builds with low bard skill investment. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-09–2020-01-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16201/)
- **bard skill gain on success**: Bard skills have a higher gain chance on a success than on a failure, possibly about twice as high, so easily barded small critters train faster than hard-to-bard targets, which mostly fail and still give reduced gains. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-01-15–2022-05-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25283/)
- **bard skill success rate**: Bard skills such as Provocation and Peacemaking can fail often on Outlands, but better instruments and Lyric armor increase the chance of success. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-16–2019-03-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11473/)
- **being noticed stealing outside town**: If a thief is noticed by players or human NPCs outside of town, it only generates a system message notification with no other consequences. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-11–2019-04-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13002/)
- **best places to steal**: Dungeons and farm spots are the best places to steal, along with player vendors, while bank thieving is mostly a waste of time; stealing in dungeons can yield T-maps and aspects. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-17–2019-04-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13041/)
- **camping skill training**: Camping can be trained alongside lumberjacking by making kindling: harvest logs, turn them into boards with a hatchet, then use a saw on the boards with about 50 carpentry, or use a dagger on a tree without carpentry. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-17–2018-11-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5061/)
- **carpentry training wood cost**: Raising Carpentry is very expensive in wood: roughly 135k logs from 50 to 100, with reports of about another 100k to reach 120, totaling roughly 250k logs overall. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-11-16–2020-08-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18611/)
- **cartography for treasure maps**: Cartography is only needed to decode treasure maps, and once a map is decoded anyone can use it, so the skill can be kept on a separate alt or house character. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-18–2019-08-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14548/)
- **cave bears for taming training**: Cave Bears in the New Player Dungeon are non-aggressive taming targets for roughly 60 to 70 Taming, with multiple spawns including two in the far north of level 1. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-22–2020-03-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16682/)
- **core skills by role**: Tactics, Anatomy and Evaluating Intelligence are core damage skills, and Magery, Meditation and Evaluating Intelligence are the core mage skills, which should be grandmastered. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-10-05–2022-07-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25561/)
- **crafting and gathering skill damage bonuses**: Blacksmithing and Carpentry give minor damage bonuses with player-crafted blacksmith weapons and carpentry items respectively, and gathering skills like Lumberjacking (axes), Mining (maces), and Fishing (spears) give similar minor weapon damage bonuses. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **crafting skill gain chance per item**: Smithing skill gain chance is the same for any item that can give skill, regardless of the item's success chance. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-22–2019-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11974/)
- **deciphering treasure maps**: To decipher a treasure map, use the Cartography skill with a tinker-crafted Spyglass by double clicking the spyglass and targeting the map. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-08-11–2021-02-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20343/)
- **defensive barding**: Defensive barding, added 28/09/2020, gives bards effective Wrestling and Magic Resist bonuses as long as they do not fight back in PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-03-28–2021-05-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21822/)
- **detect hidden checks**: Detect Hidden rolls a skill check against the hider's Hiding skill, so low Detect Hidden does not reliably reveal hidden players. It may also detect hidden traps on chests, which is unconfirmed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-07–2019-06-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13877/)
- **detect hidden radius**: Detect Hidden reveals all Hidden targets within 8 * (Detect Hidden skill / 100) tiles of the player, so at zero skill the radius is 1 tile. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-26–2018-11-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5322/)
- **dexxers in group pvp**: Dexxers can be viable in group PvP when run in small numbers with mages to kite and apply pressure, and they are now commonly expected to carry some Magery. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-28–2025-01-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31022/)
- **discordance skill gain**: Discordance will not gain skill if the player is at skill cap (a skill must be set to decrease) or if Peacemaking was used first. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-03–2019-05-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13282/)
- **drake taming targets**: Drake Whelps and Drakes are taming targets in the mid-80s range, found in the mountains Northwest of Horseshoe Bay and on Nusero level 2; Dragon Whelps and Flame Purgers in Nusero are used for the 90s, and Drake Whelps are also usable earlier (about 75-77.5). _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-06-10–2019-02-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10275/)
- **dungeon chest picking with hiding and stealth**: Grandmaster Hiding and Stealth make picking dungeon chests easier and help avoid player killers, and a treasure-map-picking character can also do stealth chest picking in dungeons. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-17–2019-08-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14548/)
- **flame strike scroll preservation**: Reaching about 125 effective Inscription (126 in an older claim) prevents Flame Strike scrolls from being consumed, and Inscription also provides spell disruption immunity; Eldritch aspect armor can raise effective Inscription. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-11-18–2022-09-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25937/)
- **focus skill benefits**: Focus is a passive skill that boosts mana generation, refund chance and charged spell chance, and per one player it also adds a damage bonus, swing speed and accuracy that help ensure a 100% hit rate below T15 Arcane Aspect. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-09-05–2026-07-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33469/)
- **forensic evaluation and skinning**: Using a skinning knife activates Forensic Evaluation, so you gain that skill if it is not locked, and it is commonly trained to 100 for skinning. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-03-04–2025-03-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31506/)
- **forensic evaluation skinning yield**: Forensic Evaluation increases the hides and meat gained from skinning corpses, with more at higher skill. This supplements early gold income since both sell to vendors for about 1 gold each, and it gives a small slayer bonus against the skinned monster type. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-06-28–2018-10-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3251/)
- **forensics skill bonuses**: Forensics gives extra leather when skinning corpses and a damage bonus, reported as +25% damage at 100 skill. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-05–2021-05-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21755/)
- **healing pets**: Tamers and rangers usually need Veterinary to heal pets, because Magery healing is poor given pets' large HP pools and mana runs out quickly without Evaluating Intelligence. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-11–2021-05-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21930/)
- **healing skill target level**: Healing at 80 gives a decent amount of healing and works as a fallback alongside potions; players who do not plan to resurrect with Healing can stay at 80, while those who do should take it to about 90. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-04–2020-01-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16213/)
- **herding focused aggression for summoners**: Summoners can use Herding's Focused Aggression to increase the damage their summoned followers deal to a target, and Herding is recommended over Forensic Evaluation for getting more damage from summons. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-09-01–2019-04-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12376/)
- **hiding and stealth skill use delay**: Hiding and Stealth on Outlands have a skill use delay of about 10 seconds between uses, so training macros pause roughly 10-12 seconds between skill uses. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-18–2018-11-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4594/)
- **inscription chain link**: A chain link that increases effective Inscription skill exists in Outlands: the spirit speak/inscription chain link. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-10-20–2022-10-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26253/)
- **inscription protection and bless duration**: Inscription gives duration enhancements to the Protection and Bless spells, letting a caster give group members long-lasting improvements. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-09-01–2019-11-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15655/)
- **inscription reactive armor bonus**: Inscription increases Reactive Armor's maximum melee damage prevention by an additional 40 * (Inscription Skill / 100). _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-10-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3572/)
- **inscription scroll conservation**: Casting from scrolls gives a chance not to consume the scroll: (40% * Inscription/100) for circles 1-6 and (80% * Inscription/100) for circles 7-8, so 96% at 120 Inscription for circles 7-8, with the remainder of the full chance coming from chain links or eldritch armor. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-21–2022-10-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26255/)
- **inscription spell duration bonuses**: Inscription extends the durations of Bless, Arch Protection and Invisibility to 500% * (Inscription Skill / 100) of normal, which increases group melee survival. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-09-01–2018-10-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3572/)
- **inscription training**: Inscription needs about 65,000 scrolls and roughly 12 hours of crafting to reach GM, with short-term gains varying widely from the average. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-10-24–2020-11-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19461/)
- **item id training macro**: To raise Item ID, set up a macro that repeatedly identifies a single item and let it run while away from the keyboard. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-15–2019-01-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8390/)
- **item identification effect**: Item Identification enhances the magical properties of worn gear by a percentage, and at level 100 it gives a 100% boost to the magic weapon property rather than a 100% boost to weapon damage. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-06-18–2024-07-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29985/)
- **item identification skill**: The Item Identification skill is used to identify unidentified items in Outlands. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-29–2018-12-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6009/)
- **kill bonus eligible creatures**: The skillgain bonus requires killing a creature that is not summoned or tamed (even if released), but it does not need to be a monster, so harmless animals such as skylarks or kingfishers qualify. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-05–2018-11-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3891/)
- **kill skill gain bonus**: Killing an animal or monster grants a 5x skill gain chance bonus for about 10 minutes for active skills, excluding crafting, harvesting and taming. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-02–2019-01-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8322/)
- **magery gain from spell scrolls**: Casting a spell from a scroll gives the same chance to increase Magery skill as casting the same spell with reagents. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-22–2020-12-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19796/)
- **magery requirement by spell circle**: The minimum Magery to cast a spell is its circle number times 10, and Magery 20 points above that minimum means you never fizzle (for example 40 to cast and 60 to never fizzle 4th circle). _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-02–2018-11-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4664/)
- **magery training 0-50**: Magery from 0-50 can be trained by buying training from a mage vendor or by using the 'teach magery' option. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-15–2019-05-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13228/)
- **magery training path**: Train Magery by casting Magic Arrow from 0-30, Fireball 30-40, Lightning 40-60, Energy Bolt 60-70, Flamestrike 70-90, and Summon Air Elemental 90-100. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-10-24–2018-12-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6434/)
- **magery training progression**: Suggested Magery training is to macro Magic Arrow to 30, Fireball to 40, Lightning to 60, Energy Bolt to 70, Flamestrike to 90, then Summon Air Elemental to 100; an alternative after buying to 50 is Mana Drain, Invisibility, Mana Vampire, then Summon Elemental, and while hunting it is Lightning, Mind Blast, Energy Bolt, Flame Strike, then Summon Elementals, with the last 10 points best macroed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-28–2019-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11975/)
- **magic resist potions and alchemy skill**: Replacing the Resisting Spells skill with spell resist potions takes roughly 90 Alchemy, and large potion-based society jobs succeed far more reliably at legendary Alchemy than at GM. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-12-09–2021-04-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21445/)
- **maximum skill gain journal message**: When a creature can no longer give skill gains, the system journal displays a message beginning 'You have already received the maximum', which macros use to detect maxed gains from that target. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-14–2020-08-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18623/)
- **meditation training with wizard hat**: To train Meditation, equip a magical wizard hat, remove it when mana is full, then put it back on and repeat; this is reported to work to reach GM, though the second source refers to 'some shards' and not specifically Outlands. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-14–2022-05-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25271/)
- **melodious instrument barding bonuses**: Melodious instruments add skill for success/failure checks: Melodious +5, Surpassingly +10, Eminently +15, Exceedingly +20, Supremely +25. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-03–2020-01-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16201/)
- **mining skill bonus to mace damage**: The Mining skill boosts Mace Fighting damage on Ultima Online: Outlands. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-01–2019-02-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9411/)
- **musicianship training**: Musicianship (and Peacemaking) can be trained to 70 by macroing the starter instrument inside the newbie dungeon entrance, which takes about 45 minutes. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-10–2019-02-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10275/)
- **notice chance modifiers near thief**: Each non-friendly player within 8 tiles adds 10% to the notice chance (5% without line of sight), and each human NPC within 8 tiles adds 5% (2.5% without line of sight). _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-11–2019-04-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13002/)
- **npc guard calling when stealing**: Town NPCs can see through walls when detecting stealing, so stealing from NPCs in town has a higher chance of the NPC calling guards. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-09–2024-05-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29549/)
- **npc skill training**: To train a skill with an NPC trainer, click the NPC to open the context menu, select 'Train <skill>', then drag and drop your training credits deed or new-player ticket onto the NPC to pay the cost; the ticket is worth 1000 training credits (100 skill points). _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-08-17–2019-02-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10038/)
- **npc training cost**: NPC trainers train skills up to 50 at a cost of 10 gold per 1 skill point (1 gold per 0.1 skill), paid by dragging gold onto the NPC. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-08-17–2018-10-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2753/)
- **opening trapped chests**: Use Remove Trap on a chest before lockpicking it, because a trap that goes off can hurt you or kill you. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-05-30–2018-08-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1943/)
- **passive taming gain from pets**: When your pet damages or kills mobs there is a chance to trigger stored passive Taming gain, and the chance of a 0.1 gain scales with the mob's difficulty as seen with Animal Lore; the player does not need to land the kill. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-22–2020-03-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16682/)
- **passive tracking of hostile players**: The Tracking skill gives passive, continuous detection of incoming hostile players, both grays and murderers (reds). _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-10-03–2020-05-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17439/)
- **peacemaking defensive use**: Peacemaking acts as a roughly 40-second stun on a single enemy and is a strong defensive skill that immobilizes enemies and reduces the need for bandages. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-09-03–2018-06-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1310/)
- **poisoning skill and poison spell**: The Poisoning skill strengthens a player's Poison spell, giving a chance to upgrade it to Greater or Deadly poison. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-09-01–2018-08-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2027/)
- **pure mage skill choices**: A pure mage builds around Magery and Inscription, with remaining slots filled by skills such as Alchemy, Forensic Evaluation, Tracking (damage and Hamstring), Arms Lore (Disarm and Hamstring), Camping, Resisting Spells, or Parry depending on preference. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-04–2020-12-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19976/)
- **pvm template variety**: Many skills, such as Camping, Forensic Evaluation and Spirit Speak, have useful PvM purposes on Outlands, so PvM templates differ drastically from PvP templates. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-01-16–2019-03-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12017/)
- **resisting spells absorb ability**: Resisting Spells grants an ability (at 80 skill) that absorbs a spell and stores up to 250 damage, which can be released within 5 minutes as extra damage on a melee strike. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-05–2019-01-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8694/)
- **self resurrection with healing**: A player cannot resurrect themselves with the Healing skill and needs another player or another source to be resurrected. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-24–2019-01-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8804/)
- **skill gain at 700 point cap**: Below the 700 total skill point cap all skills can only gain, so leaving them set to raise is beneficial; once at the cap, a character gains no skill unless at least one skill is marked down. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-05-30–2018-12-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7368/)
- **skill gain design**: Skill gain on Outlands favors active play over AFK macroing, and gain is quick for fighting skills, hiding, picking, barding, taming and magery. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-21–2023-01-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26935/)
- **skill gain difficulty**: Crafting skill gain on Outlands is intentionally difficult and resource-heavy, reportedly to limit the number of Grandmaster and Legendary craftsmen, while other non-harvesting skills level fairly easily. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-13–2024-12-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30867/)
- **skill gain rate by skill level**: All skills gain quickly up to 50, and after 70 gain is slow enough that training essentially requires the +25 percent skill gain dungeon. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-19–2018-12-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7358/)
- **skill mastery scrolls**: Skill mastery scrolls raise a skill's cap above 100, for example 20 Musicianship scrolls allow Musicianship up to 120, but total skill points still cannot exceed the character's total skill cap. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-08-20–2021-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21271/)
- **skill point cap**: Characters can learn any skills they want with no class system, up to a total cap of 700 skill points, and easily raised support skills can be used temporarily then dropped from the template. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-02–2018-10-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3853/)
- **snooping delay**: The 2 second delay between snooping attempts applies only to a target's main backpack, while snooping a container inside the backpack has a 1 second delay. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-10–2019-02-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9631/)
- **snooping training**: To train Snooping, use two accounts and meet in a city or house, then have one character repeatedly snoop the other's backpack, for example with a Razor macro that double-clicks the backpack in the other character's paperdoll. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-08-24–2024-02-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28828/)
- **spirit speaking spell damage and charged spells**: Spirit Speaking increases the chance for a spell to become Charged, raising its damage, and also provides longer-lasting summons. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-09-01–2018-10-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3317/)
- **stealing and hiding**: On Outlands a thief cannot activate the stealing skill while remaining hidden, and the Stealth skill is not required for stealing. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-09–2021-01-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20064/)
- **stealing kit use**: Using a stealing kit (DK) and staying hidden improves stealing success on Outlands. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-14–2019-04-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12571/)
- **stealing success chance**: Base stealing success is 50% scaled by Stealing skill (50% * skill/100), so at 100 skill a thief has 50% to steal items of 10 stones or less, and being hidden adds a flat 25%; one older claim says weight does not matter. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-11–2019-04-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13002/)
- **stealth archer arms lore level**: Stealth archer builds commonly run Arms Lore at 80 rather than 100 to free skill points for other skills. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-21–2020-07-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18504/)
- **stealth hiding skill requirement**: Stealth on Outlands requires at least 80 Hiding skill to be used successfully, so training macros switch to Stealth once Hiding reaches 80. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-17–2018-11-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4573/)
- **swords mage pvp template**: The standard 1v1 PvP dueling template is a Swords Mage: Magery, Eval Intel, Meditation, Magic Resist, Wrestling, Swords and Tactics, with about 100 str, 25 dex and 100 int, and some skills can be lowered to fit extras like 80 tracking. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-07-29–2021-02-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20737/)
- **tamer follower slots**: A tamer has 5 follower control slots to fill with tamed pets. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-11-26–2022-02-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24362/)
- **tamer taming skill levels**: A tamer with only 50 taming and 5 mucks can farm about as well as a beginner peace dexer, and the strongest PvP tamers use only about 90 taming. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-07-25–2020-10-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19250/)
- **tamer templates**: Suggested tamer templates include Music, Peacemaking, Magery, Animal Taming, Animal Lore, Tracking and Veterinary for PvM farming, or Animal Taming, Animal Lore, Magery, Meditation, Evaluating Intelligence, Resisting Spells and Wrestling for surviving PKs. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-06-10–2022-09-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25989/)
- **tamer templates**: Community-suggested tamer templates include a bard tamer (Taming, Lore, Music and Peacemaking to 120, plus Tracking, Magery 90 and Meditation), a dexxer tamer (Taming, Lore, Veterinary, Healing, one weapon skill, Tactics, Resisting Spells) and a mage tamer (105 Taming, 105 Lore, 100 Magery, 100 Wrestling, 100 Meditation, 100 Herding, 90 Eval Int). _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-11–2019-07-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14384/)
- **taming at 70-72.5 skill**: At 70-72.5 Animal Taming, players train on Scarabs, of which two spawn in the Ossuary Desert south of the Ossuary entrance, and Cave Bats near the vampire are also an option. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-03-01–2020-07-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18462/)
- **taming spots inside houses**: Taming spots inside houses no longer work after a 2019 patch. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-06-10–2019-06-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13859/)
- **taming training creatures 50-65**: For taming 50-60, Colossal Frogs in the New Player Dungeon work well (passive, near Corpsers); for 50-55 Mongbats also work, 55-65 adds Firebat and Flamehound in Inferno plus Familiar and Corpse Eater in the Noob dungeon, and 60-65 adds Fire Salamander in Inferno and Giant Spider in the Noob dungeon; Inferno pays better gold but hits much harder. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-06-10–2020-03-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16682/)
- **tank mage farming viability**: A tank mage is a poor farmer on Outlands compared to OSI, so players generally need a dedicated farming character such as a bard or tamer to get ahead. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-20–2018-12-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7385/)
- **tank pet for afk taming script**: A Rock Guar or Colossal Frog is a recommended tank pet for the AFK taming script, with a guar used when taming colossal frogs. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-05-15–2022-08-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25816/)
- **taste identification for poisoning**: Taste Identification helps with applying poison, increasing poison strength, with a skill benchmark around 80, and also helps with cure rates. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2020-05-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17439/)
- **thief template skill cost**: A thief template in Outlands spends about 400 of its 700 skill points on thievery-related skills that provide no bonuses to other activities. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-10-13–2022-04-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24923/)
- **time to gm archery with backstab**: Using the backstab zoo script, players report reaching GM Archery within roughly 5 to 24 hours, with one player using about 2000 arrows. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-06-06–2020-09-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18919/)
- **tinkering training 50 to 120**: Tinkering can be trained from 50 to 120 by crafting Lanterns from 50-75, Iron Lockpicks from 75-80, Keyrings from 80-100, and Candelabras from 100-120. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-10-24–2018-01-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/781/)
- **tinkering trap template**: A tinkering trap character with Hiding and Stealth is considered fun to play, suited to solo treasure map hunting, stealthing through dungeons to unlock chests, and decent at shrine corruptions. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-07-18–2019-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14311/)
- **tracking combat bonuses**: At GM, Tracking extends red detection range, raises detection success from 80% to 100%, and adds a damage bonus (about 20% at 80 skill) that is the same for all weapon types; it can be trained to GM very quickly. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-13–2020-03-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16804/)
- **tracking range**: Tracking range is roughly 100 tiles at 100.0 skill, halved to 50 tiles inside a dungeon; at 120 tracking the dungeon range is reportedly about 58 tiles. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-05-05–2021-06-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22008/)
- **tracking skill benefits**: The Tracking skill provides a damage boost and red alert, and its other benefits are listed in the skills summary. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-18–2019-05-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13554/)
- **tracking skill benefits**: The Tracking skill provides good bonus damage, levels quickly, helps level stats, and reveals nearby players in dungeons and the overlands. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-07–2025-12-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32618/)
- **tracking skill bonus**: Tracking set to hostile (red) targets gives a PvM damage bonus (reported as about 25%) and can show an overhead alert about five seconds before a red arrives; the setting must be reapplied at each login. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-24–2024-02-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28886/)
- **tracking skill damage bonus**: The Tracking skill grants a PvM damage bonus of 25% at GM, rising to 30% at 120 tracking, and it also lets you track incoming hostile players. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-05-05–2021-06-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22008/)
- **tracking skill effects**: Tracking on Outlands works against hidden players at a reduced rate, warns of incoming red and grey players, and reportedly gives a damage boost and a bard boost. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-06–2020-01-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16043/)
- **tracking skill for tamers**: Tamers generally lack protection against player killers, so Tracking is useful for spotting nearby reds and PKs to avoid them, but it does not significantly increase tamer DPS and is not strictly necessary. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-02-26–2020-08-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18570/)
- **tracking skill passive hostile detection**: The Tracking skill offers passive hostile tracking, letting players spot red (PK) players before they arrive so they can flee, and it is developed alongside Resist under PK threat. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2026-04-29–2026-07-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33319/)
