- **training box gain rate**: The Training Box for Lockpicking and Remove Trap gives slow skill gains, at roughly 1/10th of the normal rate. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-16–2018-12-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7180/)
- **training combat skills on another player near town**: To train combat skills against your own characters or a partner without a house or guild, go outside town away from other players, attack each other so you go gray, wait about 2 minutes until you are no longer gray to anyone but each other, then return to town and keep fighting safely. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-23–2019-06-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14000/)
- **training lockpicking and remove trap with lockbox**: Use the training lockbox to raise Lockpicking and Remove Trap up to 120, unless it is bugged, and around 78-80 skill raise Remove Trap on the box so it stays above Lockpicking. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-15–2018-12-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7278/)
- **training parrying**: To train Parrying, gather many weak animals (for example in a graveyard) and take their hits with 0 weapon skill; each mob gives limited skill, so periodically kill them all and wait for respawn. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-10-24–2019-09-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14811/)
- **training resisting spells in newbie dungeon**: To train Resisting Spells (and similar skills) up to 70, fight orc mages and imps in the newbie dungeon; one player went from 0 to 70 in about 45 minutes. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-21–2019-03-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11535/)
- **warrior template recommendations**: A common warrior template uses 100 Str, 100 Dex and 25 Int, with Tactics, Anatomy, Healing, Parrying and a weapon skill at 100; Camping, Focus and Resisting Spells are optional additions in some builds. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-09-01–2025-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31071/)
- **95 taming range**: The 95 skill range is considered the most tedious taming range before reaching grandmaster. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-03-09–2020-03-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16765/)
- **animal taming training difficulty**: Animal Taming cannot be trained with an AFK macro, whereas Magery can, so taming takes more active effort to train. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-27–2018-11-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5272/)
- **arms lore for dexxers**: Dexxers take Arms Lore (around 80) in both PvP and PvM because the Disarm and Hamstring abilities depend on it. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-10-01–2019-05-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13169/)
- **bard tamer stats**: Khal Drogo's bard tamer template uses 100 Strength, 25 Dexterity and 100 Intelligence. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-05-29–2019-05-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13697/)
- **item id macro training time**: Item Identification can reportedly be macro-trained to GM in a few days of AFK training, though one player reported it took a month without active gain. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 3 authors, 2019-01-15–2019-01-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8396/)
- **item id skill gain display**: At low skill levels the skill gain display may show a very low chance (for example 2.3%) for Item ID, which still means training works and will eventually gain, and a recent change may have made gains faster. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-01-15–2019-01-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8363/)
- **kill skillgain bonus**: Killing a creature grants a 5x skillgain bonus for 10 minutes, so killing something about every 10 minutes keeps it active while training. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-04–2018-11-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3862/)
- **magery training reagent savings**: Training Magery with the 5x kill skillgain bonus active needs roughly 5x fewer reagents than training without it. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-04–2018-11-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3862/)
- **overworld training spawns**: Overworld camps and spawns suitable for training include the Andaria graveyard, the Cambria desert pyramids, lizardman ruins and orc forts. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-15–2018-12-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7126/)
- **pet bonding after leveling**: A pet only needs to level up once to become bonded, and once leveled it stays bonded even if the owner lowers Animal Taming and Animal Lore to 0. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-27–2018-12-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7593/)
- **sacred journey tactics requirement**: Sacred Journey requires 90 Tactics as well as Chivalry above 90, because effective Chivalry is capped by Tactics. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-07-02–2024-07-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29914/)
- **skill gain and training to 100**: Staff said in 2017 they did not intend to make skill gain painful, but current player advice is that taking most skills to 100.0 usually isn't worth it, and stopping at 80.0 to spend the saved points on one or two more skills is usually better. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 1 author, 2024-11-08–2024-11-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30684/)
- **taming aggressive creatures with pets**: To keep an aggressive tameable target occupied, order your pets to 'all patrol' near it, then once combat starts order 'all stay' or 'all stop' and tame while healing your pet; pets do not auto-attack after being told to stop (this is uncertain). _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-31–2018-12-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7713/)
- **taming difficulty changes**: Outlands developers increased taming difficulty after the beta guide was written, including changing the taming requirements of some creatures, so older taming guides may list outdated skill ranges. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-10-22–2018-10-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3457/)
- **taming near houses**: Taming cannot be done inside houses; instead, place a pet inside the house on stay so it gets flagged, then stand outside the house and tame it. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-06-09–2020-06-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18000/)
- **taming training creatures 65-85**: For taming 65-85, use Inferno creatures (Firebat, Flamehound, Fire Salamander, Fire Lizard, Embear, then Searing Mantis and Searing Imp), Ossuary creatures (Giant Scorpion, Fire Ant, Scarab, Cave Bat), Aegis Slimes in Aegis Keep at 75-80, and Drake Whelp in Nusero at 80-85. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-06-10–2018-06-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1279/)
- **total skill cap**: The total skill cap is 720 points, and a later build can raise one skill to 126 Inscription. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-11-18–2019-11-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15646/)
- **training after young status**: Players who have lost Young status can buy reagents from vendors outside Sanctuary and return to the Sanctuary dungeon to finish raising skills such as Magery to 80. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2025-06-13–2025-06-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31888/)
- **training lockpicking, remove trap and cartography to 120**: Lockpicking, Remove Trap and Cartography can reportedly reach 120 by raising them above 100 using the +20 skill bonuses. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-01-02–2019-01-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7788/)
- **unattended lumberjacking training**: It is not possible to train the Lumberjacking skill unattended without collecting the resources. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-11-28–2019-11-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15748/)
- **wrestling tamer build**: Adding Wrestling to a tamer, together with GM Resist and a command suit, lets the tamer survive PKs and farm effectively. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-05-23–2019-05-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13627/)

## Harvesting & resources

- **mah captcha harvesting restriction**: Additional Multiple Account Household accounts cannot perform any Harvesting actions that require a Captcha. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-09-15–2025-09-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32235/)
- **tier 4 lustrous hues**: Tier 4 Lustrous hues (4124-4141, such as Lustrous Lavender 4124, Carmine 4125 and Dewdrop 4141) are obtained as an Ultra Rare Cloth monster drop. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **paragon chest lockpicking requirements**: Lesser paragon chests need about 80 Lockpicking and Remove Trap, and greater paragon chests need roughly 95-110 Lockpicking with Verite or better tools, with success chances still low. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2018-11-14–2020-02-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16408/)
- **valorite skinning knife bonus**: A valorite skinning knife multiplies the base 4% colored leather chance by 1.16, giving 4.64%, which is only a 0.64 percentage-point increase (about 16% more colored leather relative to base). _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-12-08–2018-12-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6551/)
- **forensics leather income**: Gathering extra leather through Forensics can be sold for income and can be a path to affording a house and a blessed runebook. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-03-04–2019-11-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15551/)
- **overland spawns**: Outlands has plentiful, profitable overland spawns that are easy to recall in and out of and need no dungeon run to start fighting, so a mount and Recall make them low-risk to farm. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-11–2019-02-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9474/)
- **custom tree graphics**: Outlands has many custom trees not covered by default tree hacks, with reported tree graphic IDs including 3563, 3565, 3527 and 3524. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-10-05–2020-10-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19141/)
- **fishing purpose**: Fishing in Ultima Online: Outlands is mostly used to acquire cooking ingredients for crafting delectable food, in addition to gold from crates. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-06-26–2022-07-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25534/)
- **forensics skinning loot bonus**: Forensics increases loot from skinning creatures, yielding more feathers and leather, and also increases spell damage against frequently fought enemies. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-09-01–2019-01-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8324/)
- **gathering kindling**: To gather kindling, stand near trees and use a dagger on a tree, or on yourself while near trees, which yields 1 kindling per use. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-09-04–2020-03-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16693/)
- **gathering kindling for camping**: Use a dagger on a tree to gather kindling, which is used to start camp fires for the Camping skill; no lumberjacking is needed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-26–2018-11-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3881/)
- **harvesting captcha**: Outlands shows an anti-macro CAPTCHA roughly every 20-30 minutes while using a gathering skill (logs, ore, fishing, wool, hides), not on every harvest. Harvesting cannot continue until it is answered, so automated harvesting works only while the player is present. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-01-12–2018-11-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4565/)
- **message in a bottle fishing**: Message in a bottle fishing on Outlands can yield a couple thousand gold per crate, but it is somewhat buggy and sometimes fails to give the guaranteed creature spawn. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-05–2019-06-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14044/)
- **mining locations**: Mining can be done on mountainsides or inside caves in Outlands; caves still exist, but mountain faces are also minable. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-01–2019-02-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10038/)
- **mining ore color per spot**: A mining spot yields only one ore type until it refreshes, and a single tile never gives two ore colors in one round of harvesting. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-10–2019-06-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13831/)
- **reagent farming**: Reagents can be farmed on Outlands because many monsters, including some high-end ones, drop large stacks of a single reagent (50-75) on death. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-07–2018-10-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3562/)
- **special fishing spots**: Special fishing spots exist where monsters can occasionally spawn while fishing, at a rate players estimate at about one monster per two spots. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-20–2019-03-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10984/)
- **auto-movement removal**: Auto-movement is not possible on Outlands; it was removed relatively recently, and players previously triple-boxed gathering for large profits. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2023-10-29–2023-10-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28432/)
- **avarhide forensic evaluation requirement**: Getting avarhide reportedly requires an effective Forensic Evaluation skill of about 110. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2021-10-20–2021-10-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/23237/)
- **captcha anti-botting during gathering**: Outlands uses captchas during resource gathering as an anti-botting measure, though some players reportedly find ways around them. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-01-23–2019-01-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8753/)
- **paragon chest tool selection**: The paragon chest gump does not automatically use the best tool; before lockpicking or removing the trap, click through the arrows on the gump to select the correct tool type (e.g. gold tools). _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-02-03–2020-02-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16401/)
- **sanctuary dungeon safe farming**: The Sanctuary dungeon is a lower-risk area for farming where players can avoid murderers. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2025-10-28–2025-10-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32400/)

## Crafting

- **druid's tome requirement**: Using Druidism Animal Forms requires a Druid's Tome, which is Blessed and craftable with Inscription. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **gemshards**: Gemshards are the ingredient cost for summoning a Golem; they are crafted with Tinkering in bundles from multiple Gems (Citrines, Tourmalines, Amber, etc.), and each successful construction consumes 1 Gemshard. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **golem creation toolbox**: To construct a Golem, craft a Golem Creation Toolbox (a Blessed item made with Tinkering) and double-click it to open a menu showing each Golem type. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **monsterbone crafting material**: Outlands may introduce a new high-level material for Armor and Weapons that players craft using rare Monsterbone ingredients, which drop only from high-end content such as Bosses, Omni-Bosses, and high-level Quests. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **arcane essence from recycling**: Arcane Essence, needed to craft Aspect Gear, is obtained by recycling magical items; the amount does not scale with crafting skill, and recycling works even with 0 crafting skill (for example with a tailoring kit, or by carrying an ID wand and tinker tools). _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-01-25–2024-11-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30732/)
- **item id wands**: Item ID wands and Container ID wands exist in Outlands; they are crafted by tinkers (requiring Item Identification skill) and can be bought from player vendors, and they are not monster drops. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-12-02–2024-12-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30832/)
- **arcane scroll uses**: Arcane scrolls are a finite resource used by Inscription crafters to make runebooks, spellbooks, codexes, grimoires, tomes, bestiaries, rune tomes and level 7-8 scrolls, so they should be saved rather than used for macro skill gain. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-08-27–2021-04-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21445/)
- **avarite weapons versus magic weapons**: Player-crafted avarite weapons are generally not worth using over high-end magic weapons, such as exceedingly damage increase weapons, which are arguably better and usually cheaper. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-07-03–2025-01-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31021/)
- **city crafting exceptional bonus**: The 15% city or regional crafting bonus to exceptional chance is multiplicative, adding 15% of your existing chance rather than a flat 15 percentage points, so a 50% chance becomes 57.5%. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-18–2025-12-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32649/)
- **clothing unbless deed**: The Clothing Unbless Deed lets a player unbless a clothing item so it can be cut up to recover its cloth, including special colors such as rare shimmers; it is not meant for the high-end trading market or for proving an item's clean or pre-patched status. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2025-12-31–2026-04-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32989/)
- **crafter income**: Crafting on Outlands is both interesting and financially worthwhile, and crafters earn their main income by crafting for societies rather than from vendor sales. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-08-22–2025-04-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31657/)
- **crafting design**: Crafting in Outlands is a resource- and time-intensive grind that is intentionally designed to make players rely on crafters; nearly everything can be crafted by the player, but it requires skills and gathered materials, and it can be profitable long term. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-14–2025-05-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31832/)
- **delectable food regeneration buffs**: Delectable food made from special fish buffs regeneration, with a 100% chance to restore 2 hits or 2 stamina and a 25% chance to restore 2 mana, and it adds about 5% to PvP mana regeneration. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-10–2023-02-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26968/)
- **efficient smithing training**: To train smithing efficiently, craft items shown in green on the crafting list, choosing the cheapest ones with the highest success chance, then smelt them to recover ingots. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-27–2019-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11974/)
- **id wands**: Players can buy ID wands, including container ID wands, to identify items without needing the Item ID skill. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-06-01–2022-04-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24966/)
- **making kindling**: To make kindling, use an axe on trees as a lumberjack to get logs, then use the saw in your backpack, select bowcraft/fletching, and craft the logs into kindling. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-10–2019-09-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14797/)
- **mastery chain link reforging tools**: Mastery chain link reforging tools come in bronze, silver and gold versions, each reforging links of the matching material into a link with a desired bonus, and can be bought with society points or for gold from the Prevalia vendor. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-29–2025-12-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32656/)
- **recycling magic items for essence**: Unwanted magic items can be broken down into arcane (magic) essence by using any crafting tool's recycle function, at the cost of losing the item's magic properties. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-03-23–2020-08-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18617/)
- **smithing ingot cost to grandmaster**: Reaching Grandmaster smithing takes roughly 200k to 220k ingots without recycling, or about 150k in practice if you recycle everything, and about the same again from GM to 120. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-10-24–2019-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11974/)
- **tailoring leather cost to gm**: Raising Tailoring to GM takes roughly 150,000 leather in practice if you recycle everything (up to about 200,000-220,000 without optimizing), and about 24 hours of straight crafting. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-10-24–2018-01-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/762/)
- **armor rating from crafting skills**: Blacksmithing increases the armor rating of metal armor and Tailoring increases the armor rating of leather armor, and the Blacksmithing bonus is reportedly counted in PvP as well as PvM. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-17–2019-03-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11445/)
- **artisan hue**: Outlands has Artisan hue items and an Artisan Bow, and the Artisan hue is a special hue distinct from standard dyes. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-12-09–2020-12-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19838/)
- **blacksmith armor rating and meditation penalty**: The armor rating bonus from Blacksmithing does not directly affect the meditation penalty from armor, so it does not make mindblasts hit blacksmiths harder. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-03-11–2019-03-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11445/)
- **cutting blessed items**: Blessed items cannot be cut up with scissors to obtain cloth. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2025-12-31–2025-12-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32689/)
- **ship crafting requirements**: The wiki lists a ship as requiring 1 Mastercrafting Diagram, 1000 Boards, 100 Iron Ingots and 100 Cloth, but its carpentry skill figure is outdated, so verify in game. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-04-24–2019-04-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12926/)
- **tailoring 95-100 training item**: For Tailoring from 95 to 100, crafting Studded Arms is recommended over tailoring repair kits because it uses much less leather when recycling. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-28–2018-12-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7632/)

## Combat & pvm

- **acid golem secondary skill**: The Acid Golem's Secondary Skill is the higher of Alchemy or Cooking; it focuses on area attacks and debuffing enemies and is largely immune to enemy debuffs. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **animal form bonuses and passives**: Animal Forms may have Form Bonuses giving constant bonuses (Melee Damage, Damage Resistance, Poison Resistance, etc.) and Passive Abilities that trigger randomly or in certain circumstances (Spell Reflection, Health Regen, Applying Poison, etc.). _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **animal form weapon modifications**: Some Animal Forms have Weapon Modifications that alter equipped weapon stats while in that form; for example, Goldtusk modifies the Wrestling Weapon stats to match a Quarter Staff (slower, heavier hits). _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **aoe spell area preview**: AoE spells can preview the area they affect using the color configured in Combat-Spells => Intention Hues, based on the spell's intention colors. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **cloth drop hue tiers**: Tier 1 Metallic hues (e.g., Cherry 2380, Orange 1989, Amber 1990, Sapphire 2379, Pewter 2167, Umber 1470) come from a Rare Cloth monster drop, and Tier 3 Powder hues (e.g., Ruby 2227, Coral 1495, Peach 1496, Lemon 1382, Snow 1590, Fallon 1266) come from an Ultra Rare Cloth monster drop. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **dungeon clothing loot sets**: Ultra rare Dungeon Clothing loot sets may be introduced, with each set's pieces found only as loot in its corresponding dungeon. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **elite island pvp arena**: Elite Island is a PvP arena for pre-formed teams of 3-5 with no rewards or leaderboards; matches are K/D-based, last up to 15 minutes, with instant respawns, mana nodes, and strict anti-automation rules. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **evolved creature traits**: Each [Evolved] Trait typically features one mechanic such as an activatable Ability, a Reaction, a unique damage Resistance, or another combat modification; for example, a Giant Spider with the Detonate trait randomly explodes during combat, damaging nearby targets. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **evolved creatures**: Any creature that can spawn as a Paragon or has a Societies Job can spawn as a rare [Evolved] creature, which gains a randomized Trait rather than a stat/skill scalar, and a creature can be both Paragon and Evolved. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **faction and pvp updates**: The expansion updates Arena and Faction Rewards, changes Faction scoring and VIPs, and adds Faction Champions with Aspect Triumph Fragments. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-01–2025-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32437/)
- **field guide damage bonus**: The Field Guide, craftable with Inscription, tracks Field Studies progress; the Global Damage Bonus and each specific creature bonus max out at +5% each, for up to a 10% total damage bonus against creatures. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **field spell placement preview**: With targeting options enabled under Options => General => Targeting, field spells preview their approximate placement on the target cursor, but the preview does not show whether a tile will block the field. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **field studies bonuses**: Field Studies grants permanent cumulative damage bonuses against a specific creature type, plus a smaller global damage bonus against all creatures, from researching rare [Evolved] creatures; bonuses apply to all damage types including followers and are shared across all characters on the OutlandsID. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **flesh golem secondary skill**: The Flesh Golem's Secondary Skill is the higher of Forensic Evaluation or Tailoring; it applies damage over time effects and is extremely resilient to non-physical damage. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **goldtusk animal form**: Goldtusk is a Melee Animal Form focused on slow, heavy-hitting attacks; it can debuff enemies, provide temporary damage shields, and increases damage when at reduced health. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **golem follower stats**: Golems are 5 Control Slot followers that are sturdy with decent damage; they require no Magery or Mana but have a construction duration and a Recovery Cooldown after dying or unsummoning, both reduced by higher Geomancy skill. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **inferno, ossuary and pulma dungeon redesign**: Inferno Dungeon is being redesigned by splitting part of Level 2 into a new Level 3 and adding a new Level 4, while Ossuary and Pulma Dungeons will each gain a new Level 4. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **iron golem secondary skill**: The Iron Golem's Secondary Skill is the higher of Mining or Blacksmithing; it focuses on powerful melee hits and extreme resilience to physical damage. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **line of sight targeting**: Targets requiring line of sight do not execute if aimed at a ground spot that would give a 'target cannot be seen' result, so the target is not lost. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **melee animal form weapon requirement**: All Melee Animal Forms require an equipped Wrestling Weapon (such as Fistblade, Cestus, or Martial Manual) to attack, and players keep weapon material and magical bonuses, weapon specials, abilities, and Wrestling Codex Stances and Finishers; players may instead fight Unarmed but lose the Wrestling Weapon mechanics (Codexes, Weapon Specials, Weapon Abilities). _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **paragon spawn tiers**: Paragon spawns are now Lesser Paragon, Paragon, or Greater Paragon, with Stat/Skill increases scaled by tier and the loot chest automatically matching the creature's tier (a Greater Paragon always has a Greater Paragon Chest). _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **paragon tier system**: The expansion adds a Paragon Tier System with Lesser, Regular, and Greater Paragons, each with matching chests and rewards. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-01–2025-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32437/)
- **scumbug animal form**: Scumbug is a Melee Animal Form focused on Heavy Defense, Damage over Time and Mobility; it can inflict disease, use a dig ability to teleport and deal area damage, and gains damage resistance when fighting multiple targets. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **shadowspire cathedral redesign**: Shadowspire Cathedral is being redesigned with new Side Rooms and Boss Rooms, expanded Levels 1 and 3, and a new Level 4. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **slamphibian animal form**: Slamphibian is a well-rounded Melee Animal Form excelling at survival and aggro management, with abilities to summon frog followers, inflict poison, and shed Poison, Disease and Bleed. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **spell chain buffs and effects**: Potential Spell Chain Buffs/Effects include caster Damage Resistance, Health Restored, Mana Restored, Arcane Buildup, Damage Shield, Reduced Casting Delay, and target effects such as Hex, Chill, Burn, Bleed, Disease, Poison, Knockback, Entangle and Hinder, plus Bonus Damage. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **spell chain targeting**: Spells do not need to be cast against the same target to progress a Spell Chain; the player only needs to follow the chain order with offensive spells. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **spell purger animal form**: Spell Purger is a Spellcasting Animal Form focused on burst attacks and enhancing spells, with abilities to restore mana, unleash burst attacks, and increase spellcasting range. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **spellcasting in animal form**: Players cannot cast spells in Animal Form unless it is a Spellcasting Form such as Spell Purger, in which they can cast spells as normal and make ranged attacks with an Arcane Staff if they have the required skills, with Arcane Staff and Magic Spellbook bonuses and mechanics like Wizard's Grimoires and Codexes still applying. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **target range limit**: Targets with a range limit can display the range as a ring around the player and do not execute if the cursor is aimed at an out-of-range tile. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **tidal tomb dungeon**: Tidal Tomb is a new 3-level dungeon with boss and mini-boss encounters, new creature models, unique aquatic rares, underwater mechanics on its lower levels, a new dungeon hue, underwater plants, and redline trophies. _(official, conf 0.85, 2 authors, 2025-05-25–2025-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32437/)
- **tier 2 dark hues**: Tier 2 Dark hues (e.g., Dark Coffee 1467, Dark Rust 2807, Dark Crimson 1939, Dark Blood 1465, Dark Chocolate 1589) are obtained from a Very Rare Cloth monster drop. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **weekly challenger dungeon**: A weekly rotating Challenger Dungeon designation increases the designated dungeon's difficulty and its rewards. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-01–2025-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32437/)
- **wood golem secondary skill**: The Wood Golem's Secondary Skill is the higher of Carpentry or Lumberjacking; it focuses on incapacitating enemies, fighting multiple enemies, and regeneration. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **avoiding pks in dungeons**: To avoid PKs in dungeons, know your nearest exit gate and stay near it, run Tracking to spot hostiles, avoid dead ends, loop around approaching PKs, flee straight to the gate when threatened, and consider grouping with an anti-PK guild and using escape scripts. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2020-02-03–2026-07-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33375/)
- **chivalry in pvp**: Chivalry abilities are disabled in PvP on Outlands, and Chivalry and Necromancy only contribute to the PvP damage cap. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2022-01-26–2025-10-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32305/)
- **disarm requirements**: Disarm requires 80 Arms Lore and 80 skill in the currently equipped weapon (or 80 Wrestling when unarmed); it is toggled with the [disarm command and grants +25% damage and +25% accuracy for 10 seconds. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 8 authors, 2018-04-05–2020-01-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16201/)
- **evaluating intelligence spell damage**: Evaluating Intelligence increases the damage of a mage's spells and is the largest single damage increase for mages; the exact bonus is unclear, with claims ranging from about 25% to 50% against mobs. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 7 authors, 2018-04-05–2022-07-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25561/)
- **inscription scroll damage bonus**: Casting spells from scrolls (not reagents) grants an Inscription damage bonus of 25% * (Inscription/100) against creatures and 10% * (Inscription/100) against players, along with a chance not to consume the scroll. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2017-09-01–2019-05-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13395/)
- **outlands pvp systems**: Outlands has PKing, Guild War/Global War (a flag making participants orange to each other), and Factions (mostly PvP-focused, where factionmates at guild war appear orange to each other), but Global War currently gives no extra gains for dungeon farming and the older O/C flagging system was not widely adopted. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2018-08-18–2024-05-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29442/)
- **parrying versus spells**: Parrying can parry spells, reducing mob spell damage by 75% on a successful parry, and also gives reflect and taunt effects. Its codex can reduce magic, poison, bleed and disease damage and apply stun. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2018-06-28–2021-05-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21820/)
- **scheduled pvp events**: Outlands runs a rotation of scheduled PvP events each hour (Town Struggles, Corpse Creek, Stygian Rifts, Dungeon Flashpoints) with rewards, stat tracking and leaderboards and no loot taken, alongside Global Warfare, open-world PvP, factions and the Arena. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2019-11-06–2024-02-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28924/)
- **armor meditation penalty**: Instead of dex loss, Outlands armor imposes tiered meditation penalties (studded leather and up start at 20%), computed per piece as location penalty times material penalty and summed across worn armor; Mind Blast deals extra damage to armored targets, making heavy armor with a shield a poor PvP choice. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-08-06–2023-01-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26849/)
- **aspect effects in pvp**: Aspect bonuses and weapon procs (such as Fire Aspect's flamestrike) are disabled in PvP against players, though aspect still gives some protection against monsters. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2019-11-23–2025-04-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31655/)
- **backstab damage bonus and requirements**: Backstab on Outlands is a stealth attack that adds a damage bonus of +450% to +1000% depending on weapon speed, further scaled by Stealth skill divided by 100%, and requires Hiding, Stealth and 80 in the relevant weapon skill. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-11-19–2020-03-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16862/)
- **escaping pks**: To escape PKs, add Hiding, Tracking and Camping to the template and run along known escape routes; use Resist and a defensive weapon skill to fight back, and never farm more than you can afford to lose. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-11-11–2024-07-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29967/)
- **greater heal stats**: Greater Heal heals approximately 35 to 45 health (average 40), has a 1.25 second cast time and costs 11 mana. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-04-05–2023-02-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26968/)
- **hamstring requirements**: Hamstring is unlocked by having at least two skills at 80 or higher among Anatomy, Arms Lore, Forensic Evaluation, Tracking and Wrestling. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-04-05–2020-01-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16166/)
- **idoc house competition**: IDOC houses on Outlands are heavily contested by large or skilled guilds and PKs, who may track and kill even stealthed players, so solo or small-group players are unlikely to win one. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2020-09-21–2021-06-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22104/)
- **mind blast damage bonus vs armor**: Mind Blast deals bonus damage based on the target's Total Armor Meditation Penalty rather than armor rating: +25% of the target's Mana Rate Reduction, capped at +50%, so heavier armor takes more damage. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-12-14–2020-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16138/)
- **mind blast versus plate armor**: Mind Blast deals much more damage to targets wearing plate armor (reported as +50% for full plate), so PKs use it against plate wearers; Resisting Spells can reduce the effect. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-04-05–2023-09-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28267/)
- **paragon spawn chance**: A monster has roughly a 1 in 400 chance of spawning as a paragon, and the chance is increased for monsters that have a Monster Hunter Society job that week. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-11-13–2024-06-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29625/)
- **parry for dexxers**: Parry lets a dexxer block both melee attacks and spells, greatly improving survivability and enabling a tank build with Resisting Spells, and it also makes dexxers harder to fight in PvP; dropping Parry and Resist for Tracking and Camping gives a pure-damage build with more risk against casters. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-09-10–2026-07-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33359/)
- **parry in pvp versus pvm**: In PvP, Parry protects only against melee attacks and not spell damage, while in PvM it also reduces spell damage by about 33%; for melee PvM, both Parry and Resist are recommended. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2019-12-02–2024-10-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30426/)
- **red activity by dungeon depth**: Reds are most active on early dungeon levels (1 and 2), which every red must pass through, while deeper levels such as level 3 see far fewer PKs, partly because anti-PK groups kill reds before they get there. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2022-12-11–2026-07-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33319/)
- **trapped pouch**: Opening a Trapped Pouch deals 1 damage to the player and releases them from Paralyze (and reportedly stun), making it essential for PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-09-03–2019-02-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10074/)
- **arena tournaments**: Outlands has an arena system and holds regular tournaments, including 1v1 and 2v2 dueling tournaments reported on Saturdays. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-12-09–2021-04-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21606/)
- **attacking grey players**: Attacking a grey player carries no consequences for you, but you become grey to that player and they can attack you back with impunity. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-10-02–2019-04-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12833/)
- **backstab in pvp**: Backstab does not work in PvP on Outlands. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-26–2019-02-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9535/)
- **bandage application time**: Bandaging yourself on Outlands takes about 10-15 seconds, scaled by healer Dexterity, while bandaging another target takes about 5-7.5 seconds; players commonly cite roughly 10 seconds for self-heal in PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-11–2024-02-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28900/)
- **disarm cooldown**: After a successful Disarm the attacker must wait 30 seconds before another attempt, a missed attempt with Disarm toggled delays the next attempt by 15 seconds, and a disarmed target cannot be disarmed again for 30 seconds. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-04-05–2022-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26353/)
- **discordance effect**: Discordance debuffs a target by lowering its skills and other values, so it deals less damage and takes more, making it easier to kill. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-10-02–2021-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21271/)
- **earth aspect**: Earth Aspect is a melee-focused aspect: on armor it gives armor rating, physical resistance, melee damage mitigation, a chance to increase damage by 150% and reduced monster debuffs, and on the weapon it gives hit chance, tactics and a high-damage special chance, but it is weak defensively against non-physical damage. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2021-02-21–2021-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21271/)
- **escape ropes against pks**: Escape ropes are a useful way to flee from PKs, including for mages, but mages can also use ropes to escape, so knowing when to run versus engage matters. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-05–2026-04-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33004/)
- **escaping pks with recall**: To escape PKs in the wilderness, keep a blessed runebook and a quick-recall macro or script (a runetome works too), use Tracking to spot incoming PKs, and run away then recall; a PK can interrupt the recall. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2020-05-05–2026-04-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32995/)
- **escaping reds**: To escape red players, use potions, trapped pouches, recall, or run toward a gate while bandaging, ideally with resist on. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-02-15–2019-02-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9917/)
- **explosion potion tracking**: Explosion potions do not heatseek or home onto targets in PvP on Outlands; they require Telekinesis to stay on the opponent, and a thrown potion can hit both the thrower and a nearby target. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-07-17–2020-12-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19856/)
- **explosion potions**: Purple (explosion) potions are not heat seeking and are hard to land usefully, for example they cannot be thrown on the run to hit a moving target. They have a 5 second timer that keeps ticking in flight and must be thrown before 1 second remains. Damage is modified by 50% * (Alchemy/100). _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-08-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1703/)
- **forensic evaluation damage bonus**: Forensic Evaluation grants a damage bonus against a creature type that stacks and lasts 12 hours, and at 120 skill the bonus is reportedly 30%. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-01-15–2024-10-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30454/)
- **group hunting safety**: Hunting in a group or with a guild improves safety against PKs and makes a player a less desirable target. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-11-30–2026-01-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32729/)
- **hamstring cooldown**: After a successful Hamstring the attacker must wait 30 seconds before another attempt, a missed attempt with Hamstring toggled delays the next attempt by 15 seconds, and a hamstrung target cannot be hamstrung again for 30 seconds. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-04-05–2022-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26353/)
- **hamstring effect**: Hamstring reduces the target player's stamina to zero for 3 seconds, forcing them to walk, prevents stamina regeneration and use of Refresh Potions or food during that time, and restores the previous stamina value afterward. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-09-11–2019-06-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14028/)
- **hamstring effect**: A creature hit by Hamstring is Entangled for 6 seconds, unable to move but still able to melee, cast spells, and use abilities, while a player hit by it is reduced to 0 stamina for 3 seconds, forcing them to walk. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-04-05–2019-06-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14028/)
- **hamstring for dexxers**: Hamstring is a highly useful ability for dexxer builds, especially pure dexers in PvP, both for escaping PKs and for fighting. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-11–2021-03-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21174/)
- **hamstring with archery**: Hamstring does not work with Archery in Outlands, although during beta archery could use it like the other melee skills. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-03-01–2019-03-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10827/)
- **mace fighting stamina drain**: Mace Fighting increases chance to hit with mace weapons and adds extra stamina drain per hit, draining more than swords or fencing weapons. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-04-05–2019-06-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13857/)
- **mage teleport and rope combo**: Mages can combine Teleport with the adventurer's rope to close distance, which pure dexxers cannot do, and some players argue this makes the rope more effective for mages than for the dexxers it was meant to help. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2024-03-26–2025-08-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32158/)
- **magic resistance potions**: Greater magic resist potions help against spell-casting mobs in PvM, but they work only in PvM and last about 1 minute. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-13–2022-08-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25847/)
- **outlands pvp mechanics hybrid**: Outlands PvP is a loose hybrid of T2A and UOR mechanics, with T2A predominant. Pre-casting, instahit, tank mages and heal through poison exist, but instahit and heal through poison are modified and differ from era-accurate versions. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-01-16–2021-08-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22497/)
- **parrying skill**: Parrying improves defense while wearing a shield by absorbing hits so you take less damage, and a starting character with Parrying receives a free shield. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-07-19–2018-11-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3881/)
- **pet damage balance**: Pets on Outlands are heavily nerfed compared to other shards after balance changes that cut their formerly very high burst damage, so summoners now deal considerably more damage than pets and pets are of limited use against player killers. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-08-04–2019-09-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15127/)
- **pets in player versus player combat**: Most pets are slow and can be outrun on foot by PKs and thieves, so only a few are useful in PvP, though tamers on foot have become popular among PKs and are weak when mounted. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-08-23–2021-04-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21411/)
- **pk looting in dungeons**: Player killers, blue or red, will be encountered in dungeons at some point, and a PK who kills you can loot items from your corpse, including tools like pickaxes, lock picks or a skinning knife, plus armor, reagents and bandages, even a single armor piece to block your armor aspect on recovery. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-19–2024-07-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30064/)
- **player killer locations and prevalence**: Player killers make up about 1% or less of the population according to staff, and they mostly operate in dungeons, though they sometimes hunt in low-level PvM areas. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2021-04-12–2024-07-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30161/)
- **player killing and griefing**: Outlands is a open-PvP environment where players can be killed and looted by other players, including blue (non-criminal) griefers, and PKs ('reds') often clear players out of dungeons. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-01-12–2026-05-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33072/)
- **pre-casting**: Pre-casting, where a mage casts a spell ahead of time and holds it until a target appears, is enabled on Outlands and is a core part of gameplay, often causing large mana dumps in big group battles. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-02-21–2026-04-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33004/)
- **pvp events**: Outlands offers group-oriented PvP events hourly, along with dungeons and factions, and players lose nothing in PvP events, so they are a safe way to learn PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2021-04-12–2023-02-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26959/)
- **pvp melee hit chance**: Weapon hit chance in PvP and dueling is about 50% baseline, capped at roughly 55%. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2021-02-24–2023-07-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28006/)
- **pvp skill scaling and balance**: Outlands PvP uses linear skill scaling and is intended as build versus build and skill versus skill, with Aspects, Mastery Chains and 120-skill-point skills largely kept out of PvP so players are on similar footing. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-02-28–2024-07-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30070/)
- **pvp templates and classes**: In Outlands PvP, tank mages (weapons plus magic) are the most optimal build and deal the biggest burst damage, but staff balancing and strong defensive templates give pure dexxers and pure mages counterplay. Most mage templates work in groups, most dexxers work solo, and outside of mages most classes are at a significant disadvantage. No single mage template is good for both PvM and PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-01-16–2026-03-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32943/)
- **red guilds farming dungeons**: Guilds of reds hold dungeon farming spots by clearing out other players, and blues can organize to stop them. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-11–2023-06-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/27487/)
- **sanctuary dungeon reduced rewards**: Sanctuary dungeons on Outlands are PvP-safe but reduce rewards, with loot (including gold) cut roughly in half and skill gains reduced compared to full-PvP dungeons. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2024-07-06–2025-07-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31969/)
- **stealth archer healing**: Stealth archers mostly heal with potions, and many have no Healing skill, though some train 60-80 Healing or Magery for sustain. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-02-21–2019-09-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14942/)
- **stealther builds**: Stealthers are effective in group PKing because Tracking stops targets from bailing out, but solo they are mainly good for ganking low-health players; Tracking and Hiding also help non-PvP players avoid PKs. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-08-12–2024-05-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29414/)
- **stun mechanic**: Stun was tested on Outlands but judged unsuited to the era and not kept, so the stun punch does not work there. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-09-01–2020-03-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16771/)
- **tamer pet combinations**: Commonly used tamer pet combinations include Arboreal Wisp with Eldritch Drake, Smoke Drake or Reef Serpent; Aegis Scorpion with 4 imps or 2 blood hounds; water drake with fire crawler; and 2 Smoke Drakes, 2 Ember Drakes or Smoke Drake with Dragon at 120 taming. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-01-06–2020-08-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18570/)
- **tamer solo pve strength**: Tamers are a dominant PvE class in Outlands, able to act as healer, tank and damage dealer solo and to clear dungeons, bosses, shrines and the high seas. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-11-05–2020-07-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18494/)
- **taste identification poison in pvp**: Taste Identification boosts player-applied poison damage and is effective in PvM; in PvP it was countered by cure potions, but a January 2019 patch made high-level poison, especially Taste ID-boosted, harder to cure and Taste ID poisoners more effective. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-10–2019-01-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8177/)
- **tools for surviving player killers**: Players survive PK encounters using escape tools such as rope, tracking, hiding, trapped pouches, potions, wrestling, resist and recall macros, and by running. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2022-12-10–2026-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32743/)
- **town struggle event**: Town Struggle is a recurring team PvP event on Outlands, held multiple times per night and open to most players, alongside other PvP event formats such as Dungeon Flashpoints. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2022-01-31–2025-01-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31022/)
- **weekly dungeon bonuses**: Each week certain dungeons carry bonuses such as extra gold (for example 15%) or special item bonuses, and the featured dungeon changes weekly. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-02-07–2022-11-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26422/)
- **wrestling versus weapon hit chance**: Wrestling works like other combat skills: at grandmaster Wrestling versus a grandmaster weapon skill, each melee strike has about a 50% chance to hit. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-04-05–2019-03-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12024/)
- **1v1 kill difficulty**: It is very difficult to kill a competent player 1v1 in Outlands; a kill generally requires several consecutive full-damage hits. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-19–2019-03-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11743/)
- **agility potion swing speed**: Drinking an agility potion increases actual swing speed even though the swing speed shown in Arms Lore does not change. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-19–2020-11-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19537/)
- **archer stealth backstab**: Archers can hide or stealth, wear shadow aspect gear, and backstab with bows, leaving stealth to deal damage and then re-hiding to escape. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-09-16–2019-10-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15346/)
- **arms lore special hit chance**: Arms Lore increases the player's special hit chance, and combined with Swordsmanship and a lucky streak a katana or scimitar can reach up to about 45% special effect chance. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-26–2019-09-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14888/)
- **avoiding pk deaths while farming**: To reduce PK deaths, avoid farming in corners or narrow corridors and choose spots with an easier escape, such as those closer to gates. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-10-18–2024-10-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30470/)
- **backstab attack bonus scope**: Backstab only provides its attack bonus against monsters (PvM) and gives no attack bonus in PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-03-21–2020-03-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16927/)
- **backstab damage bonus**: Backstab grants a large damage bonus of roughly 450% to 1000%, depending on weapon speed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-08–2019-10-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15346/)
- **bard group support**: In group PvM a bard can use Discordance, Provocation and Peacemaking on creatures to keep them provoked or peaced and make them easier for the group to kill. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-08-04–2023-05-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/27253/)
- **bard peacemaking**: Peacemaking makes a monster stand still without retaliating and can be used on any number of mobs with about a 10 second pause between uses, letting a bard spend roughly 36 seconds peacing three monsters before engaging. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-03-04–2021-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21271/)
- **barding breaks**: Barding breaks exist to prevent full immobilization of mobs, but they occur only some of the time, weaken the mob, and force additional Barding attempts. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-10-17–2020-10-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19290/)
- **black widow pets**: Black widows are stealth poisoner pets that inflict Deadly Poison: Black Widow needs 100 Taming and 2 slots, Giant needs 110 Taming and 3 slots, Colossal needs 120 Taming and 4 slots; the Black Widow is among the highest dps-per-control-slot utility pets. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-01–2026-04-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32999/)
- **bless spell**: Bless increases strength, dexterity and intelligence by 11 points with GM Magery. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **blood worm pet**: The blood worm is among the strongest and most popular pets: it does good damage, heals itself, uses a damaging bleed attack, is fast, takes 2 pet slots, and requires 120 taming skill. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-10-07–2020-02-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16651/)
- **blue player grey baiting**: Some players who stay blue use game mechanics, such as stone wall, precast explosion, pulling mobs, standing close or hiding nearby, to trick other blues into turning grey so they can be killed without repercussions. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-13–2018-12-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7204/)
- **caster facing direction**: A caster's character faces the player it is casting on, so experienced players can predict who a synch dump will land on, and groups can fake this by facing a different player when casting. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **chivalry in pvp**: Chivalry gives instant heals, including on others who are not adjacent, which would break PvP balance and make dexxers very hard to kill, so it is not usable in PvP the way it is on other shards. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-07-04–2025-10-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32307/)
- **colossal slug damage**: Colossal Slugs have a large hitpoint pool and deal ranged spit damage of about 3 hits of 10-15 each, with a special that lowers target armor, making them stronger than Giant Slugs, which deal about 2 hits of 5. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-15–2019-04-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12668/)
- **command aspect**: Command Aspect reduces damage to pets from all sources and increases pet damage, taming chance and pet experience; its proc heals pets and greatly increases damage for a short while. It works as well as Water Aspect for farming, but gives the user no buffs beyond those every aspect gives. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-01-18–2021-06-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22251/)
- **countering off-screening**: To counter off-screening, everyone pre-casts Explosion except one player who pre-loads Paralyze. That player times the Paralyze with the Explosions, and everyone follows with Energy Bolt while the target is held in place and cannot pop pouches. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **curing poison**: Cure potions are the recommended way to cure poison, since bandage curing is unreliable even with Anatomy and Anatomy is not required when using cure potions. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-05–2019-07-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14271/)
- **daemon summon usage**: Daemons have the highest DPS potential of all summons when run at low health (about 25-30%) to benefit from the damage bonuses that scale with lower life and longer lifetime, and they should be healed to stay near that range. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-11-08–2021-07-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22444/)
- **defensive skills against pks**: Resisting Spells, Wrestling (for mages) and Tracking help players survive PK encounters, while templates without them are more vulnerable and have less ability to fight back. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-08-24–2025-02-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31461/)
- **dexer healing methods**: Dexers can stay alive and heal without interruption using potions, mini heals and bandages, and Healing combined with Parrying is generally enough to sustain them. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-05-20–2022-01-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24224/)
- **dexers in field pvp**: Dexers are at a disadvantage in large open-field group PvP because they must close distance to hit and risk being spell dumped, so most groups prefer mages whose synchronized damage wins out. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-22–2022-01-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24301/)
- **dexxer interrupts of mage spells**: A dexxer using an interrupt weapon has roughly a 50-55% chance per swing to interrupt a mage's casting, which typically lands once or twice in a mage's 10 second window and lets a bandage through. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-24–2023-02-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26960/)
- **dexxer macer end-game template**: A suggested dexxer macer template is Musicianship, Peacemaking, Healing, Anatomy, Macefighting, Tactics and one of Arms Lore, Tracking, Camping or Forensics, with the second character possibly using Discordance instead of Peacemaking. It is considered viable for all content including end game. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-06-28–2020-03-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16837/)
- **dexxer macer required codexes**: A dexxer-macer build typically needs the weaponskill (Macing), Parrying and Healing codexes plus a Chivalry book. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-11-08–2024-11-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30706/)
- **dexxer magery utility**: Dexxers who take roughly 40-60 magery, using scrolls if needed even at low intelligence, gain mage-like utility such as teleport, magic reflect, healing, wall of stone and dispel. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-01-17–2024-02-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28900/)
- **dexxer versus mage balance**: In one-on-one PvP a skilled dexxer with 100 resist will almost always beat a skilled mage, but as group size increases the balance shifts in favor of mages. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-01-16–2025-07-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32071/)
- **disarm cooldown**: A player can be disarmed (and therefore hamstrung) at most once every 30 seconds. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-09-11–2019-07-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14278/)
- **disarm effect**: A disarmed creature takes 25% additional damage from all sources and has a 25% penalty to melee defense for 10 seconds. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-23–2019-07-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14278/)
- **disarm effects**: A disarmed player has any items in their hands dropped into their backpack; those items cannot be moved or stolen by anyone for 5 seconds, and the player cannot equip hand items for 5 seconds. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-11-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4944/)
- **disarm toggle**: Players toggle Disarm attacks with the [Disarm command or a Razor/Steam hotkey; while toggled, the next successful hit disarms the opponent. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-11-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4944/)
- **disarm weapon stealing**: Stealing an equipped weapon via disarm is impractical: disarm requires attacking, so it is effectively impossible in town unless the players are at war, and it has only a 50% hit chance and can still fail. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-09-22–2019-11-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15650/)
- **discordance bonus pet damage**: Discordance combined with Peacemaking or Provocation on a target gives pets bonus damage (about 50% extra) and makes them hit more often. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-10-23–2021-06-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22251/)
- **dispel field spell**: Dispel Field removes walls of stone and summoned gates. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **dual wielding**: Dual wielding two weapons at once, such as two daggers, is not possible on Ultima Online: Outlands. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-31–2019-01-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9146/)
- **dungeon exit gates and red looters**: Reds often loot in dungeons and leave quickly through exit gates, and players cannot recall home to stash partial loot, so fleeing from deep in a dungeon often means passing the killers because exit gates are few. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-12–2026-06-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33279/)
- **eldritch aspect**: The Eldritch aspect emphasizes spell damage, strengthens spells with each cast, allows defending with magical energy instead of Wrestling, and its channeling creates mana wells that lend mana, sustaining Dragon stance and giving very high damage with fire armour. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-07-13–2025-01-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31013/)
- **escaping pks near moongates**: To reduce PK risk, farm near dungeon moongates with an escape macro ready, learn layouts, favor less-traveled areas, and keep Tracking running; alternatively farm overland using marked runes near city moongates with a recall macro ready. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-08-15–2025-05-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31825/)
- **fire elemental role**: Fire Elementals are the magic damage summon and are great DPS when upgraded for maximum DPS and Ranged. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-11-08–2021-07-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22444/)
- **fishing spear damage bonus**: Fishing skill provides a damage bonus when using spears (long spear and short spear), reported as 25% for PvM and 10% for PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-07-19–2018-11-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5494/)
- **focus fire against cross healing**: Against groups that cross heal, you will not kill anyone unless you focus damage on one target, ideally with a caller and synchronised spells, because scattered non-burst damage is easily healed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **gear importance in pvp**: Gear has very little impact on PvP in Outlands, which is mostly build versus build and skill versus skill, so expensive PvM gear is not required for PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-10-12–2026-01-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32705/)
- **grandmaster wrestling defense**: A GM wrestler avoids (parries) about 50% of attacks from other GM weapon-skill users, and this defense is enough to fight other players if they play defensively; whether it applies to ranged weapons is unclear. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-10–2019-07-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14395/)
- **greater strength potion and cross healing**: Staying under a Greater Strength potion raises your health pool, making it easier to be cross healed out of a synch dump. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **harm spell**: Harm deals damage that depends on the distance from the target and is a strong interrupt spell. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **heal potion cooldown**: Heal potions have a 10 second cooldown, and the system message 'some damage has been healed' now works reliably for setting up a cooldown timer, so potions should be drunk on cooldown in PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-11-01–2024-10-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30411/)
- **healing at 80 skill**: At 80 Healing, a bandage heals about 32-48 health (cost 2 gp), and with a greater heal potion (20-40 hp, 18 gp) this gives roughly 52-88 health every 10 seconds, so raising Healing higher is not essential. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-22–2019-06-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13857/)
- **healing while moving in pvp**: Bandages and health potions can be used while moving in PvP, so both fleeing and chasing players can heal on the move. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-08-23–2026-03-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32965/)
- **herding and summon damage**: Herding boosts the damage of Summon Creature and 8th circle summons, but it does not work on Blade Spirits or Energy Vortex. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-21–2019-04-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12253/)
- **herding pet damage bonus**: At 100 Herding, pets deal about 25% more damage against the herded target, and Fencing indirectly helps pet damage by making pets hit more often. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-07-26–2021-06-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22251/)
- **hidden archer forced retaliation**: A hidden crossbow or archer character that takes damage from a nearby mob, such as area-of-effect damage, is forced to attack back at that mob, revealing the character. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-03-25–2024-03-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29095/)
- **hinder mechanic**: Hinder is a random chance proc from archery attacks with no cooldown, it stacks, and its chance reportedly increases with more Arms Lore. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-28–2019-03-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10853/)
- **husk crab pet**: The Husk Crab is a 1-slot pet tamed at 90 skill with high armor, suited to low-key tanking. It gains 1% accuracy per nearby ally, so a pack of 5 works well, and it procs a stacking armor reduction attack. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-05-19–2020-02-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16651/)
- **husk crab tamer build**: At around 90 Taming and Animal Lore, a team of 5 Husk Crabs offers some of the best DPS per skill point spent and is strong in middle-to-late dungeon levels. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-15–2020-08-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18570/)
- **invisibility spell**: Invisibility hides the caster and lasts only a few minutes. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **kiting larger groups**: A smaller, more mobile group can kite or run from a larger, poorly mobile group, stringing it out as members break ahead of the pack so they can be killed outside cross-heal range. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **last target from casting**: Casting a spell on a player or on their hp bar automatically sets them as your last target, and a select-last-target hotkey brings up a cursor to set it without casting a spell. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **mace damage versus other weapons**: Maces deal significantly more damage than swords in PvM and other non-PvP situations, though this advantage does not clearly apply in PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-14–2021-05-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21921/)
- **mace fighting advantages**: Mace Fighting is preferred over swords or fencing because its special reduces target armor in PvM so damage stacks and drains stamina in PvP, and mace damage is considerably better, with Fencing mainly worthwhile for poisoned weapons. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-05–2019-05-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13421/)
- **mage pre-casting**: Mages can pre-cast spells without a target and release them later on a target, so long-cast spells like Paralyze or Flamestrike can be used with the cast not interrupted, giving an advantage in PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-02-01–2025-04-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31692/)
- **magic arrow**: Magic Arrow is the lowest-damage nuke but the fastest-casting damage spell at about 0.5 seconds, dealing roughly 3-7 damage with 10% SDI, and is useful for targeting and breaking magic reflect. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-07-19–2022-08-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25847/)
- **magic trap pouches**: A Mage normally casts Magic Trap on a pouch to make a trapped pouch; opening it before a fight deals 1 damage to yourself and breaks paralyze. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-07-19–2019-02-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10065/)
- **melee template**: A common melee template is Healing, Anatomy, Tactics, Magic Resist, Parrying and a weapon skill (Swords, Macing or Fencing) at 100, optionally adding Poisoning with Swords/Fencing or Arms Lore for disarm and hamstring; macers can use a war mace with a shield or a gnarled or black staff. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-06–2019-01-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8685/)
- **mind blast damage**: Mind Blast can deal roughly 27 to 45 damage against heavily armored targets wearing plate and a shield when the 20% extra damage skills are maxed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-25–2019-03-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10818/)
- **mind blast damage**: Mind Blast gains bonus damage equal to 25% of the target's Mana Rate Reduction, capped at +50% damage, and is a cheap spell that deals roughly 30 HP of damage. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-14–2024-03-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29111/)
