- **mini heal versus greater heal**: Greater heal is more mana-efficient and heals more HP faster, but mini heal cannot be disrupted; mini heal suits opponents wasting mana on small spells, greater heal suits opponents spamming big spells like energy bolt. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-02-24–2022-01-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24151/)
- **mob spellcasting line of sight**: Mobs on Outlands do not insta-cast; they have a casting time like players, but after the initial line-of-sight check to start the cast they do not recheck it, so a spell such as Flamestrike can still land after you move out of sight or behind a wall, even from long range. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-25–2019-01-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8268/)
- **modified spellbooks in pvp**: Modified spellbooks (might, force, power) do not apply their increased spell damage in PvP on Outlands; the old timed-equip bonus was removed and replaced by skill-based spell damage bonuses. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2019-11-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15520/)
- **monster loot tables**: Any monster on Outlands can reportedly drop any item, with only the drop chance differing, and bosses drop the same items as regular mobs. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-09–2022-08-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25830/)
- **non-combat skills and pet damage**: Tracking, Forensic Evaluation, Camping and similar skills do not increase pet damage. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-27–2020-08-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18570/)
- **nox tic energy bolt dump**: Nox tic energy bolt dump: cast Poison and immediately load Magic Arrow, disrupt the opponent's cure or greater heal with a weapon hit, drop Magic Arrow on their next cast, drop Harm on the next, then cast and drop Energy Bolt, which lands because poison damages about 5 seconds after the initial cast; a shorter Poison then Energy Bolt version works against opponents who habitually cure, best when they are at 30-40% HP. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-02-24–2022-01-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24151/)
- **outlands pvp ruleset**: Outlands PvP is based on T2A rules at its core, with some UOR elements and minor changes, in a T2A-inspired era where tank mages are dominant. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-01-16–2021-09-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22889/)
- **overland hunting safety from pks**: Hunting in the overland is generally safer from PKs than in dungeons because it offers multiple escape routes and a larger area for PKs to cover. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-05-05–2020-05-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17446/)
- **paralyze spell**: Paralyze deals no damage and freezes the target; the effect breaks if the target takes damage, and trapped pouches counter it. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **parry and magic resist in pvm versus pvp**: In PvM, Parrying lets players taunt enemies onto them to increase shield AR and parry spells, and Magic Resist lets spells be parried, storing the damage to add to the next hit besides reducing spell damage by a percentage; in PvP these abilities are unavailable and only the percentage spell damage reduction from Magic Resist applies. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-10-21–2020-12-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19856/)
- **parry mages in rifts**: In Rifts, mage comps running parry can play offensively, nuking each mob quickly and kiting until the next spawns, because less mana goes to healing and running; this was strong against dexxer comps. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-14–2022-01-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24107/)
- **parry skill chances**: At 100 (GM) Parry there is a 50% chance to parry melee attacks, a 25% chance to parry spells, and 50% reduced stamina loss from damage; at 80 skill spell parry chance is still about 20%. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-12-05–2024-10-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30409/)
- **parry spell absorption**: A character with Parry who is dexxing can absorb spells and return the absorbed spell damage, up to 250 stored, back onto mobs, letting tanks absorb large amounts of spell damage from daemons and other mobs. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-18–2018-12-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7286/)
- **peacemaking for hunting**: Peacemaking is slower but safer than Discordance for hunting, and it can remove much of the risk of mob hunting. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-09–2019-10-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15348/)
- **pet damage balance**: Pets are now fairly balanced and deal damage roughly on par with dexxers for fewer skill points, whereas mage pets were weaker than physical pets before the balance patches. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-11-13–2022-11-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26424/)
- **ping requirements for mage pvp**: Mages in PvP should have a ping of about 90 ms or less and a decent computer; at 150-200 ms, mage PvP is nearly impossible. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-11–2026-01-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32699/)
- **pk-avoiding builds**: Non-traditional builds such as a stealth backstabber or dungeon picker are safer against player killers, and a backstabber build is a way to avoid being killed by PKs. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-01-31–2025-03-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31499/)
- **pre-casting spells**: You can cast a spell and hold it on your cursor to use later, for example pre-casting Greater Heal while meleeing so it cannot be interrupted when you drop it on yourself. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **provocation effect**: Provocation, if successful, makes two targets attack each other, and the two mobs involved deal halved damage, so they struggle to kill each other quickly. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-02–2019-01-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8761/)
- **punching while casting**: Punches (about 50% hit chance, 1 damage) can be thrown while casting a spell, so you can punch an opponent's harm cast to disrupt it and finish your own greater heal; stay next to the opponent you are targeting. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-02-24–2022-01-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24151/)
- **pvm bonuses in pvp**: PvM damage bonuses are uncapped, but in PvP bonuses that directly affect PvP are disabled only against players while those with indirect effects are disabled entirely, and the specifics vary by context such as overworld or events, so only a narrow range of setups works well. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-10-05–2026-08-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33679/)
- **pvp balance design**: Outlands PvP is balanced around an effective HP formula and a 10-second bandage window, and PvP templates beat PvE templates, so players farming with PvE-tuned templates are vulnerable to red PvPers. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-07-04–2026-04-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32995/)
- **pvp flag effects on bonuses**: When a player becomes PvP flagged on Outlands, skills that are PvP-disabled deactivate, and codex and aspect bonuses deactivate immediately. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-08-21–2022-08-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25813/)
- **pvp mage builds**: Common PvP builds include the archer mage (best for group play), hally mage (magery plus halberd), tank mage (magery with parrying) and nox mage, plus melee builds such as macers, archer mace and poison fencing. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-08-12–2021-08-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22497/)
- **pvp spell damage cap**: Spell damage is capped in Outlands PvP at 20%, and some mage templates already reach that 20% Spell Damage Increase. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-25–2020-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16099/)
- **pvp survival basics for pvmers**: To survive PvP as a PvMer, use potions, adventure ropes and a trapped pouch (self-damage to start a bandage), since players who use these three tools are hard to kill. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-07-03–2023-07-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28006/)
- **pvp threat**: PvP is a constant threat on Outlands, since other players can kill you and take everything you carry. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-22–2025-03-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31579/)
- **pvp weapon choice swords**: Swords are generally the preferred PvP weapon over maces, partly because of the halberd. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-10–2020-04-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17371/)
- **quick dump**: For a quick dump, everyone clears their pre-casts and, on the caller's call, casts Energy Bolt and drops it on the same target, useful against an isolated lead chaser. A 'FULL' quick dump has everyone cast Explosion then Energy Bolt on the call with nothing pre-loaded. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **reactive armor**: Reactive Armor is a Magery spell that provides a buff that absorbs melee damage. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-07-19–2018-12-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6272/)
- **recommended dexer pvp stock**: A recommended dexer pvp stock is a Power or Vanquishing magic weapon, full exceptional dexer armor, 50 of each reagent, 10 healing, 10 refresh, 8 greater strength, 8 greater agility and 10 greater cure potions, 15 trapped boxes and 150 bandages. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **recommended mage pvp stock**: A recommended mage pvp stock is an exceptional halberd and katana, a full suit of exceptional leather armor, 75 of each reagent, 10 healing, 10 refresh and 10 greater strength potions, 5 greater agility and 5 greater cure potions, and 10 trapped boxes. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **reveal and dispel spells**: Reveal reveals hidden players, and Dispel removes summoned creatures such as energy vortexes or daemons. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **scribe mage template**: The scribe mage with Tracking (Inscription plus Tracking, giving 20% SDI and hamstring) is the most common and strongest template for group PvP on Outlands. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-07-29–2020-01-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16166/)
- **small group attendance at pvp events**: Solo players and small groups generally do not attend Dungeon Flashpoint or Corpse Creek Capture events unless they have a group of roughly 5 to 10 or more, because small groups cannot ally together. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-25–2022-02-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24464/)
- **snowdrift pets**: Snowdrifts take one pet slot and have a breath special attack in addition to melee, with a reduced chance of using that special attack in PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-06–2019-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8216/)
- **soldier template equipment**: The Soldier template is suggested to use a Viking Sword, Broadsword or Longsword, a Metal Kite Shield or Heater Shield, and Chainmail or Platemail; one Full Chain, Viking sword, Kite Shield setup earned about 3834 gold per hour in Nusero Level 2 but struggled against spellcasters. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-09-01–2018-01-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/807/)
- **spell damage delays**: Spell damage delays on Outlands are: Harm 0.05s, Lightning 0.25s, Magic Arrow, Fireball, Mind Blast, Energy Bolt, Flame Strike, Chain Lightning, Meteor Swarm and Earthquake 0.5s, Explosion 2.5s; delayed spells cannot be reliably used for delayed disrupts, whereas instant ones like poison, harm and lightning can. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2022-01-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24151/)
- **spell disruption by circle**: On Outlands, spells of circle 4 and higher (lightning, mind blast, energy bolt, explosion, etc.) always disrupt an opponent's spell cast, while circles 1-3 do not always disrupt. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-02-24–2022-01-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24151/)
- **spell interrupt mechanics**: Spell interruption on Outlands is not 100% on all circles: circle 1-3 spells give a limited number of heal interrupts, repeat Harm casts within 5 seconds reportedly interrupt only 50% of the time, and Poison shares the interrupt cooldown with Fireball and can be resisted. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-03–2020-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16121/)
- **spell interrupts**: Damage spells or melee hits landed on a caster mid-cast disrupt the spell. Harm, Magic Arrow, Fireball, Poison and Lightning are common interrupt spells because lower-circle spells cast faster than higher-circle ones such as Energy Bolt or Greater Heal. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **spirit speak for mages**: Spirit Speaking lets a mage summon thralls to tank and deal damage, and also makes the thralls and the mage's spells somewhat stronger; Poison adds damage over time. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-15–2022-07-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25561/)
- **spirit stone filling speed**: With a summoner build, kills fill Spirit Stones quickly: a single daemon kill fills about 60% of a stone, and a stone fills in a couple of kills. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-01-12–2018-11-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5701/)
- **stealth above 100 skill**: Having 120 Stealth instead of 100 gives about 20% more Backstab damage, since it scales with Stealth skill / 100, and allows a few more steps while stealthed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-19–2019-02-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10479/)
- **stealth archer playstyle**: Stealth archers can attack from any distance and use stealth to avoid a large share of dangerous situations, making the build comparatively low-effort and safe. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-09-03–2021-01-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20287/)
- **stealth backstab armor**: Stealth backstab templates commonly use shadow aspect armor, and a hide/stealth backstab template in shadow aspect armor is reported to be able to take down top-tier mobs. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-10–2024-01-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28737/)
- **stealth pet behavior**: Stealth-capable tamed pets such as jaguars hide on their own, stay stealthed most of the time (about 80% by one report), and follow their owner while stealthed even when the owner is not stealthed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-02–2019-03-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11013/)
- **stealth tamer trapdoor spider build**: Some players build stealth PK tamers with about 75 taming and lore using Trapdoor Spiders, which hinder, backstab and poison; the spiders are very squishy but useful by surprise, mostly in group situations, and may have been nerfed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-01–2020-08-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18570/)
- **summoned earth elementals**: Summoned Earth Elementals perform well and take more abuse than summoned daemons, especially with the Spirit Speak buffs from the Eldritch aspect; daemons do not hold up as well unless Spirit Speak is 120. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-08–2019-04-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13107/)
- **summoner aspects**: Void, Eldritch and Fortune are A-tier summoner aspects that work alone as both book and armor, Blood and Fire are B-tier book aspects, and the spell-based summoner build uses Eldritch on book and armor, with Death, Void or Blood armor as alternatives. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-11-08–2022-09-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25937/)
- **summoner tank summons**: Earth Elemental and Water Elemental are the recommended tank summons for new summoners; both are situational, and Water Elemental is preferred as the magic tank, and a grimoire upgrade increases their aggro. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-11-08–2021-07-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22444/)
- **summoner vulnerability and damage output**: Summoners are very vulnerable to melee and spell damage when they have no summoned followers, and high-level creatures can deal massive damage to summons via Auto-Dispel. Unlike a tamer, the summoner is expected to do more damage than the summons. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-09-01–2020-11-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19478/)
- **summoner's tome and wizard's grimoire**: The Wizard Grimoire is used for wizard spells, the Summoners Tome gives summons special abilities, and the Codex is used for combat skills such as mace. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-03-28–2024-05-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29446/)
- **surviving mage burst damage**: Cure and heal potions are important for surviving mage burst damage and poison, and players should also keep resistances at 80 or higher. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-17–2026-04-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33009/)
- **synch dump**: For a synch dump, a caller on voice chat has mages pre-cast Explosion, then on the call (e.g. '2 1 drop') everyone drops Explosion at once, followed by Energy Bolt on the same target, to burst through cross healing. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **tactics damage bonus**: Tactics increases wrestling and weapon damage by 50% * (Tactics skill / 100), so GM Tactics gives a 50% bonus. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-11-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4664/)
- **tamer pet damage**: Tamer pets have a narrow, consistent damage range (reportedly about 1:1.25 min to max) and a per-second damage cap in PvP that prevents burst damage. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-12-06–2022-09-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26014/)
- **tamer pet damage**: Tamers rely on pets to deal damage and absorb hits, so they need less personal survivability. Higher level pets do more damage, but the damage is a steady flow with no notable high-end hits. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-09-16–2022-07-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25639/)
- **tamer survivability against pks**: Tamers with defensive skills such as Resist and Wrestling can often survive or beat two PKs at once while PvMing, whereas tamers without those skills are floored by two PKs and possibly by one. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-23–2019-11-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15656/)
- **tamers in pvm**: Tamers are very strong at PvM, and many players farming level 3 dungeons are tamers. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-08-28–2019-08-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14712/)
- **tank mage template**: The standard tank mage template is 100 Str, 100 Int and 25 Dex, with 100 Magery, Evaluating Intelligence, Resisting Spells, Meditation, Wrestling and Tactics plus one weapon skill (such as Swordsmanship, Macing, Fencing or Archery). _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-07-19–2019-02-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10589/)
- **target closest restriction**: On Outlands, the 'target closest' option is disabled for humans and humanoids (to prevent sync dumps and for PvP reasons), so players must cycle targets, while it still works for most non-humanoid mobs. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-27–2019-10-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15307/)
- **thieves stealing farming tools**: Thieves on Outlands can steal essential farming tools such as instruments, shepherd's crooks, reagents and bandages, leaving the victim unable to continue. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-07-09–2025-02-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31399/)
- **town struggle pvp practice**: Town Struggle offers team PVP with numbers as even as possible, usually with a caller directing the team, and helps players learn PvP skills to prepare against reds. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-19–2024-10-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30462/)
- **town struggle zerging**: In Town Struggles a large guild or alliance can hold the point by standing on it and resurrecting teammates, and this is problematic mainly when it leaves new players and non-voice players stacked together on one team. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-24–2022-04-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24901/)
- **tracking and stealth versus reds**: Players commonly use tracking to detect reds, and reds can use tracking and reveal to find and kill hidden stealthers, so stealth is not a guarantee of safety. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-04–2019-05-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13276/)
- **tracking damage bonus**: Tracking lets you find monsters off your screen, and tracking the mob you are fighting gives a damage bonus. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-01-15–2018-10-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2753/)
- **tracking skill for pk avoidance**: To avoid PKs while farming, keep the Tracking skill active (hunting mode set to the closest or red players) and run or recall as soon as the arrow starts blinking. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-01-15–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31813/)
- **trapdoor spider pets**: Trapdoor spiders are stealth poisoner pets that inflict Greater Poison: Trapdoor Spider needs 75 Taming and 1 slot, Giant needs 95 Taming and 2 slots, Colossal needs 120 Taming and 4 slots. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-01–2019-03-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10839/)
- **triggering backstab via stealth**: To trigger Backstab, hide and move at least one step so stealth is triggered (or activate stealth manually), then attack your target. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-03-15–2020-03-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16866/)
- **void armor aspect**: Void armor aspect provides mana sustain, some healing, and defensive and Parry/Resist benefits, and is considered mandatory for Time Dungeon runs. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-09-05–2023-09-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28266/)
- **wall of stone spell**: Wall of Stone creates a 3 tile long wall of stone that can block runes or help the caster escape. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **water aspect armor bonuses**: Water aspect armor gives an additional healing percentage and a higher chance to trigger special attacks (helpful for mace users), which helps keep the player and pets alive and compensates for lower Anatomy. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-04–2020-04-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17204/)
- **weapon skill difference effect**: Having lower weapon skill than your opponent reduces your chance to hit and increases the opponent's chance to hit you. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-05–2018-12-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6255/)
- **weapon special moves**: Outlands does not use production-server weapon special moves; the mace special procs randomly, and some weapon specials work only in PvM and not in PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-06-09–2021-12-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/23829/)
- **wizard's grimoire flame strike upgrade**: The Wizard's Grimoire Flame Strike upgrade adds a damage-over-time effect worth an extra 35% damage, which players consider better than flat damage bonuses even if some damage is wasted while farming. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-11-08–2022-09-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25937/)
- **wrestling mechanics**: Wrestling gives a chance to parry or avoid enemy melee attacks using the default weapon skill hit/miss formula, and wrestling attacks inflict 2 to 8 damage. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-10–2019-07-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14398/)
- **wrestling special attack**: Wrestling attacks have about a 10% chance to trigger a Wrestling Weapon Special Attack against creatures, which increases damage by 400%. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-07-26–2020-04-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17165/)
- **wrestling spell interruption**: Mages use Wrestling because its fast punch can interrupt an opponent's casting and strike a melee attacker first, delaying their hit so the mage can finish casting. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-08-18–2018-08-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2018/)
- **wrestling usefulness as a weapon skill**: Wrestling is a weak damage skill in both PvM and PvP; its fist damage is low and it mostly offers interrupt capability. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-04-08–2023-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/27145/)
- **wyvern pet**: The Wyvern (105 taming, 3 slots) has lethal poison, high accuracy and okay DPS, making it a decent beast sting pet, though it is not as good as black widows and uses one slot too many. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-05-19–2026-04-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32999/)
- **zen strike**: Zen Strike inflicts 350% damage to the target and restores 25 health, 25 stamina and 25 mana, and the health recovery enables a very aggressive playstyle. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-03-28–2023-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/27146/)
- **accuracy property**: The Accuracy property on an item affects your chance to hit a monster, compared against the monster's wrestling skill. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 3 authors, 2022-02-08–2022-02-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24421/)
- **animal lore wrestling values for demons**: After a patch, Animal Lore reportedly showed Ancient demons with 125 Wrestling, with Elder demons showing lower values than Ancient. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-27–2018-11-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5287/)
- **armor vs special ranged elemental attacks**: Armor is reportedly said to reduce damage from special ranged attacks of higher-tier elementals and demons, such as water pellets and demon aura damage. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-04-01–2019-04-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12082/)
- **buckler meditation penalty**: A buckler, including a valorite buckler, has no meditation penalty, so it does not reduce mana regeneration or increase Mind Blast damage taken. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-14–2018-12-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7102/)
- **chivalry spells in pvp**: Chivalry spells can be activated in PvP but with a reduced effect: less healing, less swing speed and less damage. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-10-04–2024-10-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30409/)
- **community bonus board dungeons**: Dungeons listed on the community bonus board are heavily crowded and are a primary focus of PK groups, so red players are more likely to be found there. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-02-28–2019-02-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10766/)
- **defense property**: The Defense property on an item affects your chance to be hit by a monster, compared against the monster's wrestling skill. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 3 authors, 2022-02-08–2022-02-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24421/)
- **dexxer halberd damage**: A full dexxer damage build hits for about 50 damage with a halberd, though hits against low-armored opponents rarely exceed about 30. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2023-01-22–2023-01-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26876/)
- **dexxer weapon abilities**: Outlands has hamstring and disarm as active weapon abilities with cooldowns that are available to dexxers. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2023-01-13–2023-01-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26827/)
- **dungeon flashpoint layout**: Dungeon Flashpoint levels contain mobs during the event, and the maps are narrow hallways where Meteor Swarm and fields are especially problematic. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-01-11–2022-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24018/)
- **escaping reds in dungeons**: To escape reds in dungeons, use pouches, heals and tracking, and position near a red gate so you can run when reds show up. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 3 authors, 2023-01-29–2023-01-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26930/)
- **field limits in dungeon flashpoints**: Town Struggles limit how many fields a player can have up, but Dungeon Flashpoints and contested bosses do not, so players can keep 10+ paralyze fields active at once; players have asked for the same limit. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-01-08–2022-01-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/23984/)
- **flamestrike damage**: Flamestrike deals ((30 to 45) * (Magery/100) * (.75 + .25 * (Eval Int/100))) damage to a player, so 30-45 at 100 Magery and Eval Int, and it can hit for 40+ against a GM Magic Resist target with 20% SDI. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-09-04–2020-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16111/)
- **grey flagging and murder counts**: Attacking a red player's pet flags the attacker grey, which lets them fight the red, and killing the red as a grey does not add to the attacker's murder count, though other players may see grey players as hostile and attack them. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-10-04–2019-02-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10648/)
- **grey healers supporting reds**: Grey characters carrying reagents for X-healing can heal reds during a fight to help them escape, and a dead grey must wait 120 seconds before resurrecting. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-11–2018-12-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6784/)
- **hamstring effect**: Hamstring is not a stun and does not stop the target from acting; it only restricts movement, draining stamina to 0 for about 3 seconds in PvP so the target must walk, and acting as a root in PvM. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-06-24–2019-06-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14026/)
- **healing summoned creatures**: Summoned creatures can be healed with the Healing skill and bandages, or through Magic. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-10-08–2018-10-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2954/)
- **herding in eldritch suit**: With 120 Spirit Speak, Herding may be a good option in an Eldritch suit because it is believed to increase the damage of summoned creatures. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-04-08–2019-04-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12373/)
- **hiding with a spell on cursor**: You cannot hide while a pre-cast spell is held on your cursor; you must drop the cursor first. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-02-08–2019-02-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9546/)
- **invulnerable armor damage mitigation**: Invulnerable armor is believed to provide substantial damage mitigation against heavy hits, though no specific numbers are given. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-03-19–2019-03-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11743/)
- **komodo pet team farming speed**: Many other GM pet teams farm at a speed comparable to Komodos (GM Husk Crabs), and some perform better in many cases. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-08-13–2020-08-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18620/)
- **magic resist cap and void armor**: Void aspect armor gives a chance to ignore armor or magic resist plus Parry and Magic Resistance bonuses, and Magic Resist can reportedly be raised past 100 using it, though the exact source is uncertain. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-02-20–2021-01-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20269/)
- **mindblast armor penalty workaround**: To avoid the Mindblast armor meditation penalty in PvP, carry GM bone armor and swap into it with a dress/undress macro or agent, or mix lighter pieces such as a studded leather chest with bone legs and arms; dropping shield, chest and legs usually suffices against a solo mage. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2023-01-20–2023-01-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26859/)
- **mounts in dexxer versus mage fights**: Outside dungeons, a mount lets you run faster, so a mounted dexxer can range or close on a mage more easily, while a fleeing mounted mage is hard for a dexxer to finish. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-03-27–2024-03-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29111/)
- **pk blue mule gold looting**: Blue healers can heal reds, and a grouped PK can use a gate after a longer wait; a PK can bring a blue mule to carry gold so loot escapes even if the PK is caught. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-12–2018-12-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6953/)
- **player killer ambush tactics**: Player killers ambush players using stealthed mages (e.g. energy bolt) and dungeon level transitions or entrances to nuke single players as they enter. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2025-11-30–2025-11-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32601/)
- **poison fencer stealth pk build**: A stealth PK build using a poison fencer template is viable on Outlands, and a player known as Trammy Surprise has posted videos of it. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-05-01–2019-05-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13217/)
- **poison pet teams**: Poison pets such as Swamp Drakes and Wyverns can be used as a team with backloaded DPS, which works best against many tanky mobs: poison everything first, then focus each one down. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-07-17–2020-08-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18570/)
- **poison upgrade chance in pvp**: In PvP, a Poison spell cast with 120 Poisoning cannot upgrade to Lethal Poison. The chance to upgrade to Deadly Poison is about 24% after the target's resist chance. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-05-05–2019-05-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13319/)
- **poisoning pvm viability**: Poisoning is a weak PvM template that needs roughly 240 skill points, including GM Magery, to be decent and ranks last in damage among templates; a claim that GM Poisoning adds about 75% PvM damage is uncertain. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-10-12–2019-05-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13665/)
- **poisoning use cases**: Poisoning works best as supplemental damage when farming areas with many mobs, and it is useful against minibosses and bosses. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-05-27–2019-05-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13680/)
- **pvp sdi template norms**: Many PvP players run 8% SDI templates, mostly to support Hamstring on the halberd. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-01-11–2020-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16105/)
- **red dr and defense links while aggressor**: A red player's DR and Defense mastery links stay active while the red is flagged as an aggressor against a player, so reds take negligible damage from mobs. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2026-06-26–2026-06-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33283/)
- **refresh potion for dexxers**: A refresh potion restores a dexxer's stamina after it has been drained by taking damage. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2023-01-13–2023-01-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26827/)
- **sanctuary dungeon**: Outlands has a single sanctuary dungeon that serves as a safe option for PvM players. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-07-06–2024-07-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29963/)
- **snowdrift flurry damage nerf**: As of 2019-01-17, Snowdrifts' projectile special ability (flurry) damage was reduced by 50%, from 'damage max' to 'damage max * 0.5', in both PvM and PvP, and staff said this was not intentional. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-01-17–2019-01-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8509/)
- **thrown explosion potions in pvm**: Thrown explosion potions can miss moving PvM monsters because the target moves before detonation, and players say the Telekinesis skill makes the potion stick to its target. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-01-15–2019-01-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8379/)
- **trapped pouch usage**: Use a trapped pouch to break paralyze, or to deal 1 damage to yourself so you can start your bandage timer on your own terms. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-02-20–2024-02-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28900/)
- **void weapon aspect proc timing**: The Void weapon aspect proc (Doom) is slow to trigger, so when soloing, mobs are often dead or nearly dead before it procs and the proc is wasted. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2023-09-13–2023-09-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28237/)
- **weapon damage range and pvm balance**: Weapon damage rolls are probably tied to PvM damage calculations, so tightening weapon damage ranges would likely significantly affect PvM DPS balance. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-04-20–2022-04-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25000/)
- **wrestling swing speed and damage**: Wrestling on Outlands swings about as fast as a katana at 25 dex, and wrestlers are reported to hit for a consistent 32 HP at the fastest swing speed in PvP. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-10-21–2023-01-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26932/)

## New players

- **accelerate connection via launcher**: To speed up the connection to the live server using Outlands' internal VPN, click the Settings Cog in the top right corner of the launcher and select Accelerate. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2022-12-06–2022-12-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26609/)
- **creating an outlandsid**: To create an OutlandsID, run the launcher to download the latest client (ClassicUO.exe) and apply the patch, click Create OutlandsID, enter a valid e-mail address, retrieve the verification code from your e-mail, then enter the code and choose a password. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2022-12-06–2022-12-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26602/)
- **launcher fonts directory error fix**: If the launcher shows the error "Could not create directory for 'ClassicUO\Data\Client\Fonts\alagard.ttf'", create a new folder named "Fonts" in the 'ClassicUO\Data\Client' folder and run the launcher again. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2022-12-06–2022-12-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26609/)
- **linking accounts to an outlandsid**: To link existing accounts to an OutlandsID, click Link Account and repeat the process for each of your accounts (up to 3); this is required to access existing characters and game data. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2022-12-06–2025-08-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26602/)
- **logging in after outlandsid setup**: After setting up your OutlandsID, select the shard you want (UO Outlands or Test) and log in as normal. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2022-12-06–2022-12-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26602/)
- **outlands discord invite link**: The official Ultima Online: Outlands Discord server can be joined via the invite link http://discord.uooutlands.com, which is also linked from the Outlands home page. _(official, conf 0.85, 2 authors, 2018-05-26–2019-10-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2396/)
- **outlandsid login system**: Outlands uses a single email-based login called an OutlandsID, under which players link their existing accounts and access all of them from one login instead of separate account name and password combinations. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2022-12-06–2024-05-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26602/)
- **running the launcher as administrator**: Run the Outlands launcher (and installer/patcher) as Administrator, which resolves most launcher issues. _(official, conf 0.85, 2 authors, 2018-10-28–2022-12-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26609/)
- **vpn when creating outlandsid account**: Players should turn off any VPN when creating or linking an OutlandsID account. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2022-12-06–2022-12-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26609/)
- **discord newplayer channel**: The Outlands Discord has an active #newplayer channel where new players get quick answers, tutorials, and help from volunteer Companion players, and a #guilds-recruiting channel for finding guilds. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2018-10-05–2026-09-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33754/)
- **new player dungeon skill gain**: Skill gain inside the New Player Dungeon is increased by 400% of normal (a flat bonus that needs no kills) for non-crafting, non-harvesting skills, and applies only up to 70 skill. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2018-04-24–2025-07-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31977/)
- **newbie dungeon loot drops**: Monsters in the Outlands newbie dungeon can drop magic items and scrolls, including unidentified magic gear, invulnerable studded gorgets, arcane and discordance scrolls, with seemingly random drops and more chances from higher-tier monsters. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2018-11-21–2018-11-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4848/)
- **newbie dungeon skill gain**: The newbie dungeon on Shelter Island gives an always-active 10x skill gain bonus that does not stack with the mob kill bonus, and skills there are capped (reported as 70 in older claims and 80 in the newest claim). _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2018-03-18–2024-03-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29037/)
- **newbie dungeon skill gain bonus**: The newbie dungeon grants a 10x skill gain bonus (reported to last about 10 minutes after a kill) at the cost of roughly 50% gold from kills, while kills outside it give about a 5x bonus, and overall skill gain on Outlands is slower than on other shards but favors active play over macroing. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2018-11-05–2024-02-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28883/)
- **outlands client**: The Outlands client is a bundled build of ClassicUO that includes Razor and is required by the server, and staff recommend it for new players. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2019-03-16–2023-11-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28558/)
- **sanctuary dungeon safety**: The Sanctuary dungeon is a safe, small Trammel-like dungeon in Outlands where PK combat does not occur, making it a refuge for new players and anyone avoiding PKs, separate from the newbie dungeon. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2023-07-10–2026-08-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33649/)
- **new player dungeon gold drops**: Gold drops from creatures in the New Player Dungeon are reduced to 75% of normal, so they are small but enough for basic equipment, with most second-level monsters dropping about 200 gold. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-01-15–2026-01-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32736/)
- **new player starting location**: New players start on Shelter Island, a safe haven with a newbie dungeon, and are advised to stay there and keep new player status until they learn the basics. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-10-28–2026-06-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33308/)
- **outlands pvp ruleset**: Outlands is a Felucca-style non-consensual open-PvP shard where PKing is allowed and PvM players can be attacked by PKs in unsafe dungeons; a 2019 mention of planned guild wars is unconfirmed. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2019-02-13–2024-05-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29448/)
- **pvm progression systems**: Outlands has PvM progression systems of codex, aspect, skill scrolls/orbs, and the mastery chain (with redline also mentioned), typically progressed in that order and documented on the Outlands wiki. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2022-02-01–2026-08-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33625/)
- **shelter island skill cap**: On Shelter Island characters start with 700 total skill points and a 70 per-skill cap. Skill gain there gets a 10x bonus until 70 skill and no further bonus beyond that, so Mining can be trained to 70 there. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2019-02-18–2022-02-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24362/)
- **shelter island skill gain bonus**: The Shelter Island new player dungeon gives 10x skill gains on most skills up to 70 skill, excluding crafting and taming; combat skills get the bonus inside the dungeon, while other skills get it on the island proper. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2019-02-02–2025-01-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31056/)
- **steam deck compatibility**: UO Outlands can run on the Steam Deck and works for basic activities like farming and mining, but touch-screen-only play is poor, so a mouse and keyboard are recommended, especially for mages who bind 20+ spells. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2023-01-02–2024-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28689/)
- **account password recovery**: To recover a forgotten Outlands account or password, join the Outlands Discord, go to the #support channel, and open an Account Support ticket; only staff can help with recovery. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2020-02-01–2024-08-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30256/)
- **character creation and skill progression**: With the Advanced creation option the player picks 3 skills and divides 100 starting points among them; skills increase through use rather than levels, and skills can be lowered to raise others, so early choices are not permanent. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-10-25–2025-03-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31579/)
- **character creation workaround**: To avoid the character creation map problem, create the character using Steam/UOS or Razor, then restart and log in with ClassicUO to play. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-12-23–2020-01-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16059/)
- **end game and server style**: Outlands is a sandbox with no single fixed end game, where players choose among PvM, PvP, gathering, crafting, sailing and roleplay, and it has full-loot PvP risk, so players must adapt. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2023-02-09–2025-03-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31499/)
- **new player guilds**: Outlands has no official new-player guild, but player guilds such as Beard Brothers [BB], a non-PvP guild, and Prev, plus New!, a guild for new players, are suggested for newcomers to learn the shard. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-01-22–2020-07-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18442/)
- **newbie dungeon skill gain limits**: The newbie dungeon gives no taming skill gain boost, and skills cannot be gained past 70.0 there. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-06-10–2021-05-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21841/)
- **non-consensual pvp**: Outlands is a non-Trammel shard with non-consensual PvP, so players can be attacked, killed and stolen from by other players, including murderers, while gathering resources or travelling. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2023-08-09–2024-10-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30494/)
- **peace dexxer build**: A peace dexxer, which peaces or discords mobs and then kills them with little risk, is a common warrior starting build that is good for farming dungeons and scales into the late game, including high-tier mobs with Lyric Aspect. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-08–2020-09-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19060/)
- **pulling health bars**: To pull a health bar, hold the left mouse button on the player or monster and drag away from it; holding ctrl+shift can make pulling bars easier. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-10-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2753/)
- **sanctuary dungeon purpose**: Sanctuary dungeons are mainly useful for new players, who can learn dungeon layouts and farm early gold and XP without fear of PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2024-07-16–2024-08-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30245/)
- **sanctuary dungeons for new players**: Sanctuary dungeons let players avoid PvP interaction and serve as a safe stepping stone from Shelter Island, suited to new players learning dungeons. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2024-07-08–2025-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31989/)
- **shelter island vendors after young status**: Once a player loses Young status, the NPC vendors on the starter island (Shelter or Sanctuary Island) no longer buy from or sell to them, but skills can still be leveled there. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-02-07–2025-06-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31884/)
- **tracking hunter mode setup**: To find monsters to hunt, activate the Tracking skill, turn on Hunting mode, and set it to target the closest hostile or current combatant. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-05–2020-03-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16693/)
- **vpn use on accounts**: A VPN may be kept on when logging into existing accounts, including from another location, but it should be turned off when creating new accounts because using one to circumvent the account creation limit is not permitted. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2022-04-01–2022-04-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24887/)
- **bard dexxer build**: The bard dexxer build is a high-efficiency dexxer build that is easy and safe for killing mobs early on, but it is weak against player attackers, and a bard codex has made it among the best out-of-the-gate builds again. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-13–2021-09-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22793/)
- **character build variety**: Outlands has no pre-defined path and supports many viable builds, including tamers, summoners, mages, dexxers, bard dexxers, shadow rogues, and 7x, 8x, 4x100 and 4x80 templates, so new players can start with any character. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-08-09–2023-04-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/27229/)
- **character progression speed**: Building a character is faster and easier on Outlands than in original UO, since the server aims to get players up and running quickly. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-24–2021-11-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/23465/)
- **cheap arrow supply**: To get arrows cheaply, make a crafting mule with about 50 Lumberjacking and 50 Carpentry plus some Forensic Evaluation, buy tools (hatchets, saws, skinning knives or swords with the starter check), then chop wood and skin the birds killed on Shelter Island to craft arrows. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-01–2019-03-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11127/)
- **classicuo client support**: ClassicUO, a high-FPS client lead-developed by Karasho, can be used to play Outlands and serves as the replacement for the Orion client. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-25–2020-11-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19681/)
- **classicuo data folder location**: The Outlands ClassicUO data folder is by default under Program Files (x86) in the Ultima Online Outlands directory at ClassicUO\Data and contains Client, Plugins and Profiles folders; if installed elsewhere, it is under that install directory. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-08-14–2022-05-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25278/)
- **classicuo windows scaling**: In ClassicUO, set Windows display scaling to about 125-150% (125% on a 2K monitor, 200% on a 4K monitor) to enlarge the game so zooming is not needed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-07-23–2019-08-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14488/)
- **custom skill template starting points**: A custom skill template uses 100 total points, and a 50/49/1 split gives three sets of starting items; the template also grants 100 training credits, enough to train two other skills to 50. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-03-18–2018-10-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3530/)
- **dungeon survival potions**: Players should carry potions such as greater strength, healing, cure and refresh when hunting in dungeons and use them all when PKs arrive. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-11-30–2026-01-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32739/)
- **fast early skill training route**: Train all skills to 70 in the newbie dungeon (possible in a couple of hours), then move on to a starter undead ruins south of Prevalia or any other dungeon and work through its levels. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-05–2018-11-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4823/)
- **full screen mode**: The Ultima Online client's full screen option is hardcoded legacy from the CRT era, does not suit modern displays and can cut off the view, so players should avoid it on Outlands. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-27–2018-10-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3836/)
- **game window resolution**: A larger game window resolution in the Outlands client shows more of the world, and the launcher can be used to set sizes such as 1100x720. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-19–2018-11-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4766/)
- **high ping pvp viability**: Players with 250-300ms ping can still play and win PvP on Outlands, including against prominent miners and lumberjacks. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-14–2018-12-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7079/)
- **installing on linux with wine**: To run Outlands on Linux, install wine, winetricks and winbind (winbind is required for the patcher), install dotnet40 via winetricks into a 32-bit Wine prefix, then run the installer with 'WINEARCH=win32 WINEPREFIX=~/.wine32 wine ./OutlandsUO.exe', after which the client files download automatically. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-04–2019-03-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11618/)
- **language support**: The Outlands server and wiki are in English, but there is an active Spanish-speaking community, so Spanish speakers must read English or translate the wiki. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2025-05-31–2025-05-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31832/)
- **larger game window**: To get a larger game window on high resolution monitors, use the forced resolution option in the Outlands launcher, or force a higher client resolution via an assist program like Razor or UO Steam instead of using full screen. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-29–2019-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14319/)
- **leaving shelter island**: Leaving Shelter Island removes young/New Player status and new adventurer guide assistance, so returning players can no longer interact with the island's NPCs. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-02–2019-02-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10044/)
- **mage template difficulty**: Starting as a mage is generally harder than starting other templates, because the character begins with magic skills and only a few basic spells. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-01-07–2018-10-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2753/)
- **mongbat gold drops in newbie dungeon**: Mongbats in the Outlands newbie dungeon drop small amounts of gold, around 6 gold each. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-23–2019-03-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12031/)
- **new character start location**: After character creation on Outlands a Welcome Screen is shown and the new character starts on Shelter Island with Young status. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-27–2025-07-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31977/)
- **new player dungeon location**: The New Player Dungeon is on the northwest side of Shelter Island, with an additional entrance in the mines, and counts as part of Shelter Island for skill gain and Young player handling. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-18–2025-07-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31977/)
- **new player quests**: Outlands has a system of beginner quests aligned with the starter templates that introduces codex, aspect, skill scrolls/orbs and the mastery chain and gives free items, so new players should work on them early. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2025-07-10–2026-08-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33625/)
- **new player training scroll**: New characters receive a Training Scroll worth 10K that can be spent on vendor skill training, and it works similarly to vendor training for gaining skill. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-03–2024-08-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30241/)
- **newbie dungeon**: The Newbie Dungeon on Outlands is a safer dungeon with a trammel-like ruleset where players cannot be killed by other players. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-28–2019-03-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11365/)
- **newbie dungeon gold and skill gain tradeoff**: The newbie dungeon gives roughly half the normal gold with very low drops but a much higher skill gain rate (about 10x), letting a character reach 70 skill quickly, and mob choice for farming gold there does not matter. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-06-28–2018-11-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3874/)
- **newbie dungeon skill training**: The safe newbie dungeon lets players quickly train almost any skill from 0 to 80 with little downtime, except crafting and gathering skills. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-07-03–2024-02-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28818/)
- **newbie dungeons**: New players can safely start in a couple of low-level dungeons, with dungeon levels 1 and 2 usually manageable. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-06-21–2026-07-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33375/)
- **newbied items on death**: Items labeled [Newbied] stay with the player on death and do not drop to the corpse, but once Young/New Player status is lost they become normal and drop to the corpse. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-18–2025-07-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31977/)
- **obtaining a house**: To get a house on Outlands, buy one from a player, win one when it goes IDOC, rent an inn room while saving, or enter an expansion lottery. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-11-13–2025-10-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32360/)
- **outlands installer**: The Outlands installer downloads a separate installation of UO, all custom patch files, and Razor/UOSteam in one package, so players do not need to supply their own client files. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2017-10-13–2018-10-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2814/)
- **outlands population and focus**: Outlands is the largest free Ultima Online shard, with roughly 1,500 to 2,000+ players online, and focuses on PvM, PvP and heavy customization without being strictly roleplay. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-06-30–2025-05-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31831/)
- **outlands system rework and skill changes**: Outlands has heavily reworked most classic UO systems and revamped formerly useless skills, and players can change their skills after character creation, so a bad initial build is not permanent. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-08-16–2025-07-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32060/)
- **outlands wiki**: The Outlands wiki is thoroughly documented and contains detailed information on skilling and other topics, including the Camping skill. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-22–2025-01-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31056/)
- **pet command controls**: Pets can be controlled with commands such as All Follow, All Guard, All Attack and All Patrol, including pets like the hind. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-09-18–2023-11-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28526/)
- **player killer risk**: Outlands has many player killers who frequent certain areas and attack new and veteran players, so players should bank farmed loot often, for example after about 3-4k gold. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-10–2019-11-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15538/)
- **returning to shelter island after young status**: Players who have lost Young status can still return to Shelter Island and enter its dungeon, but they do not receive Young benefits such as NPC, vendor and bank access or Newbied item handling. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-03-28–2025-07-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31977/)
- **server address**: The Outlands server address is play.uooutlands.com on port 2593 (a 2019 claim also lists IP 74.91.113.38), and ClassicUO is configured with client version 7.0.15.1. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-23–2019-05-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13635/)
- **shelter island bank access**: Only characters with Young status can open the bank on Shelter Island; once Young status is lost, the player must use a bank in another city. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2025-01-11–2025-07-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31977/)
- **shelter island newbie dungeon**: Shelter Island has a Newbie Dungeon where new players can explore and try out combat, and it is described as a place that is relatively protected from PKs. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-17–2023-04-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/27229/)
- **shelter island restrictions**: Players who are no longer new players may stay on Shelter Island to welcome friends but cannot use its bank or shops, and must leave the island to use NPC banks and shops. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-06–2019-02-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10044/)
- **shelter island safety**: Shelter Island is a safe area in Outlands where players are protected from murderers until they leave the island. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-07-08–2020-08-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18607/)
- **starting bandages**: Every character template starts with bandages, and having 1 point in Healing at the start lets a new character get the free starting bandages, so throwaway characters can be used to stock early bandages. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-03-18–2019-01-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8322/)
- **starting clothing for new characters**: Starting clothing on Outlands is not affected by chosen skills; characters start with a shirt or doublet, pants or a skirt/kilt depending on gender, and sometimes shoes. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-22–2018-12-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7495/)
- **starting dungeon level for new players**: New players should begin hunting on level 1 of a dungeon (level 2 is also possible depending on the dungeon), get a feel for it, and work up to deeper levels such as 2 or 3 while staying near the gates until comfortable. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-02-18–2020-05-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17556/)
- **starting training credits deed**: New characters start with a deed worth 1000 credits that pays for skill training, where 1 credit equals 1 gp. The credits can only be spent on training, while training can also be paid for with gold. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-28–2019-02-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10038/)
- **supported game clients**: Outlands must be played with the ClassicUO client supplied by Outlands; the KR client and the Broadsword Enhanced Client are not supported. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-10-25–2020-11-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19681/)
- **tab key war and peace mode**: Pressing Tab toggles between War and Peace mode, and in War mode you can attack by double-clicking targets. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-02–2018-11-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4536/)
- **template support and flexibility**: Most community template and build help is on the Outlands Discord (e.g. #templates-builds, #taming, #pvm, #newplayer), and almost any skill template can be made to work. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-09-19–2022-12-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26645/)
- **treasure map drops in newbie dungeon**: Treasure maps, including level 4 maps, can drop in the newbie dungeon; a level 4 map was reported dropping from a harpy. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-23–2018-11-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4924/)
- **urukton new player pks**: Urukton, the new player dungeon, attracts inexperienced PKs who prey on new players, which can make it riskier than level one of a harder dungeon. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-07-16–2024-07-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30110/)
- **variety of playstyles**: Outlands supports many activities beyond a single playstyle, including faction battles, seafaring, treasure hunting, crafting, gathering, dungeon adventuring, thieving, PKing and farming. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-01–2020-08-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18607/)
- **wiki new player guide**: The Outlands wiki has a New Player Guide at https://uooutlands.com/wiki/New_Player_Guide, but it is generic and does not cover what to do after the first day. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-11-20–2024-03-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29037/)
- **young status on shelter island**: Players keep Young status until they leave Shelter Island, and staying costs nothing, though a confirmation is required to remain. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-11-20–2025-07-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31977/)
- **youtube tutorial video series**: Outlands has a YouTube tutorial video series for new players covering topics from downloading the game and running a first dungeon to PvP, as well as UI interactions and targeting. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-08-05–2026-09-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33754/)
- **benefits of joining a guild**: Joining an active guild helps new players get started and makes hunting safer than hunting alone. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 3 authors, 2020-02-03–2020-02-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16404/)
- **buying lockpicks**: Lockpicks can be bought from town vendors, which is easier early on than crafting them with low Tinkering skill. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-08-17–2018-08-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1943/)
- **character creation map screen bug**: If the character creation map screen shows the original OSI map and will not let you continue, click the green next arrow at the bottom right anyway, as it usually works. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-12-23–2019-12-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15971/)
- **character selection screen after auto login**: If the client logs you straight into the game without showing the character selection screen, un-check the "Auto Login" box on the client to get the character selection screen. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-12-03–2020-12-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19789/)
- **discord invite fallback**: If the Discord button on the Outlands website does not produce an invite, use a direct discord.gg invite link to the Outlands Discord server to join. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2026-07-30–2026-07-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33512/)
- **gaming vpn for high ping**: Using a gaming VPN such as WTFast can make a high ping of around 230ms workable for playing Outlands. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-14–2018-12-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7066/)
- **launcher fix under wine**: To fix the Outlands launcher under Wine, create the DWORD registry value HKEY_CURRENT_USER\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Avalon.Graphics\DisableHWAcceleration and set it to 1 (for example via 'wine regedit'). _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-02-27–2019-02-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10654/)
- **launcher virus warnings**: The Outlands launcher and game contain no virus, and antivirus alerts on them are false positives. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-04-03–2019-04-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12179/)
- **outlands client separation**: Installing the Outlands client does not affect the game files of other shards such as UOR, because Outlands uses a separate client and launcher. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2017-10-12–2017-10-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/525/)
- **pk groups in dungeon levels**: PK groups are reported to be mainly a problem on dungeon levels 1 and 2, where they kill newbies, while level 3 sees fewer players. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-12-25–2022-12-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26716/)
- **quests**: Currently the only quests available on Ultima Online: Outlands are the New Player quests, and quests for non-new players are planned for the future. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-04-04–2025-07-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32000/)
- **reagent restocking routine**: To avoid running out of reagents, buy them from vendors at login and again before logout, and keep a runebook with a rune to every mage shop. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2021-06-21–2021-06-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22241/)
- **running two accounts on one computer**: To run two Outlands accounts at once on one computer, launch the first client with the launcher's play button, then click the launcher's play button again to start a second client. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-10-26–2020-10-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19352/)
- **safer hunting for newer players**: Newer players are recommended to explore level 2 of dungeons and stay near gates so they can escape if attacked. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 3 authors, 2024-07-16–2024-07-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30076/)
- **selling items to npc vendors**: To sell items to an NPC vendor, click the vendor and choose sell, or say 'Vendor Sell' near the vendor, and a sell window will pop up; this differs from the player-to-player trade window. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-10-28–2018-10-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3818/)
- **steam deck setup guide**: A guide for running UO Outlands on the Steam Deck is available in the GitHub repository roxya/OutlandsSteamDeck. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-01-11–2024-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28689/)
- **town protection**: Town guards protect players inside town, including around the bank, but players are not protected outside of town. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-06-26–2024-06-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29818/)
- **young status duration**: A character reportedly loses Young status after about 8 hours of in-game time, although this figure is uncertain. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2025-06-13–2025-06-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31883/)

## Scripting & razor

- **assigning the thief's target**: Assign the thief's target using the newly added client macro; it cannot be set using Razor. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **client-side last target macros**: Client-side last target macros remember the targeted mobile rather than its previous ground location when a mobile was targeted, such as with explosion potions. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **inventory counters**: Counters show correct inventory counts for both worn and nested containers at any point, even if the container was not manually opened, which fixes the 'satchel bug'. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **last target and stealing target separation**: Regular last target handling is completely decoupled from stealing target handling. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **razor action queue options**: The Razor Action Queue options are disabled in the beta client, and the related options are found in the client under the General section. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **razor auto search containers option**: The Razor 'auto search containers' option has been removed because it is obsolete. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **razor lift action preview**: Any lift action performed by Razor now previews the item being held, just as it does when the lift is done via the client. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **unified action queue in beta client**: In the Outlands beta client, the Action Queue is unified so that Razor and the client use the same pathways for lift/drop and double click actions, which removes delay conflicts between them. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **using the thief's target with stealing**: To use the thief's target, use the steal thief's target client macro, or target yourself with the stealing target cursor, which also works via Razor target self. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **active skillgain bonus buff detection**: The Active Skillgain Bonus buff displays the Arcane Empowerment icon, so in UO Steam it must be detected with buffexists 'Arcane Empowerment'; the name 'Active Skillgain Bonus' does not work. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-21–2019-02-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9291/)
- **razor auto search trap risk**: Razor's auto search of new containers is on by default and can trigger a tinker trap on a nested trapped chest or crate when moved into your pack, so disable it if you handle trapped containers. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-03-19–2019-03-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11751/)
- **razor hotkeys setup**: To set hotkeys, open Razor, go to the Hot Keys tab, find the action you want (the filter search lets you type its name) and assign a key to it. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-04-05–2024-05-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29459/)
- **razor taming script**: Malbolger's Razor taming script runs from a hotkey that uses 'hotkey Animal Taming' then waitfortarget; stay within a couple of tiles of the creature, and it loops with a 12000 ms pause (10000 ms if no target) until stopped, and may report item type not found if no creature is nearby. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2020-07-31–2021-01-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19998/)
- **razor target closest hotkeys**: Razor lists target-closest hotkeys for the notoriety groups, but on Outlands the closest-target hotkeys do nothing in game because the shard disables them. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-02-16–2019-03-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11596/)
- **uo steam buffexists macro command**: In UO Steam macros, 'if buffexists' followed by a buff name in quotes (such as 'Arcane Empowerment') checks whether that buff is active, and it works with the name of any icon shown in the buff bar. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-02-04–2019-02-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9454/)
- **afk taming script failure causes**: The AFK taming script commonly fails because of incorrect Razor settings or trying to tame non-aggressive animals, and having a weapon equipped can make the character keep attacking and trigger the 'enemy has taken dmg' error, while going unarmed makes recycling the mob after a tame slower. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-03-09–2021-05-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21793/)
