- **classicuo client requirement**: Outlands players expect, and the devs have reportedly said, that the shard will likely eventually allow only the ClassicUO client so all players are on the same footing and targeting-rule workarounds can be blocked, but no firm date is confirmed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-21–2019-06-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14014/)
- **classicuo exclusive client plan**: Players report that Outlands is moving toward making ClassicUO the only allowed client and removing Steam completely, but this is not officially confirmed here. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-07-29–2020-10-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19346/)
- **dagger in afk taming script**: The AFK taming script needs a dagger in your backpack to establish the 'last target' it uses; the dagger does nothing itself, and without it the script may never detect a nearby mob once Animal Taming is over 70. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-05-17–2021-05-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21876/)
- **default client size setup**: To set the default client size, open the Outlands Launcher before choosing Razor or UO Steam, go to the Options tab, and set the client size once; if the launcher cannot force a larger window, run Razor directly (downloaded from the Razor website and pointed at the UO Outlands folder) and set the forced game size in Razor's options. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-02–2019-04-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12230/)
- **extracting gump images**: Original gump images can be extracted with UOFiddler from the classic Ultima Online client .mul files, because Outlands uses its own .uoo format that cannot be explored. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-02-19–2024-02-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28893/)
- **finding gump response ids for scripts**: Use the >info command to find the gump response ids needed for a script. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-05-27–2021-05-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21894/)
- **item graphic ids list**: Known graphic IDs include ringmail gorget 0x13d6, gloves 0x13f2, helm 0x140b; chainmail gorget 0x13c7, coif 0x13c0; platemail gloves 0x1418, helm 0x1412, gorget 0x1413; shields 0x1b72-0x1b7b; spear 0xf62; kryss 0x1401; dagger 0xf52; katana 0x13ff; hatchet 0xf43; spellbook 0xefa. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-15–2019-11-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15630/)
- **item identification journal messages**: When scripting Item Identification, a 'not found' system message appears in the journal if no identification wand is in your pack, and a message containing 'not certain' appears when an identification attempt fails. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-11-12–2019-11-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15630/)
- **macroing policy**: Macroing is allowed on Ultima Online: Outlands and many parts of gameplay can be automated, provided players follow the server rules. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-07–2020-03-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17028/)
- **outlands razor scripts website**: The website outlands.uorazorscripts.com, operated by Jaseowns, hosts example Razor scripts for Outlands, including scripts for training skills, and each script begins with a section describing what is needed for it to work. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-06-13–2024-02-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28829/)
- **razor attack target setting**: In the Razor backstab script, set your target by targeting the zoo monster first and using 'lasttarget' in the attack command, or attack by name using quotes, for example attack 'sandcrawler'. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-09-02–2020-10-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19369/)
- **razor gate travel message fix**: To stop the repeated 'you cannot gate travel from that object' message when using bandages or attacking, turn off 'show buff/debuff overhead' in Razor under Options in Speech & Messages. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-04-16–2020-04-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17288/)
- **razor hotkey toggle**: F9 in Razor enables or disables Razor hotkeys, letting you type normally in game without triggering hotkeys. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **razor instant rope scripts**: Razor scripts can automatically use a rope to escape when the player is hamstrung, and some scripts pre-target a tile up to about 10 tiles ahead so the rope is applied instantly. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-11–2022-01-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24116/)
- **razor lumberjacking macro**: A Razor lumberjacking macro does not need to loop: run at a group of trees it keeps chopping until no wood remains and then stops; a more elaborate version double-clicks kindling by type, then the hatchet, targets self, and on a system message containing 'resources' says 'lets move' so the player knows to relocate. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-25–2018-11-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5061/)
- **razor versus steam scripting**: Razor is easier for new scripters to use, while Steam is the more powerful but more complex scripting client. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-06-23–2019-07-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14423/)
- **razor waypoint on death crash**: The Razor option 'create waypoint on death' can cause the Outlands classic client to crash every time the player character dies. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-01-01–2021-01-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19985/)
- **razor weapon swap via arm/dress**: In Razor, use the Arm/Dress tab to swap weapons in PvP: create a dress entry per weapon, add the target item to each, and bind the 'dress' hotkey, which avoids losing a pre-cast spell that happens when you double-click a weapon to equip it. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-19–2024-02-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28882/)
- **steam closest enemy targeting**: UO Steam blocks the 'closest enemy' targeting function on Outlands, just as Razor blocks target closest. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-21–2018-11-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4857/)
- **steam closest target workaround**: Because closest targeting is blocked, a UO Steam macro can approximate the nearest target by collecting enemies with getenemy into a list, sorting them by testing findobject at increasing distances from 1 to 15 tiles, and storing the closest in an alias; target priority follows your notoriety color (e.g. murderers search murderer, enemy, criminal, gray, humanoid and transformation targets). _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-20–2019-02-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9521/)
- **tank taming macro timers**: The tank taming macro uses a 14 second timer between Animal Taming attempts and an 8 second timer between bandage heals of the tank pet. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-14–2020-08-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18623/)
- **target closest and pvp scriptability**: Target closest is disabled on Outlands, along with several other UOSteam features such as target share, auto bandage, autoloot, health checks and in-journal checks, which makes PvP much less scriptable, although UOSteam itself is still allowed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-01-12–2019-02-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10282/)
- **uo automap support**: UO Automap (UOAM) no longer works on Outlands because the UO Assist API was removed in the 2025.03.31 patch, and its use is no longer allowed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-31–2025-03-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31651/)
- **uo client lumberjacking macro**: In the UO client, a lumberjacking macro can use 'LastObject' or 'ObjectInHand' followed by 'TargetSelf', pressed on a timer or looped, so the player only needs to move when the trees run out of resources. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-26–2018-11-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5208/)
- **uo steam warmode macro**: In a UO Steam macro, use "warmode 'on'" or "warmode 'off'" to set war mode directly, or wrap them in a check such as "if warmode 'on' warmode 'off' endif" to turn it off conditionally. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-17–2019-04-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12656/)
- **uos weight comparison scripting**: In UOS scripting, 'diffweight' equals maxweight minus weight and is the correct way to detect nearing max weight (e.g. 'if diffweight < 10'), while 'if weight > maxweight' only detects being over the limit. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-10–2018-12-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6754/)
- **afk taming script tank pet restriction**: With the AFK taming script, your tank pet must not be the same type as the animal you are taming, otherwise the script may try to release or tame your own pet. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2021-05-15–2021-05-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21777/)
- **classicuo client basis**: ClassicUO is a client for Ultima Online: Outlands that is described as being built on or using Razor. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-07-29–2019-07-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14423/)
- **emulating target closest**: Using scripts, macros or other workarounds to emulate target closest circumvents the Outlands targeting restriction and is likely forbidden and bannable. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-02-19–2019-02-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10104/)
- **escape gate hotkey macro**: To use an escape gate quickly, bind a hotkey to a Razor script; ready-made scripts are available on the UO Outlands Razor scripts site. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-07-16–2024-07-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30077/)
- **finding item graphic ids**: To find an item's graphic ID (0x...) for use in UOS scripts, use the Counter or Scavenger agent, or the Inspector. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-15–2018-11-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4416/)
- **gump hex ids**: Outlands gump IDs include 0x7d0 for the paperdoll, 0x2A6C for the status bar, and 0x003C for the plain undyed backpack. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 3 authors, 2024-02-19–2025-10-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32382/)
- **item identification sorting macro**: An Item Identification macro can sort unidentified items by selecting a source bag, a sell bag and a keep bag, using Item Identification on each item, and moving items whose identified properties match keep-worthy qualities into the keep bag. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-11-12–2019-11-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15589/)
- **light filters**: The game engine allows assistants such as Razor and Steam to apply a light filter that keeps the game at full daylight. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-10-02–2018-10-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2761/)
- **magery trainer script recall fizzle**: If a recall spell fizzles in the Magery trainer script, the character stays at the bank and keeps casting lightning; adding 2-3 recalls to the script works around this. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-01-23–2019-01-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8771/)
- **outlands customized orion client**: The Outlands customized Orion client is an allowed client, still unfinished but playable, and it does not have the closest and nearest target commands disabled. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-02-10–2019-02-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9623/)
- **razor fishing pole dress setup**: To set up a fishing pole dress item in Razor, create a new dress on the Arm/Dress tab, click Add (Target) and select the fishing pole, then right-click the pole (0DC0) in the Arm/Dress Items and choose Convert Selected to TYPE. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-01-15–2018-01-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/781/)
- **razor max hits comparison**: Razor macros lack a comparison against max hits, so a fixed 'hits < 100' condition works but breaks when your maximum hit points change from effects such as weaken or curse. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-30–2018-11-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5708/)
- **razor target random hotkeys**: Razor's 'Target Random' hotkeys, such as target random grey monster, work on Outlands and can be used in macros. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-02-17–2019-02-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9984/)
- **toggle buff icon window macro**: The ToggleBuffIconWindow macro is created in the client options, not in UOSteam or Razor. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-10-28–2018-10-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3814/)
- **uo journal converter client launching**: UO Journal Converter cannot be used to start the UO client because it was designed to work with UO Assist rather than UOSteam. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-03-28–2019-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11998/)
- **uo steam self-bandage macro**: In UO Steam, a macro can bandage yourself only when injured by using the condition 'if hits < maxhits'. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-29–2018-11-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5629/)
- **uos script arithmetic limits**: UOS scripts do not support arithmetic expressions such as 'maxweight - 10' inside an if condition. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-11–2018-12-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6754/)
- **uosteam macro pause command**: In a UOSteam macro, adding the line 'pause XXXX' inserts a wait, where XXXX is the delay in milliseconds. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-10-29–2018-10-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3852/)

## Economy

- **arena reward clothing and battle scars tattoos**: New clothing items and unique Battle Scars tattoos are purchasable with Arena Reward points, and the tattoos come in Deed form so they can be given to other players. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **faction themed clothing**: Over 40 new Faction-themed clothing items for Andaria, Cambria, Prevalia and Terran are purchasable with Faction Silver, and players can mix pieces and wear them without being in that faction. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **hue shop standard hues**: Standard hues in the Hue Shop, such as Rich Red (1141), Forest Green (1155), Royal Red (1157), Royal Purple (1158), Charcoal (1175), Bright Red (2118), Sand (2422), Coal (2410), Teal (1415) and Ranger Armor (1436), can be bought from the Prevalian Merchant with gold or Prevalia coins. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **house prices**: Houses on Outlands sell for far more than deed price, with 8x8 houses commonly going for roughly 1-2 million gold and prime or rare houses such as moongate houses costing much more, because demand exceeds supply. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2020-09-17–2024-05-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29507/)
- **inn room rental price**: Renting a small inn room on Outlands costs about 5k gold per week, and a large inn room costs about 10k gold per week. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2021-01-09–2021-06-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22286/)
- **runebook bless deed price**: A runebook bless deed costs 50k in-game gold from the Prevalia merchant and cannot be bought with Prevalia Coins. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 7 authors, 2018-11-19–2021-03-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21148/)
- **8x8 house resale prices**: Player-sold 8x8 houses, which cost about 50k as a deed, have risen from roughly 50k-300k in 2019 to several million gold by 2024, with very large houses reaching about 150 million. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2019-10-31–2024-06-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29800/)
- **making gold via pking versus pvm**: PvM farming is a far more profitable way to make gold than PKing, which costs opportunity income, though skilled PvPers can still earn by taking other players' loot. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-12-08–2024-05-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29367/)
- **monster drops and gold**: Monster gold drops are itemized on the Ultima Mapper utility, monsters drop many unidentified items, and low-tier monsters like skeletons drop only small amounts of gold. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-11-09–2019-03-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11104/)
- **discord price check channels**: The official Outlands Discord has active buy/sell and pricecheck channels where players can check item prices and find buyers, plus a #newplayer channel for quick answers to new player questions. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-14–2019-09-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14770/)
- **adventurer's rope vendors**: Adventurer's rope can be bought from player vendors, including a vendor house just north of the Outpost moongate and a vendor north of Cambria. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-10–2019-04-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12734/)
- **anti-pk gold reward**: Killing a red as an anti-PK pays a small gold reward, roughly 200 to 350 gold. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-26–2019-04-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12990/)
- **auction seller anonymity**: Sellers in the community auction are anonymous at all times, known only to the auctioneer, and their identity is not revealed without their permission. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-05-05–2024-02-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28938/)
- **bard gold farming income**: A bard, even with a vendor-bought lute, can farm roughly 5k to 10k gold per hour while still training skills. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-26–2021-03-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21173/)
- **blessing a runebook**: To bless a runebook, buy a runebook bless deed for 50k gold from the Prevalian Merchant on the south side of the Prev north bank, then use it on the runebook. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-15–2019-05-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13706/)
- **character development gold sinks**: Aspect gear, skill balls, skill scrolls and the cost of raising skills are the major gold sinks for character development on Outlands. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-02-22–2021-02-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20681/)
- **cheapest house price**: After the land expansion, the cheapest houses on the market cost about 3 million gold, and this was still reported in January 2026. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-08-30–2026-01-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32735/)
- **community auction commission**: The community auction house charges sellers a 10% commission on the final sale price, with a cap that was reported as 50k and later as 75k. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-02-11–2024-02-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28938/)
- **converting gold to bank checks**: To turn gold into a check, say 'check <amount>' (for example 'check 10000') to a banker or with your bank box open, which makes moving large sums safer. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-02–2022-02-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24505/)
- **crafting profitability**: Crafting for sale is generally not very profitable because players often sell crafted items near cost and many crafters farm their own materials, so gathering and selling materials is a better way to earn money when starting out. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-18–2019-07-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14384/)
- **dungeon-free gold income**: Players report earning 1.5m+ gold per week without entering dungeons, while another player reports earning about 5 million gold solo farming dungeons versus roughly 50k in gear lost to PKs over three weeks. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2025-02-24–2026-06-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33291/)
- **echo creation cost**: Creating an Echo costs one of the following: 1,000,000 gold, 500,000 doubloons, 10 Mastercrafting Diagrams, 75 Aspect Cores, 50 Aspect Distillation, 100 Skill Mastery Scrolls, or 500 Arcane Scrolls. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-29–2019-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13651/)
- **echoes versus multiple characters**: A single character can unlock multiple echoes; echoes are generally more convenient than creating multiple characters, but their increasing cost makes several echoes (3 or more) a much larger gold and item sink. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-01-13–2021-11-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/23521/)
- **gold farming rate per hour**: A good farm on Outlands nets roughly 20-40k+ gold per hour, and a well-developed character can reach roughly 50-100k gold per hour. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-20–2021-07-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22369/)
- **gold from picking dungeon chests**: Picking locked dungeon chests is a reliable gold source: newbie dungeon chests hold about 50 gold each and are plentiful, and level 5 and 6 chests can yield about 6-8k gold per dungeon run. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-05-30–2018-12-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7278/)
- **leather sale price**: Leather sells to other players for roughly 2 to 3 gold each, and can be traded through the Discord #buy-sell-trade channel. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-22–2019-01-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8744/)
- **mastery chain links**: Mastery Chain Links are an end game reward obtainable with reward points from major Outlands systems such as Societies, Arenas, PvP Events, Achievements and Strangelands, and are highly coveted. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-04-06–2025-04-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31657/)
- **outlands market portal**: The Outlands Portal at portal.uooutlands.com lists items sold on all player vendors in Avadon, the Outlands world, and can be used to check prices. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2025-04-25–2025-05-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31816/)
- **player vendor search via portal**: To find items on player vendors, search on the official Outlands Portal (portal.uooutlands.com), which returns the location of the vendor listing the item. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2025-07-02–2025-07-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32047/)
- **pvm farming gold income**: Top-end PvM farmers and end-game tamer loot in dungeons earn far more gold per 10-15 minutes (roughly 25-35k) than hours of low-level resource farming. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-12-19–2026-09-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33753/)
- **pvp event rewards**: Outlands PvP events and contested bosses mainly reward cosmetics (boss rares, seed-filled loot soldiers, funky horses), PvM-only items (links), or normal loot, which players argue does not attract PvPers. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-28–2022-03-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24655/)
- **selling items to players**: To sell items such as boards to other players, post an advertisement in the 'sell' channel of the official Ultima Online: Outlands Discord, where each player can make one large post per day. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-13–2019-04-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12199/)
- **skill orb value**: Skill orbs are very valuable and costly, worth roughly 20-30k each. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-23–2019-01-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8715/)
- **spellbook cost**: Full spellbooks are expensive in Outlands mainly because 7th and 8th circle spells require arcane scrolls, which were around 1k each as of 2021. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-11-05–2021-04-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21466/)
- **stealing outside towns**: Stealing outside of towns on Outlands yields plenty of good loot that sells to other players, though a thief may need a revised build to do it. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-31–2019-01-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7757/)
- **taming as money making**: Taming is regarded as the quickest and easiest way to make money on Outlands. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-10-26–2020-08-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18676/)
- **treasure map chest loot**: Treasure map chests can contain gold, magic items, skill balls, Arcane Scrolls, arrows, gems such as Citrine, Silver Trap Wire, Command Aspect Cores, +1 skill cap scrolls, a Simple Eminent Spellbook and armor or weapons; loot varies with map level. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-08-11–2019-08-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14543/)
- **vendor search**: Outlands provides a vendor search feature, including a web item search page, for looking up items listed on player vendors, though the web page may lag so items can already be sold. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-12-31–2026-01-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32757/)
- **water aspect cost**: Water Aspect is cheap to build, costing far less than Fortune or Command aspects (roughly 1/4 of Command's cost). _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-08-08–2021-01-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20164/)
- **adventurers society dungeon lockpicking income**: The Adventurers Society lets players stealth around and pick dungeon locks unnoticed by reds, earning roughly 20-30k gold per hour while carrying little loot. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-08-11–2020-08-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18604/)
- **arcane essence economic role**: Arcane Essence was originally implemented to transfer wealth from high-tier characters to low-tier characters, with newer players selling essence to wealthier players. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-10-01–2024-10-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30403/)
- **crafted item demand**: As the shard matures, many players keep multiple characters (crafter, PvP, PWM) and craft items themselves, which reduces demand for purchased goods. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-04-24–2019-04-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12908/)
- **galleon registration cost**: The highest-tier ship, the galleon, costs 1 million doubloons to register. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-03-03–2019-03-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10988/)
- **gold sinks**: Outlands has gold sinks including the customization store and a house upgrade option, though players are unsure what other sinks exist. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-11-01–2021-01-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20059/)
- **monster hunter society paragon quests**: Many Monster Hunter Society quests require killing a set number of paragons of a given monster, but they pay only about 2 or 3 coins more than quests such as killing mountain harpies. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-13–2018-11-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4469/)
- **player-driven economy and solo income**: The Outlands economy is player-driven, and solo play by non-tamer, non-summoner characters tends to yield poor income. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-11–2025-03-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31579/)
- **scroll recycling**: Scrolls can be recycled to save gold. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-09-29–2020-09-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19089/)
- **treasure map prices**: Treasure maps can be bought from players for roughly 3k gold per map level if the buyer is patient, although some sellers ask more, such as 8k for a level 2 map. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-02-25–2019-02-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10452/)
- **valuing looted items**: To judge a looted item's worth, put it in your bank, search the UO Outlands Discord for its buy/sell price, then decide for yourself on risk versus reward. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2021-06-28–2021-06-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22326/)

## Housing & storage

- **custom guild island requirements**: Custom Guild Islands must include a Custom Guildhouse. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-03–2025-11-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32460/)
- **custom guildhouse and island wait time**: The wait time for custom guildhouses and islands is six to twelve months or longer, and more complex requests take longer. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-03–2025-11-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32460/)
- **custom guildhouse size and height limits**: A custom guildhouse or island has a maximum size of 30x30 in a square, which cannot be exceeded, and a custom guildhouse has a maximum height of 4 livable stories. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-03–2025-11-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32460/)
- **custom guildhouse tilesets**: Custom guildhouses may use any existing house tileset that is already used by other placeable houses. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-03–2025-11-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32460/)
- **expansion housing and map scope**: The Tidal Tomb Expansion Patch does not add housing space or expand the overworld map. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-01–2025-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32437/)
- **faction house decorations**: Many unique faction-themed House Decorations are purchasable with Faction Silver. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **houseboats as guild prestige rewards**: New houseboats are no longer approved as Guild Prestige custom rewards, though existing houseboats are grandfathered. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-03–2025-11-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32460/)
- **houses on custom guild islands**: Regular houses can no longer be placed on Custom Guild Islands, so a guild must relocate its existing house or forgo the island reward; an existing Custom Guildhouse on land can be moved to the guild's custom island, though staff recommend completing the island and guildhouse together as one project. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-03–2025-11-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32460/)
- **island and house modification priority**: Island and house modifications are not currently a staff priority; staff are first working through the backlog of guilds awaiting their initial reward deliveries before follow-up revisions or modifications. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-05-28–2026-05-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33181/)
- **island customization classification**: Docks, custom foliage, arenas, ponds, waterfalls and similar elements are all classified as island customizations. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-03–2025-11-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32460/)
- **large land expansion**: A large land expansion is planned that introduces new Cities, Points of Interest, Exploration and Farming Zones, with thousands of potential Housing locations across multiple new biomes. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **mah house ownership restriction**: Additional Multiple Account Household accounts cannot own a standard House or Houseboat, or take part in House IDOC Lotteries. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-09-15–2025-09-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32235/)
- **multiple account household co-ownership and rental rooms**: Additional Multiple Account Household accounts can rent a Rental Room, co-own the primary player's house, and adventure alongside the primary player. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-09-15–2025-09-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32235/)
- **tile hues in custom guildhouses and islands**: Tiles in custom guildhouses and islands cannot be hued, due to a technical limitation of the game. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-03–2025-11-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32460/)
- **house decay and idoc**: A house decays and goes IDOC (in danger of collapse) if it is not refreshed by logging in and accessing its door, after which other players can claim it, for example through an auction, and items left inside can be lost. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2019-12-29–2023-06-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/27643/)
- **house idoc decay timer**: Outlands houses go IDOC (in danger of collapsing) if not refreshed within the owner IDOC timer, which staff can adjust; it was 45 days in 2018, was shortened to 30 days in April 2020, and is described as roughly 28-30 days (possibly up to 60) in later reports, after which the owner loses the house and its items. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 10 authors, 2018-11-30–2025-05-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31770/)
- **house limit per account**: Outlands allows one house per account and up to three accounts per player, so a player can own at most three houses. Players caught exceeding the limit are banned, and the limits are audited. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 10 authors, 2017-05-02–2024-06-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29729/)
- **house limit per ip**: Outlands allows up to 3 houses per IP address, and players can circumvent the limit with VPNs because staff say IP and account-group tracking is unreliable. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 9 authors, 2021-01-14–2021-05-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21705/)
- **house placement availability**: Free land for placing houses on Outlands is scarce, especially for large houses, which makes it very hard for new players to obtain one, partly because uneven terrain and trees block otherwise usable spots. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2019-02-17–2022-08-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25793/)
- **house refresh by friends and co-owners**: Friends and co-owners, not just the owner, can refresh a house to prevent decay. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2019-10-31–2021-01-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20096/)
- **house sizes and types**: Outlands offers houses from small wagons, caravans and 8x8 plots up to very large ones such as 21x21, 30x30 and castles, and larger houses can be placed over the space of smaller ones. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2021-02-17–2024-06-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29743/)
- **inn room rental system**: Outlands has an inn room rental system offering instanced rooms players can decorate and use for storage as an affordable alternative or stepping stone to owning an overworld house, which is scarce, though a rented room is usable only by the renting character. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 10 authors, 2020-09-18–2024-12-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30894/)
- **small house deed price**: A deed for a small 8x8 house on Outlands costs 50k gold, but the market price of a built 8x8 house with its plot is much higher because the plot location drives the price. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2019-11-01–2021-06-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22301/)
- **house deed resale**: Selling a house deed back to the vendor returns about 90% of its value, so a 50k deed returns 45k and moving houses costs about 10%. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2021-01-24–2025-10-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32393/)
- **house refresh and idoc decay**: Outlands houses must be refreshed roughly once every 30 days by logging in or opening the door, otherwise they decay and go IDOC (in danger of collapse). _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2021-01-18–2024-06-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29738/)
- **house taxes**: Outlands houses have no property tax or ownership fees; owners only need to refresh the house to prevent decay. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2020-04-03–2021-03-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20943/)
- **house decay refresh**: Houses on Outlands decay if the owner does not log in to refresh them, so owners must log into their house periodically (for example monthly) to prevent decay. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2021-01-08–2021-05-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21695/)
- **house deed blessing duration**: A house deed is blessed for one hour after purchase; after that it becomes unblessed and can be lost on death, so carrying it longer is risky. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2020-02-21–2021-03-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21280/)
- **house placement placeholders and lotteries**: Players commonly use small 50k houses as placeholders while saving for larger ones, and lotteries for expansion house placements are weighted towards accounts that do not already own houses. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-10-31–2024-06-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29799/)
- **house refresh rules**: Only a house's owner can refresh it; co-owners, friends and guild officers cannot, and staff had no plans to change this, though inactive owners can currently refresh empty houses at no cost. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2020-04-03–2024-06-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29752/)
- **inn room sharing**: Rented inn rooms on Outlands cannot have co-owners or friends, so characters on different accounts cannot share a rented room. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2021-02-22–2021-02-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20764/)
- **inn room sharing**: Inn room owners can invite their other characters, or other players, into their Inn room, and two players can share one room. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2021-06-12–2021-07-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22370/)
- **real estate npc house listings**: Real estate NPCs have a 'Houses for sale' gump that lists houses currently for sale and provides a map to find them. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-10-31–2020-03-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16679/)
- **buying a house from its sign**: When buying a house from its sign, you can leave your gold or checks in the bank, which keeps them safe from being lost. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-11-04–2022-02-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24505/)
- **giving guild members house access**: Buy a guildstone from the provisioner for 10,000gp and place it in the house, then associate the house as a guildhouse so all guild members can have co-owner access without being limited by the co-owner list. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-17–2019-02-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9292/)
- **house collapse item drops**: When a house collapses from IDOC, all items inside drop to the ground and can be looted by any player until they disappear. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-30–2021-01-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20102/)
- **house collapse warning**: When a house is in danger of collapsing on Outlands, a system message announces that 'a house is in danger of collapsing' to all players. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-08-06–2022-08-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25703/)
- **house deed theft**: House deeds are not blessed and can be stolen, so players should keep them secured. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-09-29–2018-12-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6740/)
- **house door access and unlocking**: To set door access, unlock the door and say "I wish to secure this" on it to choose who may use it; to unlock a locked door, double-click the gold key in your backpack and select the doors. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-30–2020-04-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17292/)
- **house placement tool**: Outlands house placement uses an architect-style placement tool that is mainly for previewing whether a chosen house design will fit the selected location. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-09-29–2019-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15434/)
- **house placement tool**: The House Placement Tool is inexpensive and is purchased from an NPC Architect near the Broker, reportedly for about 500 gold. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-09-29–2020-03-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16679/)
- **housing availability**: Housing spots on Outlands open up regularly as players leave, and large areas suitable for building still exist. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-15–2019-05-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13504/)
- **housing lottery odds**: The Outlands housing lottery reportedly favors players who own no homes, so players who have never owned a home have better chances. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-04-16–2024-08-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30290/)
- **idoc houses and announcements**: Houses in danger of collapse are called IDOCs; they are announced globally in-game and posted in the Discord channel #livefeed-realestate, and large house IDOCs become battlegrounds as collapse time approaches. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-03-01–2024-04-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29202/)
- **rented room appearance**: A rented room on Outlands is surrounded by pitch black with no scenery around it. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-02-25–2021-02-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20764/)
- **resurrection in houses**: In Outlands, a ghost who is not on a house's friends list cannot be resurrected inside that house. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-18–2019-03-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11685/)
- **trading items between own characters without a house**: To move items between your own characters without a house or guild, either log in two game clients on a second account and trade directly between the characters, or drop the items in a bag in an out-of-the-way spot, log out, and pick it up with the other character, though the bag method is unsafe because other players can steal it. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-13–2019-03-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11489/)
- **bank crowding**: Banks are less crowded than early in the shard's life because players now have houses to stand in instead of waiting by the banks. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-01-27–2019-01-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8896/)
- **blocked house placement tiles**: Some tiles near existing houses appear placeable but are blocked, and blocker tiles prevent house placement in several spots. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-10-31–2019-10-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15421/)
- **crafting yard tile in houses**: Some Outlands houses include a CY (crafting yard) tile, which players consider a major benefit and which makes houses with a larger CY more desirable. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-06-21–2024-06-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29724/)
- **custom housing decoration**: Outlands has a custom housing selection, and gold can be spent on house decoration items from vendors. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-08-24–2022-08-24)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25847/)
- **dockmaster houses near water**: Some Outlands houses near water contain a dockmaster, and boaters often keep a second house for that dockmaster. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-06-21–2024-06-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29720/)
- **emptied house secures**: If house secures are found emptied, ask the Outlands devs to check the IP addresses that have logged into the account, to determine whether the account was hacked. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-02-28–2019-02-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10776/)
- **finding a house spot before buying a deed**: Before buying a house deed, run around with a house placement tool to find a placeable spot, and only then buy the deed and place the house. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-02-21–2020-02-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16605/)
- **guild house ownership and design**: Guild buildings on Outlands are owned by an individual player rather than by the guild itself, and the claim that some guilds may design their own houses is unconfirmed. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-08-13–2024-08-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30214/)
- **house access for non-friends**: A player cannot access another player's house until they are added as an actual friend of the house, unless the house is associated with a guild via a guildstone. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-17–2018-12-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7219/)
- **house land expansion**: Outlands staff have released additional house land over time, most recently about 4000 new house placements in a 2024 expansion, superseding the earlier small additions. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2021-06-25–2024-06-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29746/)
- **house looting mitigation**: Outlands staff mitigated house looting because it drives players away, and items stored in house secures are protected. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-11–2018-12-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6824/)
- **house trade risk**: A house sale cannot be fully completed within the trade window, so part of the deal relies on the other party following through after the transfer. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2023-10-23–2023-10-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28371/)
- **land claim lottery at expansion launch**: When the Outlands land expansion went live, new land was distributed through a land claim lottery and a land grab day, though details of how it worked are anecdotal. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-06-21–2024-06-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29722/)
- **placeable house spot locations**: Placeable house spots are mostly far from towns and off the beaten path, since areas near towns have the most traffic and few open spots. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-02-21–2020-02-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16606/)
- **player housing and collecting**: Players can buy large homes and decorate them, and can collect rares. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2023-02-09–2023-02-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/27001/)
- **rental room co-owners**: Characters from other accounts cannot currently be added as co-owners of a rental room, so a rental room cannot be shared between a player's accounts. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2021-03-09–2021-03-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21018/)
- **weekly idoc houses**: Houses go IDOC weekly and get sold off, providing a source of houses for purchase. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2021-06-16–2021-06-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22148/)

## Travel & locations

- **outpost area redesign**: The area around Outpost will be redesigned and expanded with new Points of Interest and farming areas, and no housing will be allowed there. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **prevalia sewer sanctuary dungeon**: Prevalia Sewer is being introduced as a new Sanctuary Dungeon. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **sanctuary dungeon rotation**: The Sanctuary is a main dungeon that rotates weekly, so which dungeon is the safe one changes each week. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2024-06-01–2024-11-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30706/)
- **harpy locations**: Harpies can be found in the mountains northwest of Horseshoe Bay, at the Hollow Hills (listed in the in-game Atlas), at Mount Petram, and as random overworld spawns, usually in the plains. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2019-01-02–2019-01-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7782/)
- **recall out of inn rooms**: Players can recall out of their Inn room in Outlands, following a patch that added recall and hike out of Inn rooms. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2021-06-14–2021-06-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22264/)
- **world map**: Outlands uses a fully custom-built world map; the classic UO cities do not exist and all locations are new. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2017-04-17–2019-12-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15826/)
- **boss and mini boss room locations**: Boss and mini-boss locations, including boss rooms, are marked in the Outlands UO Automap marked-locations files, and mini boss room locations can also usually be found on the Outlands wiki. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2021-01-08–2025-11-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32531/)
- **dungeon escape gates**: Dungeons contain escape gates that players can use to flee from reds, so players should note their locations and plan an escape route. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-05–2019-03-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12051/)
- **ossuary dungeon location**: The Ossuary dungeon is in the desert west of Cambria, in the southern part of the desert among sandstone township ruins, and is a good starting dungeon for gold farming. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-14–2025-01-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31056/)
- **rune library locations**: Rune libraries are located near moongates, including Outpost, Terran, Cambria and Prevelia, and can be used to get runes for recall travel. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-07-26–2026-05-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33193/)
- **runebook recall magery requirement**: A runebook loaded with recall scrolls can be used to recall with 0 Magery and 0 Camping skill, so neither is required for fast travel. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-11-19–2021-03-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21148/)
- **ultima mapper and custom map markers**: To see Outlands markers in Ultima Mapper, open the Markers menu, click Select, then tick each marker type or Select All; for custom marker XML packs in the game client, extract them into the Outlands Ultima Online\ClassicUO\Data\Client folder, load the game, right-click the world map, choose Map Marker Options and select the pack. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-05-14–2022-06-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25331/)
- **black tiles on the map**: Black unused tiles on the Outlands map can be caused by missing client art files, though a few minor map glitches also exist and are being fixed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-08-16–2018-08-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1910/)
- **camping skill teleport**: The Camping skill lets a character teleport to dungeons, and 80 Camping is the goal for that ability. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-10–2025-01-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31056/)
- **dungeon escape gates below floor one**: Escape gates on dungeon floors below the first are red gates that can only be used to escape, not to recall into or out of, while most level 1 areas (except Shadowspire and Kraul) have only yellow gates near the entrance. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-26–2024-07-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30091/)
- **dungeon exit gates**: Dungeons contain exit gates at various spots that let players escape quickly and return to a town. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-27–2020-02-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16587/)
- **dungeon exit gates**: Dungeons have exit gates that take players back to town from deep levels, so players do not need to camp to return to town. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-13–2021-06-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22111/)
- **dungeon moongate escape**: Dungeons have moongates that can be used to escape back to town, for example to flee player killers. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-14–2019-11-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15542/)
- **khal draco rune library location**: Khal Draco's rune library is outside the south gate heading from East Prevalia, within running distance of South Prevalia, which has about 3 mage shops nearby. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-06-24–2024-02-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28965/)
- **level one dungeon escape gates**: Most level one dungeon maps have several moongates that let a player escape quickly if needed. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-07-16–2025-11-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32492/)
- **mapping programs**: UOMapper, UO Automap, UO Cartographer and Ultima Mapper are third-party mapping programs that work with Ultima Online: Outlands. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-30–2021-06-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22036/)
- **newbie dungeon level 1 rear spawns**: The rear and far north of Newbie Dungeon level 1 hold ettins, cave bears, trolls and ogres, with a single drake spawn. They are better to fight than mongbats and zombies once skills allow, but wandering ettins, trolls and ogres are a threat there. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-25–2020-03-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16682/)
- **ossuary 4 dungeon**: Ossuary 4 is a dungeon area in Outlands that contains treasure chests and is used by players for farming. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-03-28–2022-03-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24668/)
- **ossuary level 3 hidden burial room**: To find the hidden burial room on Ossuary Level 3, go to the room with the ship laden with gold and treasures (also black cats and fire priestesses), check the north-facing protrusion of the west wall, and hug the western wall to reveal a hidden staircase going down. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-20–2019-02-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10152/)
- **outlands map size**: The Outlands map is roughly half the size of the original UO map, which brings points of interest closer together, while still offering nearly as much housing room as the original. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-12-09–2021-06-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22271/)
- **prevalia merchant location**: The Prevalia Merchant is in North Prevalia, in front of the caravan by the Prevalia bank. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-19–2024-02-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28965/)
- **prevalia zoo location**: The Prevalia zoo is located by Prevalia's eastern exit/entrance, far east of the main bank. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-19–2020-05-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17457/)
- **recall out of rented rooms**: Players cannot recall out of a rented inn room on Outlands and can only gate out. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-02-25–2021-02-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20764/)
- **recall without magery skill**: Recalling with a runebook or scrolls works even with 0 Magery skill on Outlands. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-01-16–2021-06-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22311/)
- **rune libraries**: Rune libraries with runes and runebooks to Outlands zones are available near moongates, including the vendor house north of the Outpost moongate and one by the Terran moongate. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-10–2020-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19433/)
- **runebook blessing and storage**: A runebook can be blessed using a prior coin donation, or an unblessed runebook can be kept in the bank for traveling to dungeons. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-01-16–2021-06-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22111/)
- **runebook recall charges**: Recalling via a runebook using recall scroll charges never fizzles, and a runebook holds a maximum of 10 recall charges that must be refilled with recall scrolls. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-19–2018-11-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4709/)
- **runetome capacity**: A runetome is a large runebook that holds 26 runes; one author believes it also holds 50 recall and 50 gate scrolls. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-02–2019-01-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8413/)
- **third-party character trackers**: As of September 2026, third-party trackers such as Ultima Mapper and exploreoutlands.com can no longer track a player's character on Outlands. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2026-09-22–2026-09-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33803/)
- **ultima mapper**: Ultima Mapper shows where mobs live and how much gold they drop. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-05–2019-03-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11107/)
- **ultima mapper outlands setup**: To use Ultima Mapper for Outlands, install the program, then download and extract the Outlands-specific files (map and marker files) from the Outlands mapper GitHub page into a folder, set File > Settings > Resource Path to that folder, and restart the program. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-05-14–2019-05-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13711/)
- **viewing outlands map in mapper**: To see the Outlands map in Ultima Mapper or UO Cartographer, change the current facet to Felucca (View > Current Facet > Felucca, or right-click the map), and close the program so it remembers the facet. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-05-14–2019-04-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12510/)
- **arena access**: The Arena can be reached by using any public moongate. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2021-04-29–2021-04-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21606/)
- **classicuo world map icons**: To show icons instead of text on the ClassicUO world map, unpack the MapIcons files into the MapIcons folder inside ClassicUO\Data\Client\. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-05-28–2020-05-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17850/)
- **exploreoutlands tmap map finder**: The exploreoutlands.com tmap map finder (decode map assistant) is still buggy and in a beta state, and its owner has not updated it for the new land locations. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2021-10-13–2021-10-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/23123/)
- **faction dungeons**: The Outlands faction dungeons are based on classic Ultima Online dungeons. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-11-25–2024-11-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30795/)
- **gold coast tavern policy**: The Gold Coast Tavern welcomes both red and blue players, but hostile actions are not permitted inside. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-01-04–2019-01-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7849/)
- **inferno bridge hazard**: The Inferno bridge can kill players who do not know its mechanics, because stepping on dangerous tiles on the bridge burns and damages the player. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-04-10–2019-04-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12448/)
- **norse village mining area red presence**: The area of the Norse village east of Andaria and west of the isle of Outpost is a mining area where red players are sometimes present, most commonly at the entrance to Cavernam. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-03-03–2020-03-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16698/)
- **rune library rune book**: The rune book at the rune library house does not allow using its recall charges, so players should cast Recall with reagents instead. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-06-09–2019-06-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13846/)
- **rune marking near dungeon escape gates**: In Outlands dungeons, players can mark runes only to a spot within 8 tiles of the yellow escape gates, which are located only on the first floor of a dungeon. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-25–2018-11-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5098/)
- **sanctuary dungeon introduction**: The Sanctuary dungeon was introduced around May 2024 as a safe environment for players who want to avoid PKs. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-05-17–2024-05-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29454/)
- **shelter dungeon**: Shelter is a dungeon in Outlands where players can farm without worrying about PvP encounters. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-11-21–2022-11-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26544/)

## Systems & mechanics

- **aetheric purity event**: The Aetheric Purity Event runs from Saturday, November 8, 2025 11AM EST to Saturday, November 15, 2025 11AM EST, and players can participate to uncover the secrets that unlock the Tidal Tomb. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-01–2025-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32437/)
- **animal form duration and cooldown**: An Animal Form lasts 30 seconds unless ended early, after which the player cannot adopt another Animal Form for 5 seconds. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **animal form scaling**: All Bonuses, Effects, and Abilities from an Animal Form are scaled by the player's effective Druidism skill divided by 100%. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **animal forms**: There are 3 Melee Animal Forms (Goldtusk, Scumbug, Slamphibian) and 1 Spellcasting Animal Form (Spell Purger). _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **arena black reward hue**: Arena Black (hue 2056) is a reward hue obtained with Arena Points from the Arena System. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **cancel loot button**: While any items are queued for looting, the Loot All button turns into a "Cancel Loot" button that removes all items inside the corpse from the queue. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **creating a multiple account household**: To create a new Multiple Account Household, open a ticket in the Outlands Discord support channel and say who in your household wants to join. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-09-15–2025-09-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32235/)
- **creation aspect**: A Creation Aspect may potentially be introduced in the future to synergize with Geomancy, but it is not confirmed. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **de-queueing a queued loot item**: To remove an item from the loot queue, click the item that is queued for looting and it will be de-queued. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **druid animal form controls**: Players change into Animal Forms and activate Form Abilities using buttons on a Druid Hotbar or individual [DruidCommands. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **druid symbol resource**: Players using Druidism generate a Druid Symbol (shown as a Leaf Graphic) every 5 seconds; adopting an Animal Form consumes 1 Druid Symbol and Form Abilities consume 1 to 3. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **evolved creature field research**: To research an [Evolved] creature, a player uses a Field Notes item, which is craftable with Inscription and consumed on each use; no skill is required, and up to 3 players can research a creature. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **faction mount gear**: New Faction Mount Gear is being introduced, including the Checkered Caparison and the Plumed Champron. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **faction shield covers**: Four new Faction-themed Shield Covers stay in the backpack and make any equipped Shield adopt the cover's appearance until the Shield is removed. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **golem construction menu buttons**: In the Toolbox Menu, click the large diamond button below a Golem type to attempt to construct it (it is greyed out if skill requirements are unmet), and click the small orb button above it to open the Golem Upgrade Menu. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **golem construction requirements**: To construct a Golem, the player needs sufficient Geomancy and Secondary skill, no active Recovery Cooldown, and at least one Gemshard in their backpack. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **golem upgrades**: Each Golem type has 6 Upgrades of which the player can select 3, and Upgrades can only be changed while that Golem type is not currently summoned. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **grid loot gump loot all button**: The Grid Loot Gump has a "Loot All" button that queues all items on the corpse to be looted into your designated grab bag. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **guild prestige reward queue**: Pending Guild Prestige rewards are tracked in a queue with separate lists for Islands & Houses, Island Modifications/Guildhouse Revisions, Statues, and Banners, and queue position is based only on when the reward was purchased (when the button was pressed, from a log file). _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-25–2025-11-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32600/)
- **item and bonus use in animal form**: In Animal Form players can use items as normal, and bonuses from Codexes, Aspects, Mastery Chains, Redline and armor, weapon and spellbook properties still apply unless otherwise specified. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **item sifting while snooping**: Item sifting while snooping can only be done manually with the mouse using drag and drop, and cannot be done by macro. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33349/)
- **loot all extra delay**: Looting items with the Loot All button applies a small extra delay compared with looting them manually. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **loot all queue order**: Items queued with the Loot All button are queued in a random order and display a spinning animation around them while queued. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **mah society jobs restriction**: Additional Multiple Account Household accounts cannot accept or participate in Society Jobs. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-09-15–2025-09-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32235/)
- **mah ticket prerequisites**: Before opening an MAH ticket, the new user must have the game installed and updated, be a member of the Outlands Discord channel, and be online and available. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-09-15–2025-09-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32235/)
- **migrating shared outlandsid accounts to multiple account household**: Players who previously shared a single OutlandsID due to old regulations can open a Multiple Account Household support ticket to have those accounts migrated into their own OutlandsID under the MAH restrictions. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-09-15–2025-09-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32235/)
- **monster rumble card packs**: Players earn Monster Rumble Card Packs through normal gameplay, bound to their OutlandsID; each pack holds random Creature Cards from the current or previous Rank, and the player picks one to keep. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **monster rumble creature card upgrade levels**: Players can obtain duplicate Creature Cards from Monster Rumble Card Packs, and each additional copy raises the card's Upgrade Level (sparkling card icon), granting the creature bonus Damage Dealt and increased Hit Points. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **monster rumble higher ladder difficulty**: Each subsequent Monster Rumble Ladder reuses the Creature Cards from the previous Ladder's ranks, but enemy creatures gain additional combat bonuses and the Cooldowns on a player's Actions become progressively longer. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **monster rumble ladders and ranks**: Monster Rumble is a single-player card and monster-battle game with four Difficulty Ladders (Bronze, Silver, Gold, Crimson) of 45 Ranks each, each Rank featuring 10 Creature Cards; players start in Bronze and advance to the next Ladder after completing all 45 Ranks. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **monster rumble match battles**: Monster Rumble Matches are Lane-based battles between the player's creatures and random enemy cards from the next Rank, using in-game Stats, Skills and Abilities; players cannot command creatures directly but can use Actions such as Lane Swap, Pacify, Heal, Evolve, or Paragon. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **monster rumble reward points**: Completing Monster Rumble Ranks and entire Ladders earns Reward Points, which can be spent on rewards such as custom Paperdoll Trophies, unique Rare Cloth, Custom Clothing (including Monster Masks), and Achievements. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **multiple account household abuse penalty**: Abusing the Multiple Account Household system results in loss of the extra accounts and a permanent ban for the whole household. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-09-15–2025-09-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32235/)
- **multiple account household eligibility**: Multiple Account Households are intended for real household members such as a spouse, a child over the age of 13, or a roommate. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-09-15–2025-09-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32235/)
- **multiple account households policy**: Outlands currently allows Multiple Account Households (MAH) for people in the same household on separate accounts, and additional MAH accounts can be banned for violating the MAH policy while the primary account is not banned. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-09-15–2026-02-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32235/)
- **multiple simultaneous accounts**: To play multiple accounts at once with the OutlandsID setup, open another client window as before and log in with your OutlandsID. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2022-12-06–2022-12-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26613/)
- **new lycaeum reward hue**: The New Lycaeum hue (1095) is obtained from New Lycaeum quest rewards. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **non-repeatable quests**: A non-repeatable Quest can only be completed a single time per OutlandsID. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **ocean updates**: The expansion includes ocean updates covering Infamy handling, Spyglass improvements, and several new ocean mechanics and features. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-01–2025-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32437/)
- **open corpse button in grid loot**: A new Open Corpse button lets you add the corpse container from the Grid Loot gump even when the "Grid Loot only" option is enabled. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **outlandsid password recovery**: An OutlandsID provides instantaneous email-based password recovery. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2022-12-06–2022-12-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26602/)
- **possible nature aspect**: A Nature Aspect may potentially be introduced to synergize with Druidism, but this is not confirmed. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **prestige queue ordering inaccuracy**: Queue ordering for Guild Prestige rewards can be inaccurate if the player who made the Prestige purchase has since switched guilds. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-30–2025-11-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32600/)
- **quest list menu features**: From the Quest List menu, players can view individual Quest Pages, see Status and Expiration of Quests, toggle Task tracking, and click a button to view a Map and Coordinates to the Quest Issuer NPC. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **quest progress across outlandsid characters**: When a player accepts a Quest, all characters on their OutlandsID are considered to have accepted it and all contribute progress towards its Tasks. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **quest task tracking and notifications**: Players can track Quest Task progress similar to Society Jobs, and can receive optional popup menus and visual/sound indications when completing Tasks and when all Tasks are complete and ready to turn in. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **redline system expansion**: Outlands is looking at expanding the Redline system with more upgrades, such as buffs to Followers, Poison Damage, Trap Damage, Barding effects, Carry Capacity, and Ship Cannon Damage, aimed at builds like Follower-based builds that lack end-game leveling impact. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **redline system overhaul**: The Tidal Tomb Expansion overhauls the Redline System with new Upgrade Types, including upgrades for Taming. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-01–2025-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32437/)
- **repeatable quests**: Some Quests are repeatable and can be completed multiple times, usually after a cooldown following the last completion. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **seer quest rewards and library codex coin**: Seer Quests often originate from the Avadonian Library, and their reward is sometimes Library Codex Coin currency, which can be spent on Decoration, Clothing, Hues, or other rewards. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **seer quests**: Seer Quests are quests built by the Outlands Staff Seers (Erik and Atropa) and often feature the complexity and potential difficulty of Time Dungeon content. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **shard rules update for multiple account households**: The Outlands Shard Rules have been updated to reflect the Multiple Account Household changes. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-09-15–2025-09-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32235/)
- **society reward hues**: Society hues are bought with Society Reward Points: Monster Hunter (2817), Adventurer Lodge (1918), Tradesman Union (2765), Order of Armorers (2915), Seafarer League (2906), Artificer's Enclave (2875). _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **spell chain progression and scaling**: Casting the spell at the current step of the Spell Chain grants the listed Bonus/Effect and advances to the next step; the power of the bonus scales with Spellchaining skill and increases further along the chain. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
