- **spell chain randomization pool**: Players can pick 3 Spells and 3 Buff/Effects to populate the randomization pool at each step of the Spell Chain, and the same Spell or Buff/Effect cannot appear in the pool for consecutive steps. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **spell chain reset conditions**: A Spell Chain ends and a new random one is generated if the player casts an offensive spell that is not the current step, casts the 4th spell, or 10 seconds pass since the last offensive spell cast. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **spell chain structure**: A Spell Chain is a collection of 4 semi-randomized offensive spells in order, with the 2nd, 3rd, and 4th spells each having a Buff or Effect tied to them. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **staff password requests**: Outlands staff will never ask for your password when providing support. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2022-12-06–2022-12-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26609/)
- **starting a monster rumble match**: To start a Monster Rumble Match, assign 3 Creature Cards from the current or previous Rank to your team and consume a Monster Meal, which is earned through normal gameplay and bound to your OutlandsID. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **strangelands reward hue**: The Strangelands hue (2752) is obtained from Strangelands. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **thief system reward hue**: The Thief hue (1067) is obtained from Black Market Goods in the Thief System. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **thief's target variable**: Thief's target is a special last-target variable that exists only in the client, and only one thief's target can exist at a time. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2026-07-09–2026-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33345/)
- **town themed plants**: New Town-themed Plants may be growable using the Plants system, with seeds potentially added to Battle Commendations from PvP Events or Factions. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **viewing accepted quests**: To view a summary of all accepted Quests, click the Quests button in your Paperdoll or type [QuestList. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **account and character limits**: Each player is allowed 1 OutlandsID with up to 3 accounts of 5 characters each (15 characters total) per IP/household, plus 1 in-game house per account, and multi-boxing is permitted within these limits. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2018-01-12–2022-12-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26607/)
- **account limit**: Outlands allows 3 accounts per household (not strictly per person), each with 5 character slots to start, with up to six accounts possible after an expensive unlock. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 7 authors, 2018-10-02–2022-08-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25902/)
- **account limit per player**: Outlands rules allow each player up to 3 accounts; anyone found with more than 3 has all associated accounts banned. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2018-10-20–2025-07-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32060/)
- **detect hidden self reveal**: On Outlands, using Detect Hidden reveals the user's own character, an intentional staff change to prevent griefing and spamming of the skill. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2018-11-07–2021-09-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22895/)
- **lockpicking society jobs**: Weekly lockpicking society jobs vary, such as picking chests of any level, in specific dungeons like Petram and Pulma, or from treasure maps, and some require picking 10 level 7/8 chests, which many players find very hard because of respawns and competition. Picking enough high-level chests may also earn achievement points. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2019-02-22–2019-03-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11223/)
- **mounts in dungeons**: Mounts can be ridden anywhere overland on Outlands but not in dungeons; every dungeon is unmounted, and entering one safely dismounts you and the mount returns when you exit. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2017-10-18–2019-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8216/)
- **player killer penalties**: Reds (murderers) on Outlands face penalties on death: a gold fine based on kill count, a roughly two-hour lockout from the region (each dungeon counts as its own region) and aggression timers. Killers get a share of the penalty, and counts decay while logged in. Some players find the penalties too light. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 7 authors, 2018-08-18–2025-10-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32307/)
- **recall in dungeons**: Recall cannot be used into or inside dungeons on Outlands, so players cannot instantly escape PKs and must use escape gates instead. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 6 authors, 2018-10-27–2019-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11997/)
- **sanctuary dungeon rewards**: The Sanctuary dungeon gives reduced loot and XP/skill gains compared with normal dungeons (one estimate is about 50% less), in exchange for no PK risk. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2024-02-14–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31813/)
- **aspect gear and endgame progression**: Outlands offers endgame progression beyond GM or Legendary skills, including aspect gear, aspect masteries, and codices. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-01-22–2022-08-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25813/)
- **multiple account logins**: Only one character per account can be logged in at a time, so a player with up to three accounts can log in up to three characters simultaneously, with up to 15 characters in total. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2019-03-13–2025-03-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31552/)
- **murderer resurrection fee**: Murderers (reds) on Outlands pay a resurrection fee when they die, and the fee scales with their murder counts (including long term murder counts), though the exact formula is not stated. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-12-11–2019-02-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10466/)
- **red player resurrection fee**: Red (player-killer) characters must pay a gold resurrection fee, around 4.4k gold and over 10k for those with many kills. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-12-14–2022-01-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24253/)
- **resurrection penalties for reds with murder counts**: A red who dies loses their items and pays a gold resurrection fee, and as of the latest claim also faces a timeout of several hours before playing normally; earlier claims of count-scaled account lockdown were slated to change, with no stat or skill loss. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-12-11–2019-01-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8932/)
- **shard ruleset and era**: Outlands is a non-consensual PvP shard with no Trammel ruleset, originally billed as T2A-based and pre-AoS in style, though it has evolved beyond a pure T2A or pre-AoS ruleset. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2018-09-23–2024-08-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30211/)
- **trapped pouches**: Some NPC merchants sell trapped (exploding) pouches, which protect stored goods from thieves when passing through town moongates and can help escape players casting Paralyze. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2019-02-18–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31813/)
- **weekly sanctuary dungeon**: A Sanctuary dungeon is available each week where players can play without risk of PKs, with the tradeoff of reduced loot, gold and XP. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2023-07-03–2026-07-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33319/)
- **aspect experience**: Players earn Aspect experience by killing monsters while wearing aspect gear of that aspect, and the gear must still carry the aspect when the mob dies. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-01-25–2022-11-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26577/)
- **dungeon ip restriction**: Outlands enforces an IP lock on dungeons, allowing only one client per IP in a given dungeon, so multi-account households on the same IP cannot hunt in the same dungeon together. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-11-15–2022-08-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25902/)
- **echoes for multiple builds**: Players can unlock Echoes in Outlands, which let one character use multiple skill builds, typically after progressing toward end game PvM. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2021-04-07–2026-05-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33191/)
- **faction struggles**: Faction Struggles are one-hour, team-based, objective-oriented events between Order and Chaos that run three times a day (once per time zone), and players take part only during the events. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-08-18–2018-08-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2014/)
- **mastery chain**: The Mastery Chain is a necklace that binds to a character once it is equipped, and afterward it gives bonuses even when carried in the inventory. Monster kills give XP that unlocks link slots, links are bought and slotted with gold (millions in total), and fully unlocking a chain takes hundreds of hours. A character can hold multiple chains with different numbers of links unlocked. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-04-28–2023-02-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/27001/)
- **mountless faction struggles**: Some Outlands faction struggles are held mountless, with mounts not allowed, while others allow mounts. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-09-03–2019-04-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12778/)
- **multi-account pvp rules**: Switching between your characters mid-fight, multi-account PvP, and multiboxing two characters in a fight (including fighting back against a PK) all break the Outlands rules. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2024-04-22–2026-04-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32996/)
- **multiple characters same ip in dungeons**: Multiple characters from the same IP cannot be in dungeons together, since an IP block was added and the rules were changed to disallow running two characters together. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-06-28–2019-11-20)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15664/)
- **passive tracking and hiding**: Outlands has a custom passive tracking mode tied to the Tracking skill that checks every 5 seconds for hostile (gray/red/orange) players and warns the user, which with Detect Hidden makes stealth harder, and there is also a wait timer between using Hiding and Stealth. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-05-01–2020-09-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18932/)
- **pk looting on death**: When a player is killed by a PK, the killer can loot items such as armor, weapons, gold, loot, and other carried equipment, and the victim also loses fame. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-10-29–2026-03-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32956/)
- **pvm and pvp separation**: Outlands deliberately separates PvM and PvP mechanics because abilities, bonuses, Codex mechanics and Aspects are designed around PvM, so PvP builds are kept distinct and many players keep separate PvM and PvP characters. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-27–2026-09-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33754/)
- **pvm stealing and non-pvm gameplay**: Outlands has a PvM stealing system with a very low chance (about 0.001%) of stealing an antiquity from monsters plus a small commodity amount, and PvM players are already at risk from PKs and thieves; other activities include treasure mapping, gathering and PvP. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2022-03-09–2022-10-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26155/)
- **repentance for red players**: Red players can repent to reduce or remove long-term murder counts, which lowers the penalty and cost of being red, and some players think this undermines the goal of curbing reds. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-01-28–2019-02-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9827/)
- **runebook blessing and death loss**: Runebooks are not blessed by default and can be lost on death, so players should bless them with a bless deed. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-05-30–2021-05-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21696/)
- **shadow aspect**: The Shadow aspect is a tiered aspect that benefits stealth-based characters such as stealth archers, and is one of the cheapest aspects to unlock. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2020-04-14–2021-01-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20287/)
- **short-term murder counts from pets and dying**: Short-term murder counts can be gained by being reported after your pets kill a player who lost their criminal flag, and can be reduced by dying, for example by having friends kill you. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-12-12–2019-02-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9837/)
- **stats and skills**: Stats do not interact with skills, so dexterity does not affect steal or snoop success; STR, DEX and INT change only by working the skills tied to them, rising naturally through normal play. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-03-01–2020-11-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19741/)
- **teleport spell range**: On Outlands, the Teleport spell can be cast up to 10 tiles away, and it can cross fences and water. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-09-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2303/)
- **town struggles team balancing**: Town Struggles teams are currently balanced by guild, which can stack guilds on one team; staff plan to cap team sizes, run multiple simultaneous instances in different towns, and are considering ELO-based auto-balancing. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2022-01-10–2022-04-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24901/)
- **armor dex loss**: Wearing armor on Outlands, even full plate, causes no dex loss, which is intended design. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-08-06–2018-08-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1683/)
- **aspect item loss on death**: When a player dies, aspect items that were equipped or in the inventory lose their aspect properties and revert to their original form, except permanently blessed Legacy ones; dying to PvM content such as bosses, mobs, trapped chests or poison while making poison potions also causes the loss, with no activations returned. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-20–2019-11-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15743/)
- **aspect leveling**: Aspects are leveled with cores, extracts and distillations, each level costs more than the last, and they can be raised up to level 13. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-31–2021-03-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21271/)
- **aspect mastery progression**: Aspect level is a character-wide mastery shared across weapon, armor and spellbook for a given aspect; once an aspect is unlocked it is kept permanently and applies to all item types. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-20–2021-07-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22407/)
- **aspect system**: Aspect is the end game system in Outlands, letting a character temporarily enhance magic or crafted items while worn, and it strongly shapes how every build plays. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-12-10–2021-11-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/23520/)
- **aspect system and gold income**: The Aspect system offers many options and is rewarding if the player invests in unlocking and leveling it, and aspect choice, along with build, playstyle and aspect chain, matters more than class for gold earned. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-12-09–2021-11-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/23520/)
- **aspect unlock and activation**: A player must first unlock an aspect for the character (multiple aspect types can be unlocked), then activate it and apply it to an equipped weapon, armor or spellbook, which temporarily turns the item into an aspect item; activating on a weapon or spellbook requires holding it in hand. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-20–2019-11-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15743/)
- **bonded pet resurrection**: Bonded pets can be resurrected by a player with 80 Veterinary skill, a player able to cast Resurrection, or any healer, but they return with -25% abilities, stacking up to 100% if resurrected repeatedly within the timer. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-27–2019-02-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9807/)
- **book of grudges**: Each character has its own season-based Book of Grudges that tracks kills and deaths against other players and guilds (allowing KDR to be seen), with Show Guilds and Show Players views; books may be reset between seasons and are not viewable on the UOO website. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-03-26–2021-03-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/21255/)
- **ccc event schedule**: CCC, a mounted PvP event, runs every 4 hours at hard-scheduled times. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-11–2022-03-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24649/)
- **client ui resizing**: In the Ultima Online client, the window frame, bags and their items, journal, world map, macro buttons and counters can be resized, but not all UI elements can be, and healthbars currently cannot be resized. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-03-30–2024-04-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29279/)
- **criminal grey and murderer red status**: A player turns grey from negative actions against another player (looting a corpse, hitting a player or their tame, or the victim triggering evil items such as poisoned food or trapped chests), and turns red only by accumulating 5 Murder Counts, each given when a blue victim dies after being attacked. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-28–2018-10-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3821/)
- **death risk and loss**: Outlands has a harsh risk/reward balance with no Trammel facet, so players die often and a single mistake can cost them everything they carry and hours of work. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-07-21–2025-03-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31579/)
- **defensive wrestling**: Outlands has no defensive wrestling mechanic, so Anatomy and Evaluating Intelligence cannot substitute for Wrestling to resist interruption. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-10–2019-02-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9620/)
- **delectable food mana regeneration**: Delectable food gives an extra mana point every 6 seconds 25% of the time, rising to 37.5% with GM Taste Identification, and speeds mana gain both passively and actively, so food buffs should always be kept up. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-08–2019-11-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15646/)
- **disguise kits**: Disguise kits disguise a player's identity and can reduce suspicion or criminal recognition from stealing in the city, but they are expensive with limited uses. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-02-15–2024-05-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29550/)
- **dungeon gate escape after fighting a red**: A non-criminal player who attacks or fights back against a red in a dungeon is reportedly unable to escape through the dungeon gates. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-26–2019-12-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15767/)
- **eldritch mana refund cap**: Eldritch raises the mana refund cap by 2% plus 0.75% per tier (about 12.5% at tier 14), and reaching the cap requires a very accurate spellbook. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-10-15–2024-06-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29657/)
- **epic summoner achievements**: Epic summoner achievements must be completed on multiple characters; one character can earn Summoner, but a second character must complete 5 more spells to get Legendary Summoner. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-07-04–2022-07-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25537/)
- **facet and pvp ruleset**: Outlands has a single world with open PvP and risk of loss. It has no Trammel-style safe facet, is not designed for PvM-only play, and the staff say it will not split into Trammel and Felucca realms. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-05-06–2025-10-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32427/)
- **faction flagging colors**: Members of the same faction (Order or Chaos) flag blue/green to each other and orange to members of the opposing faction. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-08-18–2018-08-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2021/)
- **fortune aspect armor**: Fortune aspect armor boosts trap and lockpicking abilities, gives a bonus of 3 plus 1 per tier to Lockpicking, Cartography and gathering skills, and its wand and trap damage bonus was reduced to 12.5% plus 3.75% per tier. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-20–2019-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14311/)
- **greater strength and dexterity potion bonuses**: A Greater Strength potion gives +20 strength and a Greater Dexterity potion gives +20 dexterity. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **guilds and factions**: Outlands has guilds, which function similarly to clans, as well as factions. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-17–2025-05-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31832/)
- **gump overrides for custom ui**: Outlands supports custom UI art through gump overrides: place the modified image files in a folder named GumpOverrides inside the Ultima Online Outlands game folder. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-04-12–2024-03-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29049/)
- **heat of battle after killing blue**: After a blue player is killed, every killer involved gets Heat of Battle, which blocks Recall, Gate, Moongate and Hiking for 2 minutes multiplied by the killer's count (for example 4 minutes for 2 killers). _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-11–2018-12-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6980/)
- **inn and tavern logout**: Entering an inn or tavern logs your character out immediately, letting you switch to another character on the same account right away and avoiding the vulnerable logout timeout. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-02–2018-12-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5768/)
- **item property tooltips**: Ultima Online: Outlands does not use tooltips or hover functionality to display item properties, as it emulates a period of UO history without that feature. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-06–2026-03-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32905/)
- **loot ownership on death**: When a player dies in PvP, their loot belongs to the killer or killers, though resurrection-killing is considered poor etiquette. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-06–2018-12-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6295/)
- **looting rights and grey flag**: Looting a corpse without looting rights, such as a monster another non-party player did more damage to (shown as a blue corpse), turns you grey. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-02–2018-11-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4618/)
- **lore book completion reward**: Completing a Lore Book by adding all its pages (roughly 10 to 15 depending on the subject) awards a Legendary (Level 8) Treasure Map, and one claim adds a Mastery Chain link. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-10–2019-04-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13052/)
- **mana drain and vampire skill**: Mana Drain and Mana Vampire do not let a player exceed the mana refund cap, so the skill need not be maxed if refund is already at the cap. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-09-05–2024-06-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29657/)
- **mastery chain experience**: A Mastery Chain can have up to 25 links, and each link must be unlocked with experience earned at 1 point per 1 gold earned from actions such as killing mobs or opening dungeon chests. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-28–2022-11-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26577/)
- **mastery chain experience sharing**: Mastery chain experience is shared across all characters on the same account, so PvM combat characters should be kept on one account to share it. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-11-12–2025-02-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31370/)
- **mastery chain link sources**: Mastery Chain links are obtained from Tomes of Heroism, Lore Books/Lore Maps, high-end and contested bosses (including T8s), Society jobs and rewards, and by spending Reward Points from Arenas, Factions, Achievements and PvP events. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-04-28–2022-01-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24181/)
- **meditation mana regeneration**: Meditation regenerates mana faster with higher skill, and active meditation gives a bonus rate (about 1 mana per second in PvP) while you stay still and do no other actions; armor also affects mana regeneration. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-02–2023-02-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26968/)
- **moongate cooldown after combat**: After attacking or being in combat with another player, such as a thief or red, you must wait about 30 seconds before you can use a moongate. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-08-10–2022-10-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26065/)
- **moongate lockout after pvp aggression**: Attacking another player, including fighting back against a red, flags you for combat and locks you out of moongates; this was added after reds complained about opponents fighting and then gate hopping away. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-08-04–2021-08-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22567/)
- **mount tokens**: Ethereal mount tokens are obtainable on Outlands, including order mount tokens earned as a faction goal, and were expected to become common among players. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-07–2019-01-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7956/)
- **mounts in dungeons**: Mounts cannot be used in dungeons on Outlands, which makes dexxers stronger in dungeon PvP against mages. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-27–2024-03-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29111/)
- **murderer aggressive action restriction**: Murderers receive an account-wide Aggressive Action Restriction on resurrection that stops any character on the account from attacking, looting or stealing against blue players, lasting 1 hour per 50 Long Term Counts, with a 72 hour repent duration. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-11–2018-12-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6980/)
- **murderer status scope**: Red (murderer) status in Outlands applies only to the individual character, not the whole account, so a player can switch to another character and avoid its consequences. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2026-01-06–2026-04-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33000/)
- **number of spells**: The Outlands shard has 64 spells available. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1421/)
- **opening aspect mastery menu**: Type [aspect in game to open the Aspect Mastery menu; it can also be opened from the paperdoll via Help then Aspect Mastery, or by double-clicking aspect cores, distillations or an Aspect Kit. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-20–2021-07-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/22407/)
- **pet experience gain**: Pets gain experience when they assist in killing monsters, including when they kill mobs themselves or when their owner is in a party. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-03-13–2026-07-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33478/)
- **pet leveling**: Tamed pets, including ones like a hind, level up individually, so each pet has to be levelled separately. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-04–2020-09-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19018/)
- **pet pvp scalar**: Staff adjust pets' PvP performance with a PvP scalar, which is applied as a flat value across all pets regardless of the taming skill required to control them. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-10–2019-09-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15113/)
- **pet transfer behavior**: A trained pet transferred to another character keeps its training levels and stays bonded. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-12–2019-02-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9741/)
- **pets on owner logout**: Pets, including bonded pets, do not log out with their owner and remain in the world unprotected unless they are stabled. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-26–2018-11-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5159/)
- **pk resurrection fee**: Players who die while red (murderers) on Outlands must pay a resurrection fee, which players describe as minor. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-12-16–2026-03-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32956/)
- **pvp event outcomes**: The outcome of current Outlands PvP events has no lasting effect on the game world once an event ends. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-28–2022-01-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24289/)
- **pvp event scoring design**: Outlands PvP events mainly score through control zones plus point stealing on kills rather than flat kill points, because flat kill scoring was exploited through ress-killing and farming and let one team build insurmountable early leads; each event keeps its own point system. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-11–2022-03-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24634/)
- **red player city restrictions**: Red (murderer) players are not allowed inside cities or able to use city services in Outlands, but this is largely bypassed because the PK city is accessible and players can use another character. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-11-28–2026-01-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32753/)
- **red player population ratio**: Staff-reported figures show that of roughly 2700-3000 players online, fewer than about 100 are red, and only around 8-15 of those are actively roaming the world at any given time. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-09-17–2024-05-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29376/)
- **sanctuary dungeon**: Each week one dungeon is selected as the Sanctuary dungeon, shown on the Region Bonus board, where players can farm without PKs or thieves directly interfering, though XP and loot are reduced there. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-07-02–2026-07-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33375/)
- **shard reboots and lag**: Lag builds up after the Outlands shard has run for several days, so staff reboot it when lag appears, and scheduled reboots every day or every other day were planned. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-06–2019-02-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9325/)
- **ship sinking**: A ship reduced to 0 health sinks and is permanently lost, and other players can sink a player's ship. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-03-03–2019-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14210/)
- **show target range indicator option**: The Show Target Range Indicator option under Miscellaneous displays a number next to the target cursor showing how many tiles the target is from your character. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-03-04–2022-06-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25325/)
- **snooping delay**: Outlands has a built-in delay on snooping, which exists to counter script thieves. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-09–2021-03-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20928/)
- **spell aspect maestery links**: The Spell Aspect Effect Chance link raises the chance of aspect channelling on a spell cast and gives no substantial damage bonus but greatly helps sustain the mana loop, while the Effect Modifier link improves channelling damage and some effects. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-07-13–2022-09-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25937/)
- **spell siphon bonus**: Spell Siphon gives a damage bonus, not extra resistance, and applies to both melee and spell damage by design, so melee characters have a reason to keep Resist instead of skipping it. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2024-03-26–2024-03-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29104/)
- **stealing and blessed items**: Blessed items such as house keys and bank checks cannot be stolen by thieves, while any item that is not blessed can be stolen. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-09-22–2026-05-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33193/)
- **stealing at banks**: Thieves can steal items from players' bags at banks on Outlands, including the bank in Andaria, so valuables should not be left in your pack there. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-12-20–2023-07-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/27994/)
- **stealing in towns**: Stealing inside Outlands towns has a low success chance and often ends in guard kills, which is a deliberate balance against how easy scripted stealing is. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-12-31–2018-12-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7718/)
- **template points and solo pvm templates**: An Outlands template has 700 skill points and 225 stat points, and customized skills allow varied templates, so effective solo PvM templates can be 8x builds and players need not be GM in every skill or use a 7x template. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-07-19–2019-01-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8694/)
- **test items on test server**: To have frequently used test items available after every test server reset, keep them in your bank box on the live server, because the test server is refreshed as a copy of live. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-08-11–2022-08-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25744/)
- **test server reset schedule**: The Outlands test server is reset unpredictably, as needed by the development team, usually when testing bigger patches or upcoming events (events are roughly every 2-3 months); it often stays up a couple of weeks but can reset more or less often. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-08-11–2022-08-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25750/)
- **thief death steal delay**: A thief who dies after an attempted steal receives a delay and cannot steal for 2 minutes. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-04–2019-02-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9627/)
- **tinkering trap placement limit**: The number of traps placeable per 3 minutes scales with Tinkering and Detect Hidden: 80+ gives 3, 90+ gives 4, 100+ gives 5, 110+ gives 6, and 120+ in both gives 7. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-02-21–2019-07-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/14311/)
- **town crier**: The Town Crier is an in-game announcement system in which town criers yell out important information to players. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-08-11–2025-07-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31979/)
- **town struggles team balancing**: Town Struggles teams are balanced using ELO ratings to keep teams as even as possible. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-12–2022-02-28)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24579/)
- **trammel ruleset**: Outlands does not have a Trammel facet or PvP-free ruleset, and players do not expect it to be added. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-05-15–2024-07-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30070/)
- **treasure map chest digging**: Digging up a treasure map chest takes about 20 seconds, but the chest is guarded by mobs, and clearing them and opening it can take 2-3 players 10-20 minutes. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-06-07–2022-11-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26429/)
- **war flagging and orange risk**: Guilds can enable Global War at the guild level and can also fight guild-versus-guild Guild Wars, but each player must separately opt in to war flagging, and flagged players can be attacked as orange by the opposing side. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-31–2022-02-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24458/)
- **wizard's grimoire create food upgrade**: Create Food should be maxed first when leveling the Wizard's Grimoire, since its upgrade gives an extra 25 mana every 60 seconds. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2020-11-08–2022-09-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25937/)
- **armor material and location penalty interaction**: The armor material penalty and location penalty are multiplied, so a 0% material penalty negates the location penalty (0% material * 35% shield location = 0%). _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-14–2018-12-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7102/)
- **aspect gear unbless cooldown after death**: After a player dies with aspect gear, the gear is placed on a 24-hour unblessed cooldown. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-01-27–2019-01-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/8894/)
- **aspect unlock core credit**: Players who paid the old, higher core cost to unlock an aspect were credited for the cores they paid, so their next several aspect level unlocks may be free using stored materials. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2025-02-23–2025-02-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31437/)
- **blessed clothing durability**: Blessed clothing items, including special items that count as a leather cap such as masks, are indestructible. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2025-12-30–2025-12-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32684/)
- **blessed clothing tradability and usability**: Blessed clothing items can be freely traded to other players and work for any wearer, not only the player who blessed them. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2025-12-30–2025-12-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32684/)
- **ccc event duration**: The CCC event lasts 30 minutes. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-02-04–2022-02-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24403/)
- **character transfers**: Outlands does not support character transfers. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-04-13–2020-04-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/17233/)
- **cortina runetome quest**: The Cortina runetome quest is likely tied to the account rather than the character, so a new character on the same account probably cannot repeat it. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2025-07-27–2025-07-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32059/)
- **criminal flag timers and corpse looting**: After a player's flag changes from grey (criminal) to blue (innocent), a wait of roughly a minute may be required before using a portal, and opening a blue player's corpse should not flag you as criminal unless you interact with its items. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-22–2018-12-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7704/)
- **echo and mastery chain progress**: Purchasing an echo on a character lets a player play a different template on that same character while keeping their existing Mastery Chain progress. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2021-01-13–2021-01-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20112/)
- **essence upkeep at max aspect levels**: Higher tier aspects use essence, but players running characters at max links and max aspect levels pay little to no ongoing essence upkeep, so players have argued for a throttle. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-10-01–2024-10-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30403/)
- **factions**: The three factions in Ultima Online: Outlands are Chaos, Order and Freedom. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-09-30–2018-09-30)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2701/)
- **food effect**: Eating food only increases regeneration speed and does not increase skill gain. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-04–2018-11-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3862/)
- **full-loot pvp and aspect item drops**: Outlands PvP is full-loot open-world PvP, but it is unconfirmed whether killing a player on land gives a chance to take the aspect cores, extracts, and essence they use for character upgrades. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 1 author, 2024-04-02–2024-04-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29170/)
- **grid loot**: Grid loot shows all of a mob's loot and lets you single-click an item to move it to your backpack, and it comes in an Outlands-themed version and a basic version. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-06-07–2022-06-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25325/)
- **healer scoring in events**: Event scoring favors kill points over healing, and healing points can be easily faked by healing damaged guildmates outside real PvP, so healers are not rewarded accurately. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-01-08–2022-01-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/23987/)
- **heat of battle flag in dungeons**: Players fighting a red in dungeons are affected by the HOB (heat of battle) flag, which discourages them from standing and fighting and encourages them to leave instead. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-04-01–2019-04-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12072/)
- **juggling ball cooldown**: The juggling ball cooldown was reduced in August 2020, but the cooldown timer is still shared across all juggling ball sets rather than being separate per set. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-08-22–2020-08-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18704/)
- **keeping pets when logging out**: To keep a tamed pet when leaving the game for an extended time, stable the pet before logging out, because pets do not log out with the player. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-29–2018-11-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/5547/)
- **looting blue player corpses**: A player who loots a blue player's corpse goes grey/criminal for a limited time, during which others can attack them; a looter who hides until the counter expires avoids consequences, and if the victim then kills them, the victim can receive a murder mark. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2025-01-07–2025-04-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31676/)
- **magic resistance mechanics**: On Outlands, Magic Resist does not fully stop spells such as paralyze, unlike on OSI, and it reduces spell damage instead. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-01-11–2020-01-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/16210/)
- **mastery chains**: Mastery Chains were added in the April 28 patch and are tied to an individual character rather than to the account. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 3 authors, 2020-09-02–2021-01-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20112/)
- **murder count and pk penalty**: Murder counts decay over time rather than being paid off, and a player-killer only receives the death penalty if they actually die. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-09-19–2022-09-19)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25995/)
- **murder count leaderboard**: Outlands has a leaderboard that tracks players' murder counts, with some long-term totals approaching 700. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-11–2018-12-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6781/)
- **murderer penalties**: Outlands applies murderer penalties including ravens, dungeon lockouts and increased resurrection penalties. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2025-11-07–2025-11-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32488/)
- **pets attacking returned-blue thief**: Pets may keep attacking a thief who stole from their owner after the thief turns blue again, and the owner can turn red if the pet kills that blue thief. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-12–2018-12-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6949/)
- **pit trials weekly cap**: Pit Trials (Netherzone runs) have a weekly cap of seven runs per week. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2026-08-12–2026-08-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33588/)
- **pk death dungeon lockout**: A PK who dies is locked out of the dungeon and receives an account-wide aggression lock; dungeon death restrictions are generally designed to protect other players. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-02-15–2026-06-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33232/)
- **pk fine for killing players**: Killing a player incurs a count or fine for the killer, and a higher fee was proposed for killing new players, but the exact amount is uncertain. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-06-26–2024-06-26)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29825/)
- **pvp talisman charge cap proposal**: In the proposal, PvP Talisman charges cap at 120%, with 1% equalling 1 minute of usage, for a maximum charge of 2 hours. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-01-16–2022-01-16)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24120/)
- **re-synchronize client hotkey**: Re-synchronize client is a hotkey that fixes a de-synced client, where your character appears one or more tiles away from your real position and movement can be blocked. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-04-05–2018-04-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1018/)
- **red fast travel restriction proposal**: A proposal under discussion for Outlands would stop red players from using fast travel such as recall while under an aggressor flag; it is not confirmed as implemented. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-11–2018-12-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6838/)
- **red resurrection cost scaling**: A player killer's resurrection cost rises with their murder count, and the red's share of the penalty is kept low partly to prevent abuse such as killing their own alts to recoup it. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-10–2018-12-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6838/)
- **repent forgiveness duration**: Repenting reportedly requires about 72 hours to fully forgive a red's murder counts, though this is phrased as a question and is unconfirmed. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-11–2018-12-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6812/)
- **repenting murder counts with gold**: Repenting with gold removes a red's murder counts, which lets reds cycle between red status and offset the account downtime incurred from resurrecting with counts. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-11–2018-12-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/6781/)
- **rift event frequency**: Rift events occur roughly every 4 hours, and are not guaranteed to produce even team numbers. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-02-04–2022-02-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24402/)
- **rift mob spawns and servitor**: Rifts spawn mobs quickly and use a servitor mechanic to force players to engage and fight, making the PvM portion matter for winning. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-01-29–2022-01-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24308/)
- **sacred journey restricted areas**: Sacred Journey fails with the message 'a mysterious force prevents you from using that spell' when cast in a restricted area, such as near the Prev bank. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2024-07-02–2024-07-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29914/)
- **script stealing outside guarded areas**: Stealing via scripts is possible on the shard and can be run anywhere outside guarded areas. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-02-08–2019-02-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9562/)
- **sequencer aspect**: A new 'Sequencer' Aspect may potentially be introduced to synergize with Spellchaining, but it is not confirmed. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 1 author, 2025-05-25–2025-05-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31814/)
- **shared weekly society jobs**: All players receive the same weekly society jobs, so lockpickers crowd the same dungeon where chests are usually already opened while other dungeons stay untouched. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-03-07–2019-03-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11219/)
- **ship speed pursuit**: A faster ship can generally catch and sink a slower ship, though this is a single-source claim and may not hold in every situation. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-03-03–2019-03-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/10984/)
- **skill damage bonuses**: Several Outlands skills, such as Lumberjacking and Tracking, give damage modifiers, reportedly about 10% each. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-02-28–2020-08-05)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/18549/)
- **society job types**: Society jobs include crafting exceptional avarite lockpicks, killing infernal knights, and monster hunter jobs such as killing raw fire spawns, with quantities that vary by week. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-03-07–2019-03-07)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/11238/)
- **spell interruption when hit**: Spellcasting is always interrupted when the caster is hit, regardless of the caster's wrestling skill. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-03-29–2019-03-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12021/)
- **spellbook charged property**: The spellbook property formerly called Charged was changed during beta, and the current system uses a potency mechanic instead. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-12-27–2018-12-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/7613/)
- **sprint command**: Outlands does not have a sprint command like the Rel Por sprint command. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2017-10-21–2017-10-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/559/)
- **stat gain above max from potions and bless**: Strength and agility potions and Bless (and its sub spells) can raise the Strength stat above the normal maximum in Outlands. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-23–2018-11-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/4937/)
- **stealing near banks**: Stealing near a bank is difficult to do unnoticed because many players are present and can see the attempt. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-05-10–2019-05-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/13429/)
- **stealing unblessed items**: Items that are not blessed can be stolen, which likely includes a Wizard's Satchel worn on the paperdoll and the reagents inside it. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2026-05-29–2026-05-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33192/)
- **town struggles participant count**: Town Struggles sometimes has 30 or more participants, and a proposal suggested scaling teams with sign-ups (30+ players gives 3 teams, 40+ gives 4), but this is not confirmed as implemented. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 1 author, 2022-01-12–2022-01-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24045/)
- **war aspect survivability**: Building up the War aspect makes players somewhat more survivable in dungeons. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-12-12–2022-12-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26646/)
- **water aspect**: The Water aspect boosts healing and alchemy and is one of the cheapest aspect sets. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-05-10–2019-10-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/15293/)
- **water aspect effects**: Water Aspect gives a heal bonus from all sources, bonus to bandage and vet kit heals on pets, longer potion buffs and an increased weapon proc chance (+24.5% at tier 13). _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-06-20–2021-01-18)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/20164/)

## Other

- **anniversary shimmer hues**: Anniversary Shimmer hues are Redux (1957) for the 1 Year Anniversary, Tempest (1180) for the 2 Year Anniversary, and Crystal (1059) for the 3rd Year Anniversary (October 27 to November 1, 2021), and these were limited-time releases. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **discord shard rules**: All Outlands Shard Rules apply to communicating within the official Outlands Discord server. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-09-15–2018-09-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/2396/)
- **halloween shimmer hues**: Halloween Shimmer hues by year are Paradox (2346) in 2019, Spirit (1181) in 2020, Toil (1499) in 2021, Gore (1069) in 2022, Hex (1299) in 2023, Blight (1586) in 2024, and Viscera (1598) in 2025. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **joining the outlands discord**: Players can join the Outlands Discord at discord.uooutlands.com, by clicking the Chat button in the game client's top information bar (which connects instantly), or by clicking the Discord button on the Outlands website, which auto-invites them. _(official, conf 0.85, 2 authors, 2024-08-15–2026-07-29)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33364/)
- **king's faire and special shimmer hues**: King's Faire Shimmer hues by year are Solstice (1967) and Dahlia (1956) in 2019, Gala (2074) in 2020, Nightfall (2260) in 2021, Mirage (1575) in 2022, Comet (1099) in 2023, Viridian (1583) in 2024, Dusk (1599) in 2025, and Flare (1694) in 2026; separately, Lunar (2342) was available June 4-11, 2019, Moonlight (2284) June 30 - July 6, 2020, and Dune (1692) with Ossuary on 4 May 2026. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **lunar new year shimmer hues**: Lunar New Year Shimmer hues are Vermilion (1579) in 2024 (February 10-25), Saffron (1593) in 2025, and Cinnabar (1690) in 2026. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **mid-sea shimmer hues**: Mid-Sea Shimmer hues are Tidal (1083) in 2023, Selkie (1572) in 2024, Phosphor (1597) in 2025, and Abyss (1592) in 2026. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **outlands discord purpose**: The Outlands Community Discord is used for help, trading, guild recruitment, event announcements, patch news, build discussion, and server updates and answers to questions. _(official, conf 0.85, 3 authors, 2021-12-26–2026-07-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33364/)
- **seasonal shimmer hues**: Shimmer Spice (1557) was available for Thanksgiving 2019 (Nov 27 - Dec 10), Shimmer Flora (1071) for Thanksgiving 2022 (Nov 23-30), Shimmer Flourish (1600) for Fall Harvest 2025, and Shimmer Roan (1696) for Fall Harvest 2026. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **st. patrick's day shimmer hues**: St. Patrick's Day Shimmer hues are Shamrock (2664) in 2019, Fen (1400) in 2022, Zephyr (1480) in 2023, and Clover (1189) in 2024. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **tidal tomb expansion launch**: The Tidal Tomb Expansion launches on Sunday, November 16, 2025 at 11AM EST, with official patch notes released at the same time. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2025-11-01–2025-11-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32437/)
- **valentines, spring and winter shimmer hues**: Valentines Shimmer hues are Valentine (2916) in 2019, Passion (1184) in 2021, Candy (1188) in 2022 and Serenity (1081) in 2023; Spring hues run from Periwinkle (2251, 2019) to Zinnia (1691, 2026); Winter/Holiday hues run from Garnet (1571, 2019) to Aether (1689, 2025). _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **winter holiday shimmer hues**: Shimmer Cardinal (hue 1476) and Shimmer Evergreen (hue 1479) were available during the winter holidays. _(official, conf 0.85, 1 author, 2018-07-14–2018-07-14)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/1337/)
- **server population**: Outlands population has grown over time: about 1,500 players online in 2019, about 2,000-3,000 by 2022, and about 3,000+ online with over 100,000 accounts by late 2024. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 5 authors, 2022-02-10–2024-11-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30644/)
- **wearable cosmetic items**: Outlands includes many wearable cosmetic items, such as the Wilderness Hood, Winter Boots, Regal Winter Robe, Sabertooth Mask, Deer Mask, Fur Coat, bonnet, fancy shirt, full apron, sandals, tiara, scarf, gold chain, thigh boots, utility belt and adventurers tunic. _(consensus, conf 0.80, 7 authors, 2020-12-08–2020-12-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19838/)
- **aspect themed hues and gear**: Outlands has aspect-themed dye hues (such as Blood, Shadow, Command and Fortune Aspect) used to color clothing, backpacks, armor and weapons, as well as aspect-themed armor and weapons, and players can also choose custom skin colors. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2020-12-09–2020-12-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19846/)
- **shard population size**: Outlands is a very popular shard that has grown steadily, with roughly 3,000 players online at peak in early 2023, over 75,000 accounts, and an Outlands Discord routinely showing more than 7,000 online users, so dungeons are often crowded. _(consensus, conf 0.75, 4 authors, 2019-01-12–2024-11-02)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/30644/)
- **community communication platform**: Discord is the main chat medium for the Outlands community, though it is not heavily used for group finding and there is no in-game Looking for Group menu. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2021-01-05–2026-05-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/33072/)
- **gumpoverrides file naming**: Files in the GumpOverrides folder must be named only by the gump's hex ID, such as 0x1B58.bmp or 0x4c.png, and can be bmp or png; duplicate or similar names for the same ID (such as 0x003c.png and 3c.png, or a bmp and png of the same ID) can crash the client or stop it launching. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2023-09-18–2025-06-25)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31900/)
- **moddable artwork types**: In Outlands only Gump/UI artwork can be modded via GumpOverrides; other art types such as items, potions, gems and reagents cannot be modded this way. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2023-09-15–2023-09-15)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28245/)
- **test shard**: Outlands has a Test Shard where players can try out clothing, outfits and items, though it is only available at certain times. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-02-19–2023-10-27)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/28407/)
- **wiki modifiers page**: The Outlands wiki has a Modifiers page listing weapon damage percentages and item modifiers, with some entries marked '**' as pending updates, and a graphical overview comparing the modifiers at the bottom. _(consensus, conf 0.70, 3 authors, 2019-02-20–2024-03-06)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/29004/)
- **account deletion**: Outlands staff do not delete player accounts, so accounts cannot be deleted. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-11-15–2025-08-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32081/)
- **classicuo bar swipe**: ClassicUO has a bar swipe (drag-select) feature that lets players select multiple health bars at once instead of cycling or manually pulling individual bars. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-01-11–2022-01-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24031/)
- **early shrine corruption control**: In the first few months of Outlands, a single guild (named $) controlled about 95% of Shrine Corruptions. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2022-03-09–2022-03-10)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24679/)
- **gump override folder name**: The Outlands override folder must be named GumpOverrides, with an S at the end; a folder named GumpOverride will not work. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-06-01–2023-06-01)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/27331/)
- **in-game ping command accuracy**: The in-game -ping command is inaccurate while moving and is not fully accurate even when standing still, so UOTrace or CMD ping give more reliable latency readings. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2018-10-23–2018-10-23)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3507/)
- **outlands design era**: Outlands is inspired by the T2A era of UO and treated as a continuation of it without the UOR and AoS changes, though some players feel it plays more like UO2 than original UO. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-02-05–2023-01-22)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/26876/)
- **player guilds and communities**: The Pathfinders (PATH) and Knight's Guild (Sir) are player guilds on Outlands that host community events and help members, and PATH, Sir. and Yew are tied to the roleplay community. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2023-02-17–2025-03-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/31502/)
- **roleplay channels**: The Outlands community has roleplay channels on both the forums, where people post stories, and the Discord, where players find like-minded roleplayers, plus a Discord newplayer channel for help. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2021-11-01–2021-11-04)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/23465/)
- **server birth rares**: There were 20 server birth rares on Outlands, all have been discovered and are listed on the wiki, and few other rares exist beyond event rares and rare dyes or cloth colors. _(consensus, conf 0.65, 2 authors, 2019-01-06–2019-02-13)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9768/)
- **bestiary stats for tamable creatures**: The base stats on the Outlands bestiary page for tamable creatures do not match those of actual tamed creatures, and staff are working to update the bestiary for all tamables. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2018-11-08–2018-11-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/3966/)
- **dyed backpacks**: Backpacks in Outlands can be dyed with special hues using backpack dye, such as Shimmer Gala or Shadow Aspect backpack dye. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-12-09–2020-12-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19844/)
- **exitlag node selection for outlands**: If ExitLag's automatic region finding fails for Outlands, manually select Ashburn nodes. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2025-10-11–2025-10-11)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/32318/)
- **infobar size**: The infobar probably cannot be made bigger, though this is uncertain. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-07-21–2022-07-21)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/25611/)
- **lantern variants**: Outlands has several lantern variants with different appearances or hues, including the Royal Red, Aegis Keep, Cavernam, Copper, Shimmer Solstice and Shimmer Dahila lanterns. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 5 authors, 2020-12-08–2020-12-08)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19830/)
- **llama hue**: Llamas now spawn in their original hue 0 after the color change. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-02-03–2019-02-03)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/9287/)
- **location-named hues**: Some Outlands hues are named after locations, such as the Aegis Keep hue and the Darkmire Temple hue. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2020-12-09–2020-12-09)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/19838/)
- **outlands developers**: The Outlands developers, Luthius and Owyn, were previously involved in the freeshard projects An Corp and Rel Por. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2017-10-12–2017-10-12)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/525/)
- **outlands server location for exitlag**: The Outlands server is located in Ashburn, so ExitLag should be set to use an Ashburn node to improve the connection. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2022-01-31–2022-01-31)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/24340/)
- **test server**: A test server exists where players can try out features such as polymorph enhancements before relying on them on the live server. _(single_source, conf 0.50, 2 authors, 2019-04-17–2019-04-17)_ [src](https://forums.uooutlands.com/index.php?posts/12657/)
